Method: 提出了三种四元数迭代算法：(i) 四元数快速迭代法(QRAPID)，(ii) 四元数强近似逆法(QSAI)，(iii) 十九阶四元数超幂迭代法(QHPI19)；建立了收敛定理和扰动界；将QSAI作为四元数Krylov子空间求解器的预处理器。

Result: 提出算法在精度上达到或超过四元数SVD、四元数Newton-Schulz和经典超幂方案，同时大幅降低了计算开销；QSAI预处理器显著减少了大规模线性系统的迭代次数和运行时间；在图像修复（CUR分解）和信号滤波应用中验证了可扩展性和有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的四元数迭代框架在计算四元数Moore-Penrose伪逆时兼具高精度和高效率，能够有效处理大规模实际多维数据问题。

Abstract: The computation of generalized inverses of quaternion matrices is a fundamental problem in quaternion linear algebra, with wide-ranging applications in signal processing, image restoration, and multidimensional data analysis. This paper presents three efficient quaternion iterative algorithms for computing the Moore-Penrose pseudoinverse: (i) the quaternion rapid iterative method (QRAPID), (ii) the quaternion strong approximate inverse (QSAI), and (iii) the quaternion hyperpower iterative method of order nineteen (QHPI19). Convergence theorems and perturbation bounds are established to ensure numerical stability and robustness. The QSAI method is further employed as a preconditioner for quaternion Krylov subspace solvers, resulting in substantial reductions in the iteration count and runtime for large-scale linear systems. Comprehensive numerical experiments demonstrate that the proposed algorithms achieve an accuracy comparable to or better than existing approaches, including quaternion SVD, quaternion Newton-Schulz, and classical hyperpower schemes, while offering significant computational savings. The practical utility of the framework is illustrated through two representative applications: image completion via CUR decomposition and signal filtering, which confirm its scalability and effectiveness in real-world multidimensional data applications.

Abstract: We propose a family of low-rank, completely positive and trace preserving schemes for the Lindblad equation, a common model for open quantum systems. Low-rank representation is employed at two levels: the density matrix is factorized into the product of tall-skinny matrices, and the columns of these matrices are further represented using the tensor train (TT) format, also know as matrix product states (MPS). This two-level low-rank format fits naturally into our existing Kraus is King scheme (arXiv:2409.08898v2 [math.NA]) for the Lindblad equation, whose underlying operations are arithmetic on the columns of the tall-skinny matrices. We show how these operations can be performed efficiently in the TT/MPS format, with particular emphasis on density matrix rank-truncation. We conclude with extensive numerical experiments demonstrating the convergence of this scheme and its efficiency in simulating systems with up to $10^{19}$ degrees of freedom using only modest compute resources.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 证明了在紧支撑可积核的一般假设下，解继承源项的跳跃间断；原点奇异性导致解导数 blow-up；端点跳跃产生导数间断级联。对于紧支撑 C∞ 核，不会出现导数 blow-up 或级联损失，解与源项具有等价的逐段光滑 regularity。所提半解析谱方法在数值实验中显著提高了精度和收敛性，有效缓解了间断引起的 Gibbs 振荡。

Conclusion: 核函数正则性决定了非局部问题中间断传播的结构；所发展的半解析谱方法能够在不牺牲谱方法效率的前提下有效处理间断问题，为非局部问题的数值求解提供了有力工具。

Abstract: We study a nonlocal Poisson problem with discontinuous source term and analyze how the regularity of the integral kernel determines the discontinuity structure of the corresponding solution. Under general assumptions on compactly supported integrable kernels, we show that jump discontinuities in the source term are inherited by the solution. We then identify two principal mechanisms governing higher-order regularity: singular behavior of the kernel at the origin and jump discontinuities of the kernel, or of its derivatives, at the horizon endpoints. Singularities at the origin lead to blow-up of certain derivatives of the solution at the source discontinuity, while jumps at the horizon generate cascades of derivative discontinuities at translated locations. These phenomena occur for kernels commonly used in peridynamic-type models. By contrast, compactly supported \(C^\infty\) kernels do not generate derivative blow-up or cascading losses of regularity, and in this case the source term and the solution have equivalent piecewise smooth regularity. Motivated by this analysis, we develop a semi-analytic spectral method for the accurate numerical treatment of discontinuous nonlocal problems. The method uses successive smoothing transformations and explicitly constructed correction functions to convert the original problem into an auxiliary problem with improved regularity. A spectral solver is then applied to the smoothed problem, and the approximation to the original solution is recovered by adding back the analytic corrections. Numerical experiments show substantial gains in accuracy and convergence, demonstrating that the method effectively mitigates the loss of accuracy caused by discontinuities and Gibbs oscillations while retaining the efficiency of spectral methods.

</details>

Abstract: We consider an unregularized optimal control problem subject to the steady-state Navier-Stokes equations. We derive the existence of optimal solutions and prove first- and -- necessary and sufficient -- second-order optimality conditions. To approximate solutions to the optimal control problem, we consider the variational discretization scheme. We analyze convergence properties of the discretization and prove a priori error estimates for locally optimal controls that are nonsingular and which satisfy a growth condition which implies a bang-bang structure.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 直接公式（D-ECFOIE/D-MCFOIE）及其正则化版本是求解PEC障碍物电磁散射问题的有效方法，兼具物理意义的直接性和数值稳定性，尤其电场公式的低频修正使其适用于任意低频率。

Abstract: This paper is the direct-formulation companion to [Burbano-Gallegos, P'erez-Arancibia, and Turc, ESAIM: M2AN, 60(1):273--315, 2026], which developed indirect combined-field-only boundary integral equations (BIEs) for time-harmonic electromagnetic scattering by smooth perfectly electrically conducting (PEC) obstacles, relying entirely on Helmholtz boundary integral operators. Here we exploit the same equivalence between the Maxwell PEC scattering problem and a pair of vector Helmholtz boundary value problems -- one for the electric field and one for the magnetic field -- to derive direct BIE formulations whose unknowns are the Dirichlet and Neumann traces of the total fields, decomposed into their normal and tangential surface components. These unknowns carry direct physical meaning: in particular, the magnetic-field formulation yields the surface electric currents as part of its solution. The mixed regularity of the two field-trace components requires introducing a tailored product H"older space, a distinctive feature absent from the indirect approach. We prove that the resulting Direct Electric and Magnetic Combined-Field-Only Integral Equations (D-ECFOIE and D-MCFOIE) are uniquely solvable at all frequencies, and introduce Calder'on-type regularizations (RD-ECFOIE and RD-MCFOIE) that render them of the Fredholm second kind. We further examine the low-frequency breakdown affecting the electric-field formulation and introduce a modified equation that enforces the physical charge-conservation constraints, which restores numerical accuracy and well-conditioned linear systems for frequencies arbitrarily close to zero. Numerical experiments, performed using a high-order Nystr"om solver based on the Density Interpolation Method and implemented in the Julia package Inti.jl, validate the accuracy and robustness of the proposed formulations across a range of geometries and frequencies.

</details>

Result: 建立了Voigt正则化问题的最优收敛速率；推导了未正则化Hall-MHD系统的误差估计，其中Voigt正则化在非电阻 regime 中起关键作用；2.5D和3D数值模拟验证了理论结果和方案的物理保真度。

Conclusion: 所提出的结构保持线性全离散有限元方法对Voigt正则化Hall-MHD系统是有效且物理一致的，在无散约束保持和能量稳定性方面具有优势，并为原始Hall-MHD系统提供了可靠的数值逼近。

Abstract: The incompressible Hall-magnetohydrodynamics (Hall--MHD) system presents substantial analytical and computational challenges due to its stiff, highly nonlinear Hall term and the strict requirement that the magnetic field remains solenoidal. In this paper, we study a Voigt-regularised Hall--MHD system, which is of independent analytical interest and provides a physically consistent, well-posed regularisation of the original model. We propose, analyse, and implement a structure-preserving, linear, fully discrete finite element method for this regularised problem. Using finite element exterior calculus and a mixed formulation, the spatial discretisation enforces the divergence-free condition on the magnetic field exactly, while a skew-symmetric, linearly implicit time discretisation yields unconditional energy stability. We establish optimal convergence rates for the Voigt-regularised problem and, additionally, derive error estimates for the unregularised Hall--MHD system, with the Voigt regularisation playing a crucial role in the non-resistive regime. Finally, numerical simulations in both 2.5D and 3D corroborate the theoretical results and demonstrate the physical fidelity of the scheme.

Method: 扩展Fu-Shi交替数值方案，纳入椭圆型低维拟周期解的共振处理机制；利用预解式恒等式简化多尺度分析，将全局逆表示为局部逆的线性组合（粘合过程），揭示短程弱相互作用与长程强相互作用的平衡机制。

Abstract: A numerical framework for constructing full-dimensional quasi-periodic solutions in nearly integrable systems was recently developed by Fu and Shi[2026]. Based on an alternating scheme, this approach effectively overcomes the secular drift in angle variables, a fundamental limitation of symplectic integrators. However, in many applications, such as the restricted three-body problem, lower-dimensional quasi-periodic solutions hold greater significance. The construction of these solutions is considerably more challenging due to the presence of normal frequencies, leading to intricate resonance phenomena. Beyond the subspace resonance, one must also account for the first and second Melnikov conditions to eliminate small divisors. In this study, we extend the proposed alternating numerical scheme to compute the elliptic lower-dimensional quasi-periodic solutions. Numerical experiments are presented for the Hénon-Heiles model and the Fermi--Pasta--Ulam (FPU) model, demonstrating the effectiveness of the proposed method. Furthermore, we emphasize that the perturbation is not merely a polynomial with real coefficients but is a real-valued function. As a result, the associated perturbation operator exhibits Gevrey decay without possessing a Hankel structure. Meanwhile, we further simplify the multi-scale analysis by exploiting the resolvent identity, showing that the global inverse can be expressed linearly in terms of local inverses via the gluing procedure. This representation reveals a regime-dependent interaction structure: weak interactions dominate at short range, while strong interactions emerge at long range. This balance ensures that the Gevrey decay of the inverse remains uniformly controlled. Moreover, within this linear representation, the inversion conditions provide a clearer characterization of the localization properties.

</details>


### [98] [On the Practical Impact of Local Linear Instabilities in Entropy-Stable Schemes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02081)
*Alex Bercik,David W. Zingg*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了熵稳定离散格式中的局部线性不稳定性问题，证明这种不稳定性产生的扰动增长通常很小、受振荡和边界模态主导、可通过数值耗散控制，且不直接推广到非线性通量格式，因此不构成高阶级数熵稳定格式的实际障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清熵稳定离散格式中局部线性不稳定性的本质，评估其对实际计算的影响，消除学界对此类格式鲁棒性的疑虑。

Method: 采用修正方程分析、数值实验、Floquet分析、几何通量推导扰动增长界等理论分析与数值验证相结合的方法。

Result: 局部线性不稳定性产生的扰动满足物理可解释的界且通常很小；增长由高频振荡和边界局域化非物理模态主导，易于通过少量数值耗散控制；该现象不直接推广到非线性两点通量格式；密度波问题中的鲁棒性缺陷更应归因于对数平均在近真空条件下的不良行为。

Conclusion: 局部线性不稳定性不构成高阶级数熵稳定格式实际应用中的障碍。

Abstract: Local linear instability refers to the linearized discrete operator exhibiting perturbation growth exceeding that of the corresponding continuous linearized problem. In the context of nonlinear entropy-stable discretizations, we argue that local linear instabilities should be interpreted as a source of numerical error whose practical impact is often negligible compared with other discretization errors. For split-form discretizations of the variable-coefficient linear advection equation, such as those resulting from linearizations of entropy-stable discretizations of the Burgers equation, perturbations can indeed exhibit unphysical modal growth. However, we demonstrate that this growth satisfies physically interpretable bounds and is typically small. Furthermore, through modified-equation analysis and numerical experiments, we show that the growth is dominated by highly oscillatory and boundary-localized unphysical modes, and can therefore be readily controlled by small amounts of numerical dissipation. More generally, this modal perturbation growth does not extend directly to nonlinear two-point-flux discretizations of the type used in entropy-stable discretizations of the Euler equations. Floquet analysis demonstrates that unstable spectra of frozen-baseflow Jacobians need not lead to unstable perturbation growth. Using the geometric flux for the variable-coefficient linear advection equation, we derive a sharp perturbation growth bound predicting negligible growth, then show analogous behaviour for the logarithmic flux numerically. Finally, we argue that robustness issues observed for entropy-stable schemes in density-wave problems are better attributed to poor near-vacuum behaviour of the logarithmic mean than to local linear instabilities. Overall, our results suggest that local linear instabilities do not pose a practical obstacle to the use of high-order entropy-stable schemes.

</details>


### [99] [A Structure-Preserving Stagewise Rescaling Algorithm for a Two-Dimensional Nonlocal MEMS Equation in an Asymptotically Constant-Feedback Regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02237)
*Takiko Sasaki,Tetsuji Tokihiro*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对二维非局部MEMS方程在渐近常数反馈接触（touchdown） regime下的逐阶段重缩放算法进行研究，证明了重缩放能量的精确耗散恒等式和离散能量不等式，并提出了基于缺陷平衡的几乎单调性准则以及全局可容许延拓不存在的缺陷感知判据。数值实验展示了触发检测、固定阶段能量衰减和物理时间几何累积等特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非局部MEMS方程存在有限时间淬灭（touchdown）现象，数值捕捉极为困难，亟需发展能够处理该奇异行为的数值算法。

Method: 采用逐阶段重缩放算法，基于$A^{3/2}$–$A^3$重缩放（非精确不变，但在反馈有界收敛时合理）；引入最小移动阶段求解器，建立离散能量不等式；分离切换缺陷和外更新缺陷并证明精确缺陷平衡；提出均匀切换缺陷估计下的几乎单调性和缺陷感知判据。

Result: 在二维参考计算中实现了全域逐阶段运行，展示了触发检测、固定阶段能量衰减和物理时间几何累积；通过固定域能量检查验证了有限反馈诊断；识别出理想传递切换能量诊断，为后验验证提供依据。

Conclusion: 逐阶段重缩放算法能有效处理非局部MEMS方程的touchdown数值问题，缺陷平衡框架为算法的严格分析和后验验证提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: Nonlocal MEMS equations exhibit finite-time quenching, or touchdown, which is difficult to capture numerically. We study a stagewise rescaling algorithm for a two-dimensional nonlocal MEMS equation in an asymptotically constant-feedback touchdown regime. The equation is not exactly invariant under the $A^{3/2}$--$A^3$ scaling used here; the scaling is justified when the reciprocal-integral feedback $K(t)=1+\int_Ω(1-u)^{-1}dx$ remains bounded and converges to a finite positive limit, as in the single-point touchdown profiles of Duong--Zaag. In this regime the leading-order core dynamics reduce to a local MEMS equation with an asymptotically constant coefficient.
  Using a fixed-stage scaling of the deficit variable, we obtain a gradient flow for a rescaled energy at frozen amplitude and prove an exact energy dissipation identity within each stage. We introduce a minimizing-movement stage solver and derive a discrete energy inequality. Since strict monotonicity need not hold across stage transitions, we separate the switch and outer-update defects and prove an exact defect balance. Under a uniform switch-defect estimate, this yields quantitative almost monotonicity and a defect-aware criterion for nonexistence of a global admissible continuation. The numerical section is organized around reproducible two-dimensional reference computations: a full-domain stagewise run showing trigger detection, fixed-stage energy decay, and geometric accumulation of physical time, and a direct fixed-domain energy check. These tests are not used as proof of the bounded-window criterion; instead, they report finite-feedback diagnostics and identify the ideal-transfer switch-energy diagnostics required for a posteriori verification.

</details>


### [100] [A Finite Element Method for Elliptic Hemivariational Inequalities in Non-isotropic and Heterogeneous Semipermeable Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02451)
*Ban Li,Bangmin Wu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对半渗透介质中一类一般椭圆型半变分不等式，研究了非各向同性、非均匀扩散系数以及内部和边界半渗透项的有限元逼近，证明了解的存在唯一性，导出了最优先验误差估计，并通过数值实验验证了理论分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Han (2019)提出的各向同性和均匀框架扩展到更一般的非各向同性和非均匀情形，填补该方向的理论空白。

Method: 采用线性有限元方法进行逼近，在适当的解正则性假设下推导最优先验误差估计，并通过数值实验进行验证。

Result: 严格建立了解的存在唯一性，导出了线性有限元逼近的最优先验误差估计，数值实验确认了非各向同性和非均匀情形下的最优收敛速度。

Conclusion: 本文的理论分析和数值结果共同证实了所提出的有限元方法对于更一般的半渗透介质模型是有效且可靠的。

Abstract: This work investigates finite element approximations for a general class of elliptic hemivariational inequalities arising in semipermeable media. The proposed model incorporates non-isotropic and heterogeneous diffusion coefficients, alongside both interior and boundary semipermeability terms, extending the isotropic and homogeneous framework examined by Han (2019). The existence and uniqueness of solutions are rigorously established. An optimal a priori error estimate for the linear finite element approximation is derived under appropriate solution regularity assumptions. Numerical experiments are presented to corroborate the theoretical analysis and to confirm the optimal convergence rates for the non-isotropic and heterogeneous case.

</details>


### [101] [Structure-preserving LDG methods for linear and nonlinear transport equations with gradient noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02460)
*Thomas Christiansen,Kenneth H. Karlsen*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对具有异质随机通量的守恒律，发展了局部间断伽辽金（LDG）方法，通过将Stratonovich-Itô校正纳入数值格式，实现了离散能量层面的双曲稳定性，并通过Khasminskii型论证证明了格式的适定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 简化湍流模型、平均场博弈和波动流体动力学中出现的守恒律具有异质随机通量，其Stratonovich驱动的输运项可能为线性或非线性，需要设计能够保持能量稳定性的高精度数值方法。

Method: 从Itô公式出发构建半离散化格式，将输运噪声的抵消机制嵌入数值方法中；通过数值通量项平衡二阶Stratonovich-Itô校正与二次变差，保持双曲稳定性结构；采用Khasminskii型论证结合稳定性估计证明格式适定性，无需线性增长假设。

Result: 合适的数值通量可实现路径wise或期望意义下的离散能量守恒或耗散；所构建的高阶格式被证明是适定的；数值实验验证了稳定性和高阶精度。

Conclusion: 所提出的LDG方法能够有效处理异质随机通量的守恒律，在保持双曲稳定性结构的同时实现高阶精度，且适定性证明不依赖线性增长条件。

Abstract: We develop local discontinuous Galerkin (LDG) methods for conservation laws with heterogeneous stochastic fluxes, where the Stratonovich-driven transport terms may be linear or nonlinear. Such equations arise, for example, in simplified turbulence models, mean field games, and fluctuating hydrodynamics. Starting from the Itô formulation, we construct semi-discretizations that build the cancellation mechanism of transport noise into the numerical method. At the discrete energy level, the second-order Stratonovich-Itô correction is balanced by the quadratic variation, up to numerical flux terms, so that the hyperbolic stability structure is retained. Suitable numerical fluxes yield discrete energy conservation or energy dissipation, valid either pathwise or in expectation. The resulting high-order schemes are proved well posed through stability estimates combined with a Khasminskii-type argument, without imposing linear growth assumptions. Numerical experiments confirm stability and high-order accuracy.

</details>


### [102] [Well-Balanced Subcell Limiting for Discontinuous Galerkin Discretizations of the Shallow-Water Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02560)
*Andrés M. Rueda-Ramírez,Patrick Ersing,Andrew R. Winters,Gregor J. Gassner*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的通量差分格式，用于带子单元有限体积限制器的高阶间断伽辽金方法，在不破坏平衡性的前提下实现了节点级限制，并通过对浅水方程的重构设计交错DG通量，使非守恒贡献在平衡态下自然消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对于具有非守恒项的双曲平衡律系统（如带变地形的浅水方程），现有的混合DG/FV格式在结合节点级子单元限制时通常会破坏平衡性，即使底层DG和FV方法各自是平衡的。需要一种既能保持高阶精度和鲁棒性，又能保证精确平衡性的混合格式。

Method: 提出一种针对非守恒系统的新型通量差分格式：引入浅水方程的变形式，其中源项与总水深的梯度成正比；设计交错DG通量，使其非守恒贡献呈局部时间跳跃形式并在平衡态下单独消失；实现节点级的任意混合从而兼容低阶FV通量。

Result: 得到的DG/FV方法具有高阶精度、鲁棒性，且在节点级限制下严格满足平衡性。数值实验（包括含湿/干锋和复杂障碍物相互作用的二维溃坝问题）验证了改进的稳定性和精度。

Conclusion: 该方法虽以浅水方程为例，但所发展的平衡混合DG/FV方法可推广至更广泛的非线性平衡律系统。

Abstract: High-order discontinuous Galerkin (DG) methods equipped with subcell finite-volume (FV) limiters provide an efficient framework for the simulation of nonlinear hyperbolic balance laws featuring shocks and complex flow structures. However, for systems with non-conservative terms, the design of hybrid DG/FV schemes that simultaneously guarantee high-order accuracy, robustness, and well-balancedness remains challenging. In particular, for the shallow water equations with variable bottom topography, standard flux-differencing formulations combined with node-wise subcell limiting generally destroy the well-balanced property, even if both the underlying DG and FV methods are individually well-balanced.
  In this work, we propose a novel flux-differencing formulation for non-conservative systems that enables node-wise subcell limiting while preserving steady states exactly. The key idea is to construct staggered DG fluxes whose non-conservative contributions are in local-times-jump form and vanish individually at equilibrium. To achieve this, we introduce a reformulation of the shallow water equations in which the source term is proportional to the gradient of the total water height. This reformulation allows the design of staggered fluxes that preserve equilibrium locally at the node level, thereby enabling arbitrary nodal blending with low-order FV fluxes.
  The resulting DG/FV method is high-order accurate, robust, and exactly well-balanced under node-wise limiting. Numerical experiments, including two-dimensional dam-break configurations with wet/dry fronts and complex obstacle interactions, demonstrate the improved stability and accuracy of the proposed approach.
  Although this work focuses on the shallow water equations, the well-balanced hybrid DG/FV methods developed here are applicable to a broader class of nonlinear systems of balance laws.

</details>


### [103] [Efficient generation of Gaussian random fields on metric graphs via domain decomposition and mass matrix lumping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02670)
*Mihály Kovács,Gyula Molnár,Máté András Száraz*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出将Neumann-Neumann图分解与质量矩阵集中化相结合的方法，用于高效采样定义在度量图上的分数阶SPDE高斯随机场，在保持理论收敛率的同时实现多数量级加速和内存大幅降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 度量图上分数阶SPDE驱动的GRF的有限元近似采样需要Cholesky分解质量矩阵，在大规模图上会导致非线性时间爆炸和大量内存填充，亟需更高效的采样方法。

Method: 将Neumann-Neumann图分解与质量矩阵集中化（mass matrix lumping）相结合，替代直接Cholesky分解。

Result: 在保持文献[8]建立的理论收敛率的前提下，实现了多数量级的加速和巨大的内存缩减。

Conclusion: 所提出的组合方法在大规模度量图上的GRF采样中，既保证了理论收敛性，又显著提升了计算效率与内存效率。

Abstract: We consider Gaussian Random Fields on metric graphs defined implicitly as the stationary solution to a fractional SPDE driven by Gaussian white noise. Sampling from the finite element approximation requires the Cholesky factorization of the mass matrix, causing non-linear execution time explosions and massive memory fill-in on large graphs. Hence, we combine Neumann-Neumann graph decomposition with mass matrix lumping and demonstrate empirically, that our approach preserves exact theoretical convergence rates established in [8] while achieving multi-order speedups and massive memory reductions.

</details>


### [104] [Parameter estimation for evaporation-driven tear film model in two space dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02674)
*Qinying Chen,Tobin Driscoll*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文通过降维方法（POD）将蒸发驱动的泪膜 thinning 模型拟合到正常受试者的荧光成像数据，获得了体内蒸发相关参数估计，为干眼症研究建立了定量基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 泪膜破裂与干眼症密切相关，蒸发驱动的泪膜变薄是主要机制，但关键物理参数的体内定量估计仍然有限，缺乏有效方法从实验数据中提取这些参数。

Method: 采用Braun等人开发的蒸发驱动泪膜变薄模型，由Chen等人通过本征正交分解（POD）扩展到二维。利用降维技术高效求解偏微分方程组，并将模型拟合到正常受试者的荧光成像实验数据，进行参数估计。

Result: 获得了泪膜破裂区域内与蒸发相关的参数和变薄参数的体内估计值。

Conclusion: 研究加深了对泪膜变薄和干斑形成的理解，并为未来与干眼症患者数据的比较建立了定量基线。

Abstract: The tear film (TF) plays a critical role in maintaining ocular surface health, and its disruption through tear breakup (TBU) is closely associated with dry eye disease. Evaporation-driven thinning is a primary mechanism underlying TBU, yet quantitative in vivo estimates of key physical parameters remain limited. In this work, we fit an evaporation-driven TF thinning model, originally developed by Braun et al. and extended to two dimensions using proper orthogonal decomposition (POD) by Chen et al., to experimental fluorescence (FL) imaging data from normal subjects. The use of dimension reduction enables efficient solution of the governing PDEs and facilitates parameter estimation from imaging data. Our results provide in vivo estimates of evaporation-related and thinning parameters within TBU regions. These findings enhance understanding of TF thinning and dry-spot formation and establish a quantitative baseline for comparison with dry eye patient data.

</details>


### [105] [Factorization and monotonicity methods for reconstructing impenetrable obstacles in inverse biharmonic scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02802)
*Tielei Zhu,Zhihao Ge,Bangmin Wu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对双调和波（弹性板弯曲振动）的逆散射问题，建立了远场算子的新分解，通过因子化方法严格证明了支撑集刻画的有效性（除传输特征值外），并给出了全波数适用的单调性方法，数值实验验证了Dirichlet障碍物重建的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双调和波逆散射问题因四阶微分算子和高阶边界条件而具有区别于声学逆问题的分析挑战，现有方法无法直接适用，亟需建立适合双调和波动特征的障碍物重建理论。

Method: 利用双调和基本解的结构与边界条件性质，建立远场算子的新分解；证明分解满足范围恒等式；推导基于因子化方法的障碍物支撑刻画（排除传输特征值）；建立远场算子特征值的单调性关系，获得全波数适用的替代刻画。

Result: 因子化方法在非传输特征值波数下有效刻画障碍物支撑；单调性方法在全波数下均适用；数值实验展示了不同形状Dirichlet障碍物的有效重建。

Conclusion: 本文为双调和波逆散射问题提供了严格的因子化分析框架，克服了四阶算子带来的理论困难，并给出了全波数适用的单调性方法，拓展了因子化方法在高阶波动问题中的应用。

Abstract: The inverse scattering problem for biharmonic waves, governing flexural vibrations of elastic plates, presents fundamental analytical challenges distinct from acoustic inverse problems due to the fourth-order differential operator and higher-order boundary conditions. This paper addresses the reconstruction of impenetrable obstacles with Dirichlet or Neumann boundary conditions from far-field measurements. We establish new factorizations of the far-field operator by considering structures of the biharmonic fundamental solution and the boundary conditions. We rigorously prove that the factorizations satisfy the range identities and derive characterizations of the obstacle's support by the factorization methods, valid for all wavenumbers except the associated transmission eigenvalues. Furthermore, we establish a monotonicity relation for the eigenvalues of the far-field operator, which yields an alternative characterization of the obstacle's support that remains applicable for all wavenumbers. Numerical experiments for the Dirichlet obstacles with various shapes are presented to demonstrate the effectiveness and robustness of the proposed reconstruction schemes.

</details>


### [106] [Existence, Asymptotic Behavior, and Numerical Analysis of a Generalized Abel Differential Equation with Applications in Financial Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02831)
*Dragos-Patru Covei*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文对广义Abel常微分方程（将经典三次形式推广至任意n≥1次多项式非线性）进行了系统研究，包括解的存在唯一性、精确渐近平坦性、Sobolev空间正则性，并基于Radau IIA隐式Runge-Kutta格式提出了高阶数值框架，理论结果在Merton信用风险与HJB随机控制问题中具有应用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献缺乏对非三次多项式非线性Abel ODE的系统性处理，理论与数值方法均不完善，亟需建立统一的分析框架。

Method: 采用统一的基于屏障（barrier）的方法推导解的增长率和渐近平坦性；利用Sobolev空间与经典ODE理论证明正则性；数值方面使用Radau IIA隐式Runge-Kutta格式，并给出稳定性与误差分析。

Result: 推导出精确的渐近增长率并证明了渐近平坦（asymptotic plateaus）的存在性；数值结果与理论预测高度一致，尤其在捕捉渐近行为方面表现优异。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了广义Abel ODE系统性研究的空白，理论成果可应用于Merton信用风险模型与HJB随机控制问题等实际场景，桥接了抽象非线性动力学与应用科学之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: We present a comprehensive investigation into a generalized class of Abel ordinary differential equations (ODEs), extending the classical cubic form to arbitrary polynomial nonlinearities of degree $n \geq 1$. This work provides a rigorous treatment of the existence and uniqueness of regular solutions on both bounded and unbounded domains. Utilizing a unified barrier-based approach, we derive sharp growth rates and prove the existence of exact asymptotic plateaus, establishing the first systematic treatment of such generalizations in the literature. The regularity of solutions is rigorously justified through the lens of Sobolev spaces and classical ODE theory. To complement our analytical findings, we implement a high-order numerical framework based on Radau IIA implicit Runge--Kutta schemes, providing detailed stability arguments and error analysis. The numerical results demonstrate exceptional consistency with our theoretical predictions, particularly in capturing the asymptotic behavior. Finally, we discuss the implications of our results for real-world models, including Merton-type credit risk analysis and Hamilton--Jacobi--Bellman stochastic control problems, bridging the gap between abstract nonlinear dynamics and applied science.

</details>

### [107] [The Zero Slice of Quaternionic Real Bordism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01174)
*Bertrand J. Guillou,Jesse Keyes,David Mehrle*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文利用Hill-Hopkins-Ravenel范数构造了四元数群$Q_8$的$Q_8$-谱$N_{C_2}^{Q_8} \text{MU}\mathbb{R}$，并计算了该谱的零片以及该片的$\text{RO}(Q_8)$-分次同伦Mackey函子中的一个双次子环，为计算其片谱序列奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推进对$N_{C_2}^{Q_8} \text{MU}\mathbb{R}$的片谱序列的计算，特别是通过计算其零片及相关同伦Mackey函子来理解该谱的切片结构。

Method: 利用Hill-Hopkins-Ravenel范数构造$Q_8$-谱$N_{C_2}^{Q_8} \text{MU}\mathbb{R}$，并计算其零片以及$\text{RO}(Q_8)$-分次同伦Mackey函子中的双次子环。

Result: 得到了$N_{C_2}^{Q_8} \text{MU}\mathbb{R}$的零片及该片的$\text{RO}(Q_8)$-分次同伦Mackey函子中的一个双次子环的具体结构。

Conclusion: 该计算为后续完整计算$N_{C_2}^{Q_8} \text{MU}\mathbb{R}$的片谱序列提供了基础，有助于理解该谱的同伦性质。

Abstract: Using the Hill-Hopkins-Ravenel norm, one can produce a $Q_8$-spectrum $N_{C_2}^{Q_8} \text{MU}\mathbb{R}$, where $Q_8$ is the quaternion group. Working towards a computation of the slice spectral sequence for $N_{C_2}^{Q_8} \text{MU}\mathbb{R}$, we compute the zero slice of $N_{C_2}^{Q_8} \text{MU}\mathbb{R}$ and a bigraded subring of the $\text{RO}(Q_8)$-graded homotopy Mackey functors of this slice.

</details>


### [108] [Moment-angle manifolds associated to neighbourly triangulations of spheres](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01396)
*Amaranta Membrillo Solis,Stephen Theriault*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文证明与奇数维球面的邻域三角剖分相关联的矩角流形同伦等价于两个球面乘积的连通和，解决了Buchstaber和Panov提出的问题，并将结果推广到广义矩角流形的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Buchstaber和Panov提出的关于矩角流形同伦等价结构的问题。

Method: 采用纯同伦论方法，证明与奇数维球面邻域三角剖分相关联的矩角流形同伦等价于两个球面乘积的连通和。

Result: 矩角流形同伦等价于两个球面乘积的连通和，结果可推广至广义矩角流形。

Conclusion: 解决了Buchstaber和Panov的公开问题，且方法具有可推广性。

Abstract: We show that a moment-angle manifold associated to a neighbourly triangulation of an odd dimensional sphere is homotopy equivalent to a connected sum of products of two spheres, resolving a problem of Buchstaber and Panov. The methods are entirely homotopy theoretic, allowing for an extension to a corresponding result in the case of generalized moment-angle manifolds.

</details>


### [109] [Weights of circle actions on oriented manifolds with isolated fixed points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01722)
*Donghoon Jang*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文证明了紧致有向流形上圆群作用的不动点处权重成对出现的现象，无需依赖各向同性子流形可定向的假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已知结论认为圆群作用的不动点处权重成对出现，但现有证明依赖于各向同性子流形可定向的假设，而该假设在一般情况下并不成立，因此需要给出不依赖该假设的证明。

Method: 本文提出了一种新的证明方法，不依赖于各向同性子流形的可定向性假设，从而在更一般的条件下证明了权重成对出现的现象。

Result: 成功证明了在紧致有向流形上圆群作用的不动点处权重成对出现，无需假设各向同性子流形可定向。

Conclusion: 该结果推广了权重成对现象的适用范围，使其在更一般的流形上成立，对圆群作用的拓扑与几何研究有重要意义。

Abstract: For an action of the circle group $S^1$ on a compact oriented manifold with isolated fixed points, there is a claim that weights at the fixed points occur in pairs. This phenomenon holds for other types of $S^1$-manifolds, e.g., (almost) complex, symplectic, and unitary manifolds. A known proof of this claim assumes that the isotropy submanifolds are orientable. However, this assumption does not hold in general. In this note, we prove the claim without relying on that assumption.

</details>


### [110] [Power set operads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02440)
*Mathieu Vallée*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文通过幂集函子的迭代应用，系统性地构建了集合论 Operad 的层级结构，将置换 Operad、交换三结合 Operad、替换 Operad、复合 Operad 以及相对单纯复形上的新 Operad 联系起来，并为多面体乘积构造提供了概念性解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在建立一种系统性的方法来构造集合论 Operad，揭示经典 Operad 之间的层级关系，并为多面体乘积（polyhedral products）理论中的多种构造提供统一的概念性解释。

Method: 通过对幂集函子的迭代应用：从置换 Operad 开始，第一次迭代得到交换三结合 Operad；第二次迭代得到 Ayzenberg 和 Abramyan–Panov 在多面体乘积理论中引入的替换 Operad 和复合 Operad；进而构造了基于相对单纯复形的新 Operad，其由连接多面体乘积（join polyhedral product）控制。

Result: 证明了替换 Operad 和复合 Operad 都是无限生成的；展示了任何余连续余完备对称幺半范畴中的态射在这两个 Operad 上都承载自然的代数结构，恢复出笛卡尔积、 smash 积和连接积多面体乘积作为拓扑空间上不同幺半结构的实例；构造了包含这两个 Operad 作为子 Operad 的新 Operad；证明了无内顶点的分段线性球体对及其边界球面构成一个子 Operad，扩展了分段线性球面上 J-构造的稳定性。

Conclusion: 所建立的 Operad 层级为多面体乘积构造的多样性提供了概念性解释，并为进一步研究相关代数结构和拓扑构造奠定了基础。

Abstract: We introduce a systematic method for constructing set-theoretic operads via iterated application of the power set functor, and use it to uncover a hierarchy connecting several classical operads.
  Starting from the permutative operad, the first iteration recovers the commutative triassociative operad. The second iteration produces the substitution operad and the composition operad on simplicial complexes, two structures introduced by Ayzenberg and Abramyan--Panov in the theory of polyhedral products; we prove that both are infinitely generated.
  This hierarchy yields a conceptual explanation for the multiplicity of polyhedral product constructions: the arrows of any cocontinuous cocomplete symmetric monoidal category carry natural algebra structures over both operads, recovering the Cartesian, smash, and join polyhedral products as instances for different monoidal structures on topological spaces.
  Going further, we construct a new operad on relative simplicial complexes, governed by the join polyhedral product, which contains both the composition and the substitution operads as suboperads. As an application, pairs of piecewise-linear balls without interior vertices with their boundary spheres form a suboperad, extending the stability of the $J$-construction on piecewise-linear~spheres.

</details>


### [111] [Equivariant CW-complexes homotopy equivalent to spheres: a survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02760)
*Ian Hambleton,Ergun Yalcin*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 关于CW复形上有限群作用及相关主题的综述，主要基于作者的合作工作，重点应用于同伦等价于球面的有限G-CW复形，并提供了该领域的文献概览。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对有限群在CW复形上的作用及相关主题进行综述，特别是同伦等价于球面的有限G-CW复形，旨在为读者提供该领域的简明文献概览。

Method: 以综述形式整理和分析有限群作用在CW复形上的理论，主要基于作者本人的合作研究成果，涵盖该领域的大量文献。

Result: 给出了关于有限G-CW复形（同伦等价于球面）的应用，以及该领域广泛文献的简要概述。

Conclusion: 该综述为有限群作用在CW复形上的研究提供了简明的整体视角，特别关注同伦球面上的群作用，并为未来研究提供了文献基础。

Abstract: This is a survey about finite group actions on CW-complexes and related topics, primarily based on our joint work. The main applications are to finite $G$-CW-complexes which are homotopy equivalent to spheres. We have tried to give a fairly short overview of the extensive literature in this area, and we apologize in advance for our oversights and omissions.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [112] [LiDAR for Rehabilitation: A Comprehensive Survey of Applications, AI Techniques, and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00879)
*Soumia Siyoucef,Najmeddine Dhieb,Hakim Ghazzai,Eleonora Guanziroli,Franco Molteni,Gianluca Setti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文综述了2019-2025年间LiDAR在康复、伤后护理和医院环境中的应用，涵盖3D身体扫描、步态分析、机器人康复系统、实时监控与活动识别，并分析了基于学习的处理技术，是首个针对LiDAR康复应用的全面综述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统康复监测方法（摄像头存在隐私问题，可穿戴传感器不舒适且易出错）存在局限性，亟需一种更优的替代方案。LiDAR作为一种有潜力的传感器，其在康复领域的应用尚缺乏系统性综述。

Method: 对2019-2025年间LiDAR在康复中的应用文献进行系统综述，涵盖独立LiDAR的3D身体扫描与步态分析、机器人系统上的LiDAR、实时监控与环境扫描、活动与位置识别；并采用统计分析和学习-based处理方法分析当前趋势与空白。

Result: 识别了LiDAR在康复领域的主要应用方向和技术趋势，揭示了当前研究中的空白与未来机遇，并指出这是首个针对LiDAR康复应用的全面综述。

Conclusion: LiDAR在康复应用中展现了显著潜力，尤其在隐私保护和舒适性方面优于摄像头和可穿戴传感器；但仍有诸多开放挑战待解决，未来需要更多研究填补空白。

Abstract: Rehabilitation aims to help patients with limited mobility regain their physical abilities through targeted movements, exercises, stimulation, and other therapeutic methods. Recent advances in technology have introduced sensor-based systems into rehabilitation and clinical practices, enabling real-time monitoring and providing accurate feedback on movement accuracy. Among these sensors, LiDAR has demonstrated strong potential, offering key advantages over conventional techniques such as camera-based systems, which raise privacy concerns, and wearable sensors, which can be uncomfortable and prone to errors. In this work, we review the applications of LiDAR in rehabilitation, post-injury care, and hospital environments, focusing on studies published between 2019 and 2025. Studies across several areas have been explored: 3D body scanning and gait analysis with standalone LiDAR, LiDAR mounted on robotic systems for rehabilitation, real-time monitoring and environment scanning for safe navigation, and activity and position recognition. We also analyze processing techniques, particularly learning-based approaches, and support the discussion with statistical analysis, highlighting trends, gaps, and future research opportunities. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first comprehensive survey dedicated to LiDAR for rehabilitation applications, providing an overview of current methods, AI-based processing techniques, and open challenges.

</details>


### [113] [ARIS: Agentic and Relationship Intelligence System for Social Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00943)
*Stavya Datta,Fucai Ke,Leimin Tian,Hamid Rezatofighi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ARIS，一个融合多模态推理、基于图的社交世界模型和RAG的智能体AI框架，用于社交机器人。在Pepper机器人上的用户研究（N=23）表明，ARIS在感知智能、活力、拟人化和喜爱度方面显著优于LLM基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基础模型在社交机器人应用中虽提升了感知和交互能力，但仍难以应对多轮对话、社交关系推理和上下文情境化的大规模对话。亟需一个能统一处理这些挑战的系统架构。

Method: ARIS采用模块化智能体架构，包含三个核心组件：(1) 社交世界模型——通过知识图谱显式映射和更新用户间社交关系，支持社交推理和用户跨会话重识别；(2) 基于RAG的高效对话流水线——在对话历史扩展至数千轮时仍保持有限延迟和回答相关性；(3) 系统集成——通过结构化API协调语音、视觉和物理动作。

Result: 在Pepper机器人的双人对话场景中，用户研究（N=23）显示ARIS在感知智能、活力、拟人化和喜爱度四个维度上均显著高于LLM基线。

Conclusion: ARIS通过社交世界建模、RAG高效对话和模块化架构三个贡献，有效提升了社交机器人的多轮对话和社交关系推理能力，并将在发表后开源。

Abstract: Foundational models have advanced social robotics, enabling richer perception and communicative interaction with users. However, current systems still struggle with multi-turn engagement, social-relationship reasoning, and contextually grounded dialogue at scale. We present ARIS (Agentic and Relationship Intelligence System), an agentic AI framework that unifies multimodal reasoning, a graph-based Social World Model, and retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) within a single modular architecture for social robots. We evaluate ARIS with the Pepper robot in a robot-mediated dyadic conversational setting, comparing it against a large language model baseline. A user study (N=23) shows that ARIS yields significantly higher perceived intelligence, animacy, anthropomorphism, and likeability. Our contributions are threefold: (1)~a Social World Model that explicitly maps and updates social relationships between users through a knowledge graph, enabling social reasoning and re-identification across encounters; (2)~an efficient RAG-based conversational pipeline that maintains bounded latency as dialogue histories grow to thousands of exchanges while preserving response relevance; and (3)~system integration and empirical validation of these components within a modular agentic architecture that coordinates speech, vision, and physical action through structured APIs. The implementation of ARIS will be released as open source upon publication.

</details>


### [114] [Ablation Study of Multimodal Perception, Language Grounding, and Control for Human-Robot Interaction in an Object Detection and Grasping Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00963)
*Zi Tian,Guanting Shen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过受控消融实验，系统评估了人机交互系统中三个关键模块（大语言模型、感知系统和控制器）对端到端性能的影响，并分析了不同组件组合在执行时间和成功率上的表现差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清多模态人机交互系统中各模块对端到端性能的独立贡献，明确哪些组件主要影响执行时间、哪些主要影响成功率，为未来系统改进提供工程指导。

Method: 采用两阶段实验设计：第一阶段对3种语言模型、5种感知配置和3种控制器分别进行消融对比；第二阶段对最佳候选进行因子实验，评估端到端组合性能。

Result: 揭示了不同模块对执行时间和成功率的差异化影响，明确了未来工程改进中最大收益来源。

Conclusion: 通过系统化的组件级消融研究，能够有效定位影响性能的关键瓶颈，为后续系统迭代提供明确方向。

Abstract: This manuscript extends our previous multimodal human-robot interaction system by introducing a controlled ablation study of the three modules that most strongly influence end-to-end performance: the large language model used for action extraction, the perception system used for visual grounding, and the controller used for motion execution. The goal is not to redesign the full pipeline, but to isolate the contribution of each component under a common experimental protocol and then evaluate the best combinations end-to-end. We therefore compare three language models, five perception configurations, and three controllers, followed by a second-stage factorial study over the best candidates. The resulting analysis is intended to clarify which choices primarily affect execution time, which primarily affect success rate, and where the largest engineering gains are likely to come from in future revisions of the system.

</details>


### [115] [Value Functions for Temporal Logic: Optimal Policies and Safety Filters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01051)
*Oswin So,William Sharpless,Sylvia Herbert,Chuchu Fan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了无限时域无折扣设定下，针对时序逻辑规范（特别是嵌套Until、Globally、Globally-Until）的值函数最优性与策略最优性之间的微妙关系，发现贪心最大化Q函数可能导致策略无限延迟任务完成。作者基于值函数分解构建非马尔可夫历史依赖策略，证明了其在量化鲁棒性评分下的最优性，并展示了Q函数作为复杂时序逻辑规范安全滤波器的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Bellman方程在处理无限时域无折扣设定下的复杂时序逻辑任务（如嵌套Until、Globally）时，贪心最大化Q函数可能导致策略无限延迟任务完成的病态行为，现有方法缺乏对这类问题的理论分析和解决方案。

Method: 基于近期的时序逻辑值函数分解方法，将TL值函数分解为多个构成值函数的图结构，据此构建基于状态历史的非马尔可夫策略以回避贪心病态，并证明其在量化鲁棒性评分下的最优性；同时扩展Q函数作为安全滤波器的方法至更复杂的TL规范。

Result: 证明了所提出的非马尔可夫历史依赖策略在嵌套Until、Globally以及Globally-Until规范下，针对量化鲁棒性评分的最优性；并将Q函数作为安全滤波器的应用范围从简单的避障或到达-避障任务扩展至复杂时序逻辑规范。

Conclusion: 对于复杂时序逻辑规范，标准贪心策略存在缺陷，而基于历史信息的非马尔可夫策略能够实现最优性，同时Q函数可作为复杂TL规范的安全滤波器，拓展了安全强化学习在时序逻辑任务中的应用。

Abstract: While Bellman equations for basic reach, avoid, and reach-avoid problems are well studied, the relationship between value optimality and policy optimality becomes subtle in the undiscounted infinite-horizon setting, particularly for more complicated tasks. Greedily maximizing the Q-function can produce policies that indefinitely defer task completion for reach-avoid problems, or equivalently, Until specifications, even when the value function is optimal. Building upon recent results decomposing the value function for temporal logic (TL) into a graph of constituent value functions, we construct non-Markovian policies based on state history that avoid this pathology and prove their optimality with respect to the quantitative robustness score for nested Until, Globally, and Globally-Until specifications. We further show how the Q function can serve as a safety filter for complex TL specifications, extending prior results beyond simple avoid or reach-avoid tasks.

</details>


### [116] [Online Safety Filter for Deformable Object Manipulation with Horizon Agnostic Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01069)
*Jiaxing Li,Hanjiang Hu,Zhuoyuan Wang,Yorie Nakahira,Changliu Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于约束驱动的在线安全滤波器，用于变形物体（如流体）的机器人操作任务。该方法结合了水平无关的神经算子（学习偏微分方程边界输入输出映射）和边界控制障碍函数（通过轻量级二次规划实时认证任务级安全约束），能够在不改变名义策略的前提下在线滤除不安全行为。在FluidLab上的流体操作实验中，该方法相比基于奖励塑造的基线策略，安全轨迹率提升最高22%，且到达安全集所需步数更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的变形体操作（流体、布料、软体）方法通过奖励塑造间接编码安全性，无法保证部署时的约束满足，缺乏严格的安全保证。

Method: 1) 水平无关神经算子（Horizon Agnostic Neural Operator）：学习偏微分方程动力学边界输入输出映射，跨可变滚动长度泛化且无需重新训练；2) 边界控制障碍函数（Boundary Control Barrier Function）：通过轻量级二次规划在任务相关输出层面认证安全性，使安全约束在边界输入率上呈仿射形式，支持实时在线滤波。

Result: 在FluidLab流体操作任务上，安全滤波器相比未滤波的基线策略，安全轨迹率提升最高22%，到达安全集所需步数更少。

Conclusion: 约束驱动的安全性执行比奖励塑造方法更可靠且更高效，为变形体操作提供了实时任务级安全保障。

Abstract: Safety critical control of robotic manipulation tasks involving deformable media such as fluids, cloth, and soft objects remains challenging because existing learning based approaches encode safety indirectly through reward shaping, which provides no guarantee of constraint satisfaction at deployment. We present a constraint driven online safety filter for deformable object manipulation that enforces explicit task level safety constraints in real time by minimally modifying any nominal control policy. Our approach combines two key components: a horizon agnostic neural operator that learns the boundary input output mapping of the underlying PDE dynamics and generalizes across variable rollout lengths without retraining, and a boundary control barrier function that certifies safety at the task relevant output level via a lightweight quadratic program. The resulting safety constraint is affine in the boundary input rate, enabling real time online filtering. We evaluate the proposed method on fluid manipulation tasks in FluidLab, where the filter improves safe trajectory rates by up to 22% over unfiltered base policies while also reducing the number of steps required to reach the safe set, demonstrating that constraint driven safety enforcement is both more reliable and more efficient than reward shaping approaches.

</details>


### [117] [Sentinel-VLA: A Metacognitive VLA Model with Active Status Monitoring for Dynamic Reasoning and Error Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01191)
*Wenhao Li,Xiu Su,Yichao Cao,Hongyan Xu,Xiaobo Xia,Shan You,Yi Chen,Chang Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Sentinel-VLA，一种元认知视觉-语言-动作模型，通过主动哨兵模块监控执行状态，仅在需要时触发动态推理或错误恢复，结合自演化持续学习算法（SECL）和正交持续适配器（OC-Adapter），在44个任务、260万+过渡数据上训练，真实实验表明成功率比SOTA模型PI0提升30%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型面临推理能力有限、缺乏状态监控、难以自我纠错等关键挑战。

Method: 提出Sentinel-VLA，核心包括：(1) 主动哨兵模块实时监控执行状态，按需触发动态推理或错误恢复；(2) 自演化持续学习算法(SECL)自动识别能力边界并收集扩展数据；(3) 正交持续适配器(OC-Adapter)在正交空间约束参数更新以防止灾难性遗忘；(4) 全自动数据生成与标注流水线。

Result: 在44个任务、260万+过渡数据上训练，真实环境实验成功率比SOTA模型PI0提升超过30%。

Conclusion: 按需推理的元认知机制有效增强了VLA模型的鲁棒决策能力，SECL和OC-Adapter实现了高效持续学习，将开源全部代码、权重和数据生成流水线。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have advanced the field of embodied manipulation by harnessing broad world knowledge and strong generalization. However, current VLA models still face several key challenges, including limited reasoning capability, lack of status monitoring, and difficulty in self-correction. In this paper, we introduce \textbf{Sentinel-VLA}, a metacognitive VLA model equipped with an active ``sentinel'' module to monitor real-time execution status. Only when necessary, such as during initial planning or upon detecting an error, the model triggers a dynamic reasoning or formulate error recovery solutions. This on-demand reasoning mechanism ensures robust decision-making while minimizing computational overhead. Notably, all training data (spanning 44 tasks and over 2.6 million transitions) is automatically generated and annotated through our designed pipeline. We also propose the Self-Evolving Continual Learning (SECL) algorithm, which allows Sentinel-VLA to identify its capability boundaries and automatically collect data for expansion, paired with Orthogonal Continual Adapter (OC-Adapter) to constrain parameter updates to an orthogonal space, thereby preventing catastrophic forgetting. Real-world experiments demonstrate that Sentinel-VLA boosts the task success rate by over 30\% compared to the SOTA model, PI0. We will open-source all the code, weights, and data generation pipeline.

</details>


### [118] [VLA-ATTC: Adaptive Test-Time Compute for VLA Models with Relative Action Critic Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01194)
*Wenhao Li,Xiu Su,Dan Niu,Yichao Cao,Hongyan Xu,Zhe Qu,Lei Fan,Shan You,Chang Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VLA-ATTC通过不确定性驱动的“认知离合器”让VLA模型在复杂场景下自适应分配测试时计算资源，利用相对动作批评家（RAC）模型进行成对比较选出最优动作，在LIBERO-LONG基准上将SOTA模型PI0.5的失败率降低超过50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型依赖快速、本能的决策过程，缺乏深思熟虑，在复杂或模糊场景下常产生次优甚至灾难性动作。

Method: 提出VLA-ATTC框架，包含：1) 基于不确定性的“认知离合器”动态切换反射执行与测试时计算（TTC）推敲阶段；2) 相对动作批评家（RAC）模型通过成对比较从候选动作中识别最优动作，用相对机制替代不稳定的绝对值估计；3) 高效采样策略分摊计算成本；4) 自动化数据流水线无需人工标注即可整理偏好对。

Result: 在LIBERO-LONG基准上，将SOTA模型PI0.5的失败率降低超过50%。

Conclusion: VLA-ATTC通过自适应测试时计算和相对比较机制显著提升了VLA模型在复杂操作任务中的决策质量和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have demonstrated remarkable capabilities and generalization in embodied manipulation. However, their decision-making relies on a fast, instinctive process that lacks deliberation. This strategy often leads to suboptimal or catastrophic actions when facing complex or ambiguous scenarios that require greater consideration. In this paper, we introduce \textbf{VLA-ATTC}, a framework that endows VLA models with adaptive test-time compute (TTC). VLA-ATTC employs an uncertainty-based ``cognitive clutch'' to dynamically transition from reflexive execution to a TTC deliberation phase when necessary. During TTC phase, a novel \textbf{Relative Action Critic} (RAC) model identifies the optimal action from generated candidates via pairwise comparisons. This relative mechanism replaces unstable absolute value estimation, significantly simplifying the learning objective. Furthermore, we introduce an efficient sampling strategy to amortize computational costs and an automated data pipeline that curates preference pairs without manual annotation. On the LIBERO-LONG benchmark, VLA-ATTC reduces the failure rate of the SOTA model PI0.5 by over 50\%. We will open-source all the code and weights.

</details>


### [119] [TAIL-Safe: Task-Agnostic Safety Monitoring for Imitation Learning Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01195)
*Riad Ahmed,Momotaz Begum*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TAIL-Safe 提出了一种基于 Lipschitz 连续 Q 值函数的方法，用于识别模仿学习策略的安全集，并在策略即将偏离安全集时通过梯度上升进行恢复，从而显著提升机器人在扰动下的任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的模仿学习算法（如流匹配和扩散策略）在复杂操作任务中表现优异，但由于对初始条件极度敏感和不可约的近似误差，即使在训练分布内也经常失败，导致策略无法安全部署于存在分布外场景的真实环境中。因此，亟需一种方法使策略能够判断自身能否安全执行任务。

Method: 提出一个 Lipschitz 连续的 Q 值函数，将状态-动作对映射到基于三个短期任务无关准则（可见性、可识别性、可抓取性）的长期安全分数。该函数的零上水平集刻画了经验控制不变集。当策略提出的动作超出该集合时，受 Nagumo 定理启发，采用梯度上升方法将策略引导回安全集。为学习该 Q 值函数，利用高斯泼溅构建高保真数字孪生以系统收集故障数据。

Result: 在 Franka Emika 机器人上的实验表明，原本在运行时扰动下失败的流匹配策略，在 TAIL-Safe 引导下能够实现一致的任务成功。

Conclusion: TAIL-Safe 提供了一种原则性的安全集识别与恢复机制，能够有效提升模仿学习策略在扰动环境下的安全性与可靠性，为策略的实地部署奠定了基础。

Abstract: Recent imitation learning (IL) algorithms such as flow-matching and diffusion policies demonstrate remarkable performance in learning complex manipulation tasks. However, these policies often fail even when operating within their training distribution due to extreme sensitivity to initial conditions and irreducible approximation errors that lead to compounding drift. This makes it unsafe to deploy IL policies in the field where out-of-distribution scenarios are prevalent. A prerequisite for safe deployment is enabling the policy to determine whether it can execute a task the way it was learned from demonstrations. This paper presents TAIL-Safe, a principled approach to identify, for a trained IL policy, a safe set from where the policy empirically succeeds in completing the learned task. We propose a Lipschitz-continuous Q-value function that maps state-action pairs to a long-term safety score based on three short-term task-agnostic criteria: visibility, recognizability, and graspability. The zero-superlevel set of this function characterizes an empirical control invariant set over state-action pairs. When the nominal policy proposes an action outside this set, we apply a recovery mechanism inspired by Nagumo's theorem that uses gradient ascent to the Q-function to steer the policy back to safety. To learn this Q-function, we construct a high-fidelity digital twin using Gaussian Splatting that enables systematic collection of failure data without risk to physical hardware. Experiments with a Franka Emika robot demonstrate that flow-matching policies, which fail under run-time perturbations, achieve consistent task success when guided by the proposed TAIL-Safe.

</details>


### [120] [To Do or Not to Do: Ensuring the Safety of Visuomotor Policies Learned from Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01201)
*Riad Ahmed,Moniruzzaman Akash,Momotaz Begum*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向模仿学习（IL）策略的通用安全保障机制——执行保证（Execution Guarantee），通过结合视图合成与Nagumo次切性条件，从状态空间中识别安全区域并保证策略在该区域内实现最大任务成功率，从而缓解IL中安全与性能的权衡问题。在Franka机器人上的仿真和实物实验验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统模仿学习研究以任务成功率为唯一性能指标，忽视了现场机器人应用中安全保证的关键性。现有IL研究缺乏统一的策略安全定义，且经典控制理论中的安全方法难以直接迁移到IL场景中。

Method: 提出执行保证（Execution Guarantee）这一策略无关的安全度量；利用视图合成技术识别状态空间中的安全区域；基于Nagumo次切性条件（集合不变性理论）证明并实现从该区域内执行策略的安全保证。

Result: 在Franka机器人的仿真和真实实验中，所提安全分析方法使多种IL策略在保证安全的前提下实现最大任务成功率；同时，作为方法副产物的恢复策略（Recovery Policy）能够有效提升策略性能，缓解安全-性能权衡。

Conclusion: 执行保证为IL策略提供了可操作的安全度量标准，弥合了IL理论与现场机器人安全需求之间的鸿沟，并展示了恢复策略在改善安全-性能权衡中的潜力。

Abstract: Task success has historically been the primary measure of policy performance in imitation learning (IL) research. This characteristics strictly limits the ubiquitous applications of IL algorithms in field robotics where safety assurance, in addition to task-success, is of paramount importance. It is often desirable for an IL-powered robot in the field not to roll out a policy, and hence score a poor performance, if the safety is not guaranteed. Although this trade-off between safety and performance is well investigated in classical control literature, policy safety is a heavily underexplored domain in IL research. There is no universal definition of safety in IL. To make things worst, many existing theoretical works on safety is notoriously difficult to extend to IL-powered robots in the field. This paper offers important insights on the safety and performance of IL policies. We propose execution guarantee, a policy-agnostic safety measure that guarantees the maximum task success for a visuomotor IL policy, despite minor run-time changes, from within a specific region in the state space. We leverage recent advances in view synthesis to identify such regions in the state space for an IL policy and explore a fundamental result on set invariance - namely, Nagumo's sub-tangentiality condition - to prove and operationalize execution guarantee from inside that region. Experiments with a Franka robot, both in simulation and real world, demonstrate how the proposed safety analysis allows various IL policies to achieve maximum task success with guarantee. We also demonstrate some interesting results on how a recovery policy - a by-product of the proposed safety analysis - can help to increase the policy performance and thereby mitigating the safety-performance tradeoff in IL.

</details>


### [121] [Dynamics Aware Quadrupedal Locomotion via Intrinsic Dynamics Head](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01227)
*Aman Arora,Nalini Ratha*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种新型四足机器人训练框架，通过联合训练控制策略与内在动力学（ID）头，使策略能够理解并推理物理动力学，在仿真和真实机器人上均显著提升了运动效率和平滑性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足机器人的敏捷、多样化运动依赖于对底层物理动力学的理解与估计，现有方法在这一点上存在不足，因此需要一种能让控制策略主动理解和推理动力学特性的训练框架。

Method: 在仿真中同时训练控制策略和内在动力学（ID）头（学习状态到扭矩的映射），并定义基于ID头的动力学奖励以鼓励策略产生更可预测的动态行为；通过调节ID头的训练系数来调整策略中学到的动力学特性。

Result: 仿真实验表明该方法能收敛到更优的最优点，产生更高效和平滑的策略；真实机器人实验中，扭矩效率提升16.8%，动作频率降低18.6%，机械功率降低12.8%，安全扭矩占用率提升6.4%。

Conclusion: 提出的训练框架能有效将动力学理解融入四足机器人控制策略，显著提升运动效率与安全性，且具备良好的仿真到现实的迁移能力。

Abstract: Quadrupedal locomotion plays a critical role in enabling agile, versatile movement across complex terrains. Understanding and estimating the underlying physical dynamics are essential for achieving efficient and stable quadrupedal locomotion. We propose a novel training framework for quadrupedal locomotion that enables the Control Policy to understand and reason about physical dynamics. In simulation, we concurrently train an Intrinsic Dynamics (ID) Head that learns state-to-torque dynamics alongside the Control Policy, and we define a dynamics reward enabled by the ID Head that encourages the Policy toward more predictable dynamical behavior. We also provide a mechanism to tune the learned dynamics in the resulting Policy by controlling the training coefficients of the ID Head. Our simulation experiments show that this mechanism drives convergence to better optima across a wide range of standard quadrupedal locomotion rewards, yielding more efficient and smoother policies. Our real-robot experiments demonstrate sim-to-real transfer of these improvements, with significant gains in torque efficiency (16.8%), action rate (18.6%), and mechanical power (12.8%), while improving safe torque occupancy by 6.4%.

</details>


### [122] [A Principled Approach for Creating High-fidelity Synthetic Demonstrations for Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01232)
*Moniruzzaman Akash,Momotaz Begum*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于3DGS和DMP的专家轨迹保持的演示合成框架，通过解析障碍物感知的DMP在连续密度场上实现碰撞避免，生成保留专家运动结构的多样化演示，在接触密集型操作任务中优于现有规划器和优化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS合成管道使用采样规划器或轨迹优化生成新运动，偏离专家演示路径，丢弃了接触密集和形状敏感操作中关键的时空结构，导致演示多样性反而损害下游策略学习。

Method: 1) 用DMP建模专家轨迹，重定向到新目标、物体配置和视点，实现相位一致、形状保持的运动；2) 提出解析障碍物感知DMP，直接在3DGS的连续密度场上操作，在最小扰动专家运动的前提下实现碰撞避免。

Result: 在Spot移动机械臂的三个操作任务上，相比规划器和优化方法，该方法产生更低偏离和碰撞率的轨迹，训练扩散视觉运动策略时获得更高任务成功率。

Conclusion: 演示合成应将专家轨迹作为强先验，所提方法通过保持专家运动结构并安全适应新场景，能更好地支持下游策略学习。

Abstract: Recent advances in 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) have enabled visually realistic demonstration generation from a single expert trajectory and a short multi-view scan. However, existing 3DGS-based synthesis pipelines typically generate new motions using sampling-based planners or trajectory optimization, which often deviate substantially from the expert's demonstrated path. While such deviations may be acceptable for tasks insensitive to motion shape, they discard subtle spatial and temporal structure that is critical for contact-rich and shape-sensitive manipulation, causing increased demonstration diversity to harm downstream policy learning. We argue that demonstration synthesis should treat the expert trajectory as a strong prior. Building on this principle, we propose a framework that synthesizes diverse task demonstrations while explicitly preserving expert motion structure. We model the expert trajectory using Dynamic Movement Primitives (DMPs) and retarget it to new goals, object configurations, and viewpoints within a reconstructed 3DGS scene, yielding phase-consistent, shape-preserving motion by construction. To safely realize this expert-preserving diversity in cluttered scenes, we introduce an analytic obstacle-aware DMP formulation that operates directly on the continuous density field induced by the 3DGS representation. This enables collision avoidance while minimally perturbing the nominal expert motion, unifying photorealistic rendering and geometric reasoning without additional scene representations. We evaluate our approach on a Spot mobile manipulator across three manipulation tasks with increasing sensitivity to trajectory fidelity. Compared to planner- and optimization-based synthesis, our method produces trajectories with lower deviation and collision rates and yields higher task success when training diffusion-based visuomotor policies.

</details>


### [123] [Bi-Level Reinforcement Learning Control for an Underactuated Blimp via Center-of-Mass Reconfiguration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01289)
*Xiaorui Wang,Hongwu Wang,Yue Fan,Hao Cheng,Feitian Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种双层强化学习框架，用于解决带有重心（CoM）重构的欠驱动飞艇的目标跟踪控制问题，通过任务级CoM规划与连续推力控制的显式解耦，实现了比固定CoM基线和PID控制器更高的跟踪精度和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统飞艇采用过驱动设计（冗余执行器），导致能效和有效载荷能力受限。本文旨在通过紧凑架构（两个推进器+可移动内部滑块）提升能效与载荷，但该配置引入严重欠驱动和非线性耦合（CoM动力学与车辆运动之间），现有控制方法难以应对。

Method: 提出双层强化学习框架：外层策略在飞行前根据目标确定CoM配置，内层策略生成推力命令以跟踪直线参考。采用两阶段学习策略，并进行了双层过程的收敛性分析。

Result: 在27个目标点的评估集上，通过大量仿真和真实实验验证，所提方法在跟踪精度、鲁棒性和sim-to-real迁移能力上均一致优于固定CoM基线和PID控制器。

Conclusion: 提出的双层强化学习框架有效解决了欠驱动飞艇的CoM重构控制问题，实现了硬件高效配置下的高性能目标跟踪控制。

Abstract: This paper investigates goal-directed tracking control of underactuated blimps with center-of-mass (CoM) reconfiguration. Unlike conventional overactuated blimp designs that rely on redundant actuation for simplified control, this paper focuses on a compact architecture consisting of two thrusters and a movable internal slider, aiming to improve energy efficiency and payload capacity. This hardware-efficient configuration introduces significant underactuation and strong nonlinear coupling between CoM dynamics and vehicle motion. To address these challenges, this paper proposes a bi-level reinforcement learning framework that explicitly decouples task-level CoM planning from continuous thrust control. The outer policy determines a target-dependent CoM configuration prior to flight, while the inner policy generates thrust commands to track straight-line references. To ensure stable learning, this paper introduces a two-stage learning strategy, supported by a convergence analysis of the resulting bi-level process. Extensive simulations and real-world experiments on a 27-goal evaluation set demonstrate that the proposed method consistently outperforms fixed-CoM baselines and PID-based controllers, achieving higher tracking accuracy, enhanced robustness, and reliable sim-to-real transfer.

</details>


### [124] [Terrain Perception for Agricultural UAVs in Complex Farmland via Rotating mmWave Radar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01340)
*Zhihao Zhan,Le Tao,Shaobin Li,Chenxin Fang,Xingrui Yang,Liang Li,Rui Fan,Yuhang Ming*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于旋转毫米波雷达的农业无人机地形感知框架，通过机械旋转设计扩大视场角，并结合位姿一致的地形重建管线，在稀疏噪声雷达数据中实现了高精度地面分割（F1 94.42），优于对比方法（90.48），有效提升了复杂农田环境下的地形跟随飞行鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有农业无人机在复杂农田环境中的地形感知面临遮挡、地形复杂和环境干扰等挑战；传统固定视场角的毫米波雷达系统无法提供完整可靠的地形估计，亟需一种低成本、大覆盖且鲁棒的地形感知方案。

Method: 采用机械旋转式毫米波雷达设计以扩大空间覆盖范围；设计了一种位姿一致的地形重建管线，专门针对稀疏、噪声大且部分可观测的雷达数据进行地面提取和连续地形表面估计；整套系统部署于真实农业无人机平台。

Result: 地面分割F1分数达94.42，对比方法仅90.48；地形覆盖率和估计精度均显著提升，实现了更鲁棒的地形跟随飞行。

Conclusion: 旋转毫米波雷达结合专为稀疏雷达数据设计的重建管线，能有效提升农业无人机在复杂农田环境中的地形感知性能，为低成本、高鲁棒性的地形跟随飞行提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Accurate terrain perception is essential for terrain-following flight of agricultural unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs), yet remains challenging in real-world farmland due to occlusions, complex terrain geometry, and environmental disturbances. Millimeter-wave (mmWave) radar is a promising sensing modality for this task due to its robustness to adverse conditions; however, existing UAV-mounted radar systems rely on fixed field of view (FoV) and terrain extraction methods designed for dense LiDAR data, leading to incomplete and unreliable terrain estimation. To address these limitations, we present a low-cost rotating mmWave radar-enabled terrain perception framework for agricultural UAVs operating in complex farmland environments. Specifically, a mechanically rotating sensing design is introduced to enlarge spatial coverage and improve terrain observability beyond the limitations of fixed-view radar under dynamic low-altitude flight. Building upon this sensing capability, we further design a pose-consistent terrain reconstruction pipeline tailored for sparse, noisy, and partially observable radar data, enabling reliable ground extraction and continuous terrain surface estimation in challenging agricultural scenarios. The complete system is deployed on a real agricultural UAV platform and comprehensively evaluated through extensive field experiments. Experimental results demonstrate improved terrain coverage and estimation accuracy, achieving an F1 score of 94.42 for ground segmentation, while the closest rival only achieves 90.48. Thus, leading to more robust terrain following flight.

</details>


### [125] [Assistance Without Interruption: A Benchmark and LLM-based Framework for Non-Intrusive Human-Robot Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01368)
*Yuedi Zhang,Shuanghao Bai,Wanqi Zhou,Haoran Zhang,Qi Zhang,Zhirong Luan,Badong Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出非侵入式辅助作为人机交互的新范式，构建NIABench仿真基准，并提出结合LLM与评分模型的混合架构，在减少人类操作的同时保持任务有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HRI依赖直接指令、显式协商或基于历史习惯的主动干预，忽略了在不打断人类连续活动的前提下进行辅助的重要性，因此需要形式化非侵入式辅助这一独立范式。

Method: 提出混合架构：LLM集成评分模型。评分模型先通过语义检索剪枝候选动作集，再使用排序器评估人类步骤与机器人动作对的时序和跨步依赖关系，实现何时行动和做什么的联合决策。

Result: 在NIABench仿真基准和真实场景实验中，该方法实现了主动且非侵入式的辅助，在减少人类努力的同时保持了任务有效性。

Conclusion: 非侵入式辅助是HRI中可行且重要的独立范式，所提方法能有效平衡辅助的主动性与非侵入性。

Abstract: Human-robot interaction (HRI) has long studied how agents and people coordinate to achieve shared goals. In this work, we formalize and benchmark the non-intrusive assistance as an independent paradigm of HRI, where a robot proactively supports a human's ongoing multi-step activities while strictly avoiding interruptions. Unlike conventional HRI tasks that rely on direct commands, explicit negotiation, or proactive interventions based on user habits and history, our task treats the human's plan as the primary process and formulates assistance as a joint decision over when to act and what to do. To systematically evaluate this problem, we establish a simulation benchmark, NIABench, along with new metrics tailored to the non-intrusive assistance task. We further propose a hybrid architecture that integrates an LLM with a scoring model. The scoring model first applies semantic retrieval to prune large candidate action sets, and then a ranker evaluates human-step and robot-action pairs, enabling reasoning over timing and cross-step dependencies. Comprehensive experiments on both NIABench and real-world scenarios demonstrate that our method achieves proactive, non-intrusive assistance that reduces human effort while preserving task effectiveness.

</details>


### [126] [ESARBench: A Benchmark for Agentic UAV Embodied Search and Rescue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01371)
*Daoxuan Zhang,Ping Chen,Jianyi Zhou,Shuo Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出面向多模态大模型驱动的无人机搜索与救援任务ESAR及其综合评测基准ESARBench，基于UE5和AirSim构建高保真环境与600个真实救援案例任务，实验揭示了现有方法在空间记忆、空中适应性和搜索效率与安全平衡方面的关键瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机搜索与救援研究主要依赖传统视觉和路径规划方法，缺乏面向具身智能体的统一综合评测基准，亟需填补这一空白。

Method: 定义新任务ESAR（具身搜索与救援），要求无人机自主探索复杂环境、识别救援线索并推理受害者位置。基于UE5和AirSim构建四个高保真开放环境（映射真实GIS数据），包含动态天气、时段和线索随机放置等变量，创建600个基于真实救援案例的任务，提出一套稳健评测指标。

Result: 对传统启发式方法及基于MLLM的地面和空中ObjectNav智能体进行评测，实验揭示了ESAR中的关键挑战：空间记忆瓶颈、空中适应性不足、搜索效率与飞行安全之间的权衡。

Conclusion: ESARBench可作为推动具身搜索与救援领域研究的重要资源，相关代码和项目已开源。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) has empowered Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) with exceptional capabilities in spatial reasoning, semantic understanding, and complex decision-making, making them inherently suited for UAV Search and Rescue (SAR). However, existing UAV SAR research is dominated by traditional vision and path-planning methods and lacks a comprehensive and unified benchmark for embodied agents. To bridge this gap, we first propose the novel task of \textbf{Embodied Search and Rescue (ESAR)}, which requires aerial agents to autonomously explore complex environments, identify rescue clues, and reason about victim locations to execute informed decision-making. Additionally, we present \textbf{ESARBench}, the first comprehensive benchmark designed to evaluate MLLM-driven UAV agents in highly realistic SAR scenarios. Leveraging Unreal Engine 5 and AirSim, we construct four high-fidelity, large-scale open environments mapped directly from real-world Geographic Information System (GIS) data to ensure photorealistic landscapes. To rigorously simulate actual rescue operations, our benchmark incorporates dynamic variables including weather conditions, time of day, and stochastic clue placement. Furthermore, we create a dataset of 600 tasks modeled after real-world rescue cases and propose a robust set of evaluation metrics. We evaluate diverse baselines, ranging from traditional heuristics to advanced ground and aerial MLLM-based ObjectNav agents. Experimental results highlight the challenges in ESAR, revealing critical bottlenecks in spatial memory, aerial adaptation, and the trade-off between search efficiency and flight safety. We hope ESARBench serves as a valuable resource to advance research on Embodied Search and Rescue domain. Source code and project page: https://4amgodvzx.github.io/ESAR.github.io.

</details>


### [127] [SixthSense: Task-Agnostic Proprioception-Only Whole-Body Wrench Estimation for Humanoids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01427)
*Xingzhou Chen,Xiayan Xu,Yan Ning,Jiyu Yu,Yizheng Zhang,Siyi Qian,Lingzhu Xiang,Jiahao Chen,Yuquan Wang,Haodong Zhang,Ling Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SixthSense，一种仅利用本体感知和IMU数据推断人形机器人全身接触时间、位置和力的方法，采用条件流匹配技术处理非结构化接触输入与不确定运动输出之间的多模态动力学，在站立、行走和全身运动跟踪等策略上展现出前所未有的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人形机器人缺乏可靠的力交互能力，难以完成实际任务。估计外力受到浮动基座动力学和不明确接触位置的困扰，现有分析框架需要理想化假设和难以获取的测量，实际中往往不可用。

Method: 提出SixthSense，一种任务无关的方法，仅从本体感知和IMU数据推断全身接触时间、位置和力。采用条件流匹配对本体感知历史进行令牌化，估计时空稀疏的接触事件流，捕捉非结构化接触输入与不确定运动输出之间的多模态动力学。

Result: 在站立、行走和全身运动跟踪策略上的实验展示了在各种行为中前所未有的性能。SixthSense可作为即插即用的感知模块应用于碰撞检测、物理人机交互和力反馈遥操作。

Conclusion: SixthSense有效桥接了人形机器人接触感知的缺口，通过纯本体感知和IMU数据实现了可靠的全身接触估计，无需理想化假设和难以获取的测量，为实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Humanoid robots are entering our physical world at scale, yet as oversized toys--good at singing and dancing, but short on force-interaction capabilities for practical tasks. Bridging this gap necessitates prioritizing reliable contact perception as a fundamental requirement. Estimating external wrenches in humanoids is complicated by floating-base dynamics and indeterminate contact locations. Existing analytical frameworks require idealistic assumptions and hard-to-obtain measurements, which are often unavailable in practice. To bridge this gap, we propose SixthSense, a task-agnostic approach that infers whole-body contact timing, location, and wrenches from proprioception and IMU data alone. To capture the multi-modal dynamics between unstructured contact inputs and the uncertain motion outputs, we employ conditional flow matching to tokenize proprioceptive histories and estimate a spatiotemporally sparse contact-event flow. SixthSense serves as a plug-and-play perception module for applications including collision detection, physical human-robot interaction, and force-feedback teleoperation. Experiments across standing, walking, and whole-body motion-tracking policies showcased unprecedented performance in diverse behaviors.

</details>


### [128] [Evidence-Based Landing Site Selection and Vison-Based Landing for UAVs in Unstructured Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01432)
*Sina Sajjadi,Jacopo Panerati,Sina Soleymanpour,Varunkumar Mehta,Farrokh Janabi-Sharifi,Iraj Mantegh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于证据的概率框架，用于无人机在杂乱或非结构化环境中的自主着陆。该框架将不确定性下的决策与视觉伺服执行分离，通过递归累积帧级视觉似然来推断着陆安全性，并结合硬几何约束确保物理可行性。最终通过最大后验估计选择着陆点，并利用ORB特征跟踪和IBVS实现精确对准与下降。在真实实验室实验和Nvidia Isaac Sim高保真仿真中验证了该方法的一致、谨慎和稳定着陆性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机在杂乱或非结构化环境中自主着陆面临安全关键挑战，尤其是传感器不确定性和平台引起的扰动（如振动）导致的噪声感知问题。现有方法缺乏对不确定性进行显式建模和分离决策与执行的能力。

Method: 提出基于证据的概率框架：1) 将着陆安全性建模为潜在变量，通过平坦度、坡度和障碍物线索递归累积帧级视觉似然，生成时序一致的信度图；2) 基于最小着陆半径施加硬几何约束，拒绝尺寸过小的区域；3) 采用约束最大后验估计选择最终着陆点；4) 利用ORB特征跟踪锁定目标区域，通过IBVS实现精确对准与下降。

Result: 在真实实验室实验和Nvidia Isaac Sim高保真仿真中均验证了该方法在跨域场景下表现出一致、谨慎和稳定的着陆行为。

Conclusion: 本文所提框架通过显式分离不确定性决策与视觉伺服执行，结合概率推理与几何约束，有效提升了无人机在复杂环境中的自主着陆安全性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Autonomous landing in cluttered or unstructured environments remains a safety-critical challenge for unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs), particularly under noisy perception caused by sensor uncertainty and platform-induced disturbances such as vibration. This paper presents an evidence-based probabilistic framework for autonomous UAV landing that explicitly separates decision-making under uncertainty from execution via visual servoing. Landing safety is modeled as a latent variable and inferred through recursive accumulation of frame-wise visual likelihoods derived from flatness, slope, and obstacle cues, yielding a temporally consistent belief map that is robust to transient perception errors. Physical feasibility is enforced through a hard geometric constraint based on the minimum required landing radius of the UAV, ensuring that undersized but visually appealing regions are rejected. The final landing site is selected using constrained maximum a posteriori estimation. Once selected, the UAV locks onto the target region using ORB feature tracking and performs precise alignment and descent via image-based visual servoing (IBVS). The proposed approach is validated through both real-world laboratory experiments and high-fidelity simulations in Nvidia Isaac Sim, demonstrating consistent, cautious, and stable landing behavior across domains.

</details>


### [129] [High-Speed, Scalable Sensor Readout for Dexterous Robotic Hands via Shift-Register Multiplexing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01434)
*Jaehoon Kim,Lazaros Christoforidis,Michalis Papadakis,Victor Kartsch,Robert K. Katzschmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于SIPO移位寄存器原理的可扩展模拟传感器读取架构，在腱驱动机器人手上集成16个关节传感器模块和1个四通道触觉传感器模块，实现20个传感器通道以1 kHz全扫描速率采集，关节传感器最大斜率绝对百分比误差0.446%，触觉力估计RMSE为0.125 N，五类接触位置分类准确率93.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧机器人手需要高速多模态传感，但现有读取架构在传感器数量、布线复杂度和采样带宽之间存在权衡，亟需一种可扩展、高带宽、低布线复杂度的传感器读取方案。

Method: 基于串行输入并行输出（SIPO）移位寄存器原理设计可扩展模拟传感器读取架构，使用仅三条信号线连接传感器模块，支持异构模拟输出传感器的灵活集成和可配置采样。

Result: 在20通道传感器系统上实现1 kHz全扫描速率（稳定运行达1.5 kHz）；关节传感器最大斜率APE为0.446%，误差小于1度；触觉LSTM模型力估计RMSE为0.125 N，五类接触位置分类准确率93.4%，支持1 kHz实时推理。

Conclusion: 所提架构为全传感器化机器人手提供了一条实用路径，能在不显著退化传感性能的前提下实现可扩展高速多模态传感，灵巧操作场景下关节传感器比电机估算提供更精确的交互反馈，触觉传感器实现响应式力估计。

Abstract: Dexterous robotic hands require high-speed multimodal sensing across many degrees of freedom, yet existing readout architectures often impose trade-offs between sensor count, wiring complexity, and sampling bandwidth. This paper presents a scalable analog sensor readout architecture based on a serial-in parallel-out (SIPO) shift-register principle. The proposed architecture supports versatile integration of heterogeneous analog-output sensors, scalable expansion using only three signal lines between sensor modules, and fast, configurable sampling. We validate the approach on a tendon-driven robotic hand integrating 16 joint sensor modules and one four-channel tactile sensor module, enabling acquisition of 20 sensor channels at a full-scan rate of 1 kHz, with stable operation up to 1.5 kHz. Joint sensor characterization showed a maximum slope absolute percentage error (APE) of 0.446% and sub-degree estimation error, indicating that the proposed readout system does not significantly degrade sensing performance. For tactile sensing, LSTM-based models achieved an RMSE of 0.125 N for force estimation and 93.4% accuracy for five-class contact-location classification, and were deployed for real-time inference at 1 kHz. System-level experiments showed that the joint sensors provide more accurate feedback than motor-based estimation during interaction, while the tactile sensor enables responsive force estimation in contact. The proposed architecture offers a practical path toward fully sensorized robotic hands for dexterous manipulation.

</details>


### [130] [Decompose and Recompose: Reasoning New Skills from Existing Abilities for Cross-Task Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01448)
*Xitie Zhang,Aming Wu,Yahong Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Decompose and Recompose框架，通过原子技能-动作对作为中间表示，将演示分解为可解释的技能-动作对齐，再通过组合推理重组成技能以完成未见任务，实现零样本跨任务泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于上下文学习的方法仅提供低层连续动作序列作为上下文，无法捕获可组合的技能知识，导致模型退化为浅层轨迹模仿，难以实现跨任务泛化。

Method: 1) 将演示分解为原子技能-动作对作为中间表示；2) 通过视觉-语义检索结合规划智能体的技能序列构建任务自适应动态演示库；3) 引入覆盖感知静态库补充缺失技能模式；4) 两者共同提供技能全面的演示，显式引导模型进行技能组合和执行顺序的组合推理。

Result: 在AGNOSTOS基准测试和真实世界环境中验证了方法的零样本跨任务泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过将技能分解为原子表示再组合推理，可有效解决机器人操作中的跨任务泛化问题，优于仅提供低层动作序列的上下文学习方法。

Abstract: Cross-task generalization is a core challenge in open-world robotic manipulation, and the key lies in extracting transferable manipulation knowledge from seen tasks. Recent in-context learning approaches leverage seen task demonstrations to generate actions for unseen tasks without parameter updates. However, existing methods provide only low-level continuous action sequences as context, failing to capture composable skill knowledge and causing models to degenerate into superficial trajectory imitation. We propose Decompose and Recompose, a skill reasoning framework using atomic skill-action pairs as intermediate representations. Our approach decomposes seen demonstrations into interpretable skill--action alignments, enabling the model to recompose these skills for unseen tasks through compositional reasoning. Specifically, we construct a task-adaptive dynamic demonstration library via visual-semantic retrieval combined with skill sequences from a planning agent, complemented by a coverage-aware static library to fill missing skill patterns. Together, these yield skill-comprehensive demonstrations that explicitly elicit compositional reasoning for skill composition and execution ordering. Experiments on the AGNOSTOS benchmark and real-world environments validate our method's zero-shot cross-task generalization capability.

</details>


### [131] [LLM-Foraging: Large Language Models for Decentralized Swarm Robot Foraging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01461)
*Peihan Li,Joanna Gutierrez,Fabian Hernandez,Qi Lu,Lifeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出LLM-Foraging，一种用大语言模型替代遗传算法离线调参的分布式蜂群觅食控制器，通过三个结构化决策点将LLM注入CPFA状态机，无需重新训练即可跨不同团队规模、场地大小和资源分布迁移，性能优于GA调优的CPFA基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统蜂群觅食算法（如CPFA）依赖GA或强化学习进行离线参数优化，所得策略与特定团队规模、场地大小和资源分布紧密耦合，部署条件变化时性能下降且重新训练计算成本高。

Method: 在CPFA状态机中嵌入LLM作为战术决策器，在三个决策点（投放后、中心区域到达、搜索饥饿）使用本地可观测状态查询LLM，每个机器人独立运行LLM客户端，CPFA运动与感知栈执行所选动作。

Result: 在Gazebo中与TurtleBot3机器人进行36种配置（4-10台机器人、6x6至10x10米场地、三种资源分布）评估，LLM-Foraging在所有配置下资源采集量均优于GA调优的CPFA基线，且一致性更高。

Conclusion: LLM作为通用决策策略而非针对单一配置拟合的参数，使控制器在部署时无需训练即可跨配置迁移，解决了传统方法因单配置调优导致的迁移失效问题。

Abstract: Swarm foraging algorithms, such as the central-place foraging algorithm (CPFA), typically rely on offline parameter optimization using genetic algorithms (GA) or reinforcement learning, yielding policies tightly coupled to a specific combination of team size, arena size, and resource distribution. When deployment conditions change, performance degrades, and retraining is computationally expensive. We propose LLM-Foraging, a decentralized swarm controller that augments the CPFA state machine with a large language model (LLM) tactical decision-maker at three structured decision points, namely post-deposit, central-zone arrival, and search starvation. Each robot runs its own LLM client and queries it using only locally observable state, while the existing CPFA motion and sensing stack executes the selected action. Because the LLM serves as a general decision policy rather than parameters fitted to a single configuration, the controller is training-free at deployment and transfers across configurations without re-optimization. We evaluate LLM-Foraging in Gazebo with TurtleBot3 robots across 36 configurations spanning team sizes of 4 to 10 robots, arena sizes from 6x6 to 10x10 meters, and three resource distributions (clustered, powerlaw, random). LLM-Foraging collects more resources than the GA-tuned CPFA baseline across the evaluated configurations and is more consistent, a property that the GA's single-configuration tuning does not transfer.

</details>


### [132] [Action Agent: Agentic Video Generation Meets Flow-Constrained Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01477)
*Jeffrin Sam,Nguyen Khang,Yara Mahmoud,Miguel Altamirano Cabrera,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Action Agent，一个两阶段框架，通过LLM驱动的导航视频生成（阶段一）和Flow-Constrained Diffusion Transformer（FlowDiT）控制（阶段二），统一了多形态机器人导航。在50个导航任务中视频生成成功率从35%提升至86%，在真实Unitree G1人形机器人上实现64.7%任务完成率，支持人形机器人、无人机和轮式机器人三种形态。


<details>
