  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有导航方法大多针对单一机器人形态设计，缺乏跨形态的统一范式；同时，大语言模型与扩散模型在机器人导航中的结合仍存在生成质量不稳定、动作控制与视觉生成脱节的问题。

Method: 两阶段框架：阶段一用LLM作为编排模块，选择视频扩散模型、迭代验证提示、积累跨任务记忆，将单次35%成功率提升至86%；阶段二提出FlowDiT，融合DINOv2视觉特征、学习光流和CLIP语义嵌入，在动作空间进行去噪扩散生成连续速度命令。在RECON数据集预训练，在Isaac Sim中采集203个Unitree G1人形机器人episode微调。

Result: 单43M参数检查点在仿真中实现73.2%导航成功率，在真实Unitree G1人形机器人上达到64.7%任务完成率，运行频率40-47 Hz。在三种机器人形态（人形、无人机、轮式）上均验证有效。

Conclusion: 将轨迹想象（视频生成）与执行（动作控制）解耦，为语言引导的导航任务提供了可扩展且具有形态感知能力的范式，证明了该框架在多形态机器人上的通用性和实用性。

Abstract: We present Action Agent, a two-stage framework that unifies agentic navigation video generation with flow-constrained diffusion control for multi-embodiment robot navigation. In Stage I, a large language model (LLM) acts as an orchestration module that selects video diffusion models, refines prompts through iterative validation, and accumulates cross-task memory to synthesize physically plausible first-person navigation videos from language and image inputs. This increases video generation success from 35% (single-shot) to 86% across 50 navigation tasks. In Stage II, we introduce FlowDiT, a Flow-Constrained Diffusion Transformer that converts optimized goal videos and language instructions into continuous velocity commands using action-space denoising diffusion. FlowDiT integrates DINOv2 visual features, learned optical flow for ego-motion representation, and CLIP language embeddings for semantic stopping. We pretrain on the RECON outdoor navigation dataset and fine-tune on 203 Unitree G1 humanoid episodes collected in Isaac Sim to calibrate velocity dynamics. A single 43M-parameter checkpoint achieves 73.2% navigation success in simulation and 64.7% task completion on a real Unitree G1 in unseen indoor environments under open-loop execution, while operating at 40--47 Hz. We evaluate Action Agent across three embodiments: a Unitree G1 humanoid (real hardware), a drone, and a wheeled mobile robot (Isaac Sim), demonstrating that decoupling trajectory imagination from execution yields a scalable and embodiment-aware paradigm for language-guided navigation.

</details>


### [133] [Cut-In Gap Acceptance Toward Autonomous vs. Human-Driven Vehicles: Evidence from the Waymo Open Motion Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01485)
*Abdulaziz Alhuraish,Yuhang Wang,Hao Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 人类驾驶员在面对自动驾驶车辆（AV）时，会接受更短的纵向间隙进行切入（中位7.58米 vs 9.57米），且切入速度更高（51.7 vs 37.7公里/小时），揭示了AV特有的驾驶行为不对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证人类驾驶员在切入自动驾驶车辆前方时，是否会比切入人类驾驶车辆前方接受更短的纵向间隙，从而揭示AV对周围人类驾驶行为的系统性影响。

Method: 使用Waymo开放运动数据集（WOMD）中的25,906个真实高速公路场景（10Hz），通过八准则车道变换检测器提取706次HDV-to-AV和3,172次HDV-to-HDV切入事件，统计比较间隙长度、切入速度等指标。

Result: AV目标切入的中位间隙为7.58米，HDV目标为9.57米（减少1.99米，p=5.76×10⁻⁸，d=-0.224）；AV目标切入速度比HDV目标高37%（51.7 vs 37.7 km/h，d=0.502）；68.0%的AV目标切入发生在10米间隙以下，HDV目标为51.8%（χ²=60.5，p<10⁻¹³）。

Conclusion: 人类驾驶员对AV表现出系统性的间隙接受不对称行为，这具有安全相关性，需要在运动规划安全包络和交通仿真模型中进行针对AV的标定校准。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles (AVs) are widely known to follow conservative, rule-based motion policies that surrounding drivers can learn to anticipate. A direct consequence is that human drivers may accept shorter longitudinal gaps when cutting in front of an AV than when targeting another human-driven vehicle (HDV). We test this hypothesis using the Waymo Open Motion Dataset (WOMD), which provides 25,906 real-world highway scenarios at 10 hertz. An eight-criterion lane-change detector extracts 706 HDV-to-AV and 3,172 HDV-to-HDV cut-in events from the same traffic environment. The median accepted gap in front of the Waymo AV is 7.58 meters versus 9.57 meters for HDV targets, a 1.99 meter reduction that is statistically significant (p equals 5.76 times 10 to the negative eighth power, d equals negative 0.224) and persists under speed-matched resampling. Cut-in speeds toward the AV are 37 percent higher (51.7 versus 37.7 kilometers per hour, d equals 0.502), and 68.0 percent of AV-targeted cut-ins occur below the 10 meter gap boundary versus 51.8 percent of HDV-targeted events (chi-squared equals 60.5, p is less than 10 to the negative thirteenth power). These results reveal a systematic and safety-relevant asymmetry in human gap-acceptance behavior that warrants AV-specific calibration of both motion-planning safety envelopes and traffic simulation models.

</details>


### [134] [Distributed Algorithm with Emergent Area Partitioning and Base Station's Situation Awareness for Multi-Robot Patrolling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01501)
*Kazuho Kobayashi,Shohei Kobayashi,Seiya Ueno,Takehiro Higuchi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为LR-PT的新型多机器人巡逻算法，通过局部信息决策和自组织区域划分，在仿真中实现了比现有方法更高效的巡逻性能，并增强了基站操作员的情境意识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人巡逻需要提高巡逻效率和基站操作员的情境意识，以便操作员能够预测机器人行为、支持识别与决策，并在紧急情况下进行干预。

Method: LR-PT算法：机器人基于局部可用信息独立选择巡逻目标，将巡逻需求与向基站汇报任务进度的紧迫性整合为统一的效用函数；算法自主涌现区域划分，避免陷入局部最优，实现对整个任务区域的全面巡逻。

Result: 仿真结果表明LR-PT在频繁巡逻所有感兴趣位置方面优于现有方法；展现了针对通信限制和机器人故障的鲁棒性；实现了区域划分的自组织涌现。

Conclusion: LR-PT算法利用群体机器人的优势，有效应对实际运行挑战，是多机器人巡逻的优越方案。

Abstract: Patrolling with multiple robots offers efficient surveillance to detect and manage undesired situations. This necessitates improved patrol efficiency and operator situation awareness at base stations. Enhanced situation awareness enables operators to predict robots' behaviors, support recognition and decision-making, and execute emergency interventions. This study presents the Local Reactive and Partition (LR-PT) algorithm, a novel multi-robot patrolling approach. In simulations, LR-PT outperformed existing methods by ensuring frequent patrols of all locations of interest and enhancing the situation awareness of the base station. Robots independently select patrol targets based on locally available information, integrating patrol needs and the urgency of reporting mission progress to the base station into a unified utility function. This locality also contributes to robustness against communication constraints and robot failures, as demonstrated in this research. The algorithm further autonomously emerged the area partition, which can avoid falling into local optima and realize the comprehensive patrol over the whole mission area. The simulation results demonstrated the superior performance of LR-PT for multi-robot patrolling, utilizing the advantages of swarm robotics and addressing real-world operational challenges.

</details>


### [135] [Dynamics Distillation for Efficient and Transferable Control Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01516)
*Xunjiang Gu,Kashyap Chitta,Mahsa Golchoubian,Vladimir Suplin,Igor Gilitschenski*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Sim2Sim2Sim框架，通过将高保真车辆模拟器的动力学蒸馏为可并行学习模型，实现高效强化学习策略训练，并验证了策略从蒸馏环境到原始模拟器的可靠迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶模拟器要么物理真实性强但计算扩展性差，要么可扩展但物理真实性不足，缺乏兼具两种属性的训练环境。

Method: 将高保真模拟器的动力学知识蒸馏到高度可并行化的学习动力学模型中，在该蒸馏环境中训练控制策略，再部署回原始高保真模拟器。

Result: 在蒸馏环境中进行策略优化更高效，且策略能够可靠迁移回高保真模拟器；预测精度不能完全表征学习动力学模型作为RL训练环境的适用性，还应评估其催生的策略质量。

Conclusion: 蒸馏动力学模型可作为兼顾物理真实性与计算可扩展性的训练环境，策略质量是评估该类模型的重要指标。

Abstract: Robust control policy learning for autonomous driving requires training environments to be both physically realistic and computationally scalable, properties that existing simulators provide only in isolation. We introduce Sim2Sim2Sim, a framework that bridges high-fidelity vehicle simulation and scalable reinforcement learning by distilling simulator dynamics into a highly parallelizable learned dynamics model. By training control policies purely within this distilled environment and deploying them back into the high-fidelity source simulator, we demonstrate more efficient policy optimization and reliable transfer under challenging dynamics. We further show that predictive accuracy alone does not fully characterize a learned dynamics model's suitability as a reinforcement learning training environment, which should also be assessed by the quality of the policies it enables.

</details>


### [136] [VOFA: Visual Object Goal Pushing with Force-Adaptive Control for Humanoids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01518)
*Zichao Hu,Zifan Xu,Dongsik Chang,He Yin,Linh Tran,Roberto Martín-Martín,Peter Stone,Jingyu Qiao,Joydeep Biswas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出VOFA系统，一种基于视觉目标条件的人形机器人全身推物操作框架，通过双层分层架构（高层视觉运动策略+低层力自适应全身控制器），使Booster T1人形机器人能够在仅依靠自身感知的情况下，将未知物理属性的重物（最重17kg，超过自身一半体重）推至任意目标位置，模拟实验成功率超90%，真实实验成功率超80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在仓库物料搬运等任务中需要具备利用本体自我感知推动大型物体至任意目标构型的能力，但现有方法要么依赖特权物体状态信息而缺乏自我感知，要么对不同目标构型和物体物理属性的变化缺乏鲁棒性。本文旨在解决在未知物体质量、地面摩擦、噪声感知和执行误差条件下的实时闭环推物操作问题。

Method: VOFA采用双层分层架构：1) 高层视觉运动策略（visuomotor policy），处理噪声自我观测并生成目标条件化命令，在多种物体-目标构型下闭环运行；2) 低层力自适应全身控制器（force-adaptive whole-body controller），提供对物体物理属性变化的鲁棒性。整个系统在Booster T1人形机器人上进行仿真和真实实验评估。

Result: 仿真实验成功率超90%，真实实验成功率超80%。VOFA能够成功推动重达17kg的物体，超过Booster T1自身重量的一半。

Conclusion: VOFA系统通过视觉目标条件与力自适应控制相结合的分层架构，有效提升了人形机器人在未知物理条件下推动重物的鲁棒性和泛化能力，具备实际应用潜力。

Abstract: The ability to push large objects in a goal-directed manner using onboard egocentric perception is an essential skill for humanoid robots to perform complex tasks such as material handling in warehouses. To robustly manipulate heavy objects to arbitrary goal configurations, the robot must cope with unknown object mass and ground friction, noisy onboard perception, and actuation errors; all in a real-time feedback loop. Existing solutions either rely on privileged object-state information without onboard perception or lack robustness to variations in goal configurations and object physical properties. In this work, we present VOFA, a visual goal-conditioned humanoid loco-manipulation system capable of pushing objects with unknown physical properties to arbitrary goal positions. VOFA consists of a two-level hierarchical architecture with a high-level visuomotor policy and a low-level force-adaptive whole-body controller. The high-level policy processes noisy onboard observations and generates goal-conditioned commands to operate in closed loop across diverse object-goal configurations, while the low-level whole-body controller provides robustness to variations in object physical properties. VOFA is extensively evaluated in both simulation and real-world experiments on the Booster T1 humanoid robot. Our results demonstrate strong performance, achieving over 90% success in simulation and over 80% success in real-world trials. Moreover, VOFA successfully pushes objects weighing up to 17kg, exceeding half of the Booster T1's body weight.

</details>


### [137] [Good in Bad (GiB): Sifting Through End-user Demonstrations for Learning a Better Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01529)
*Noushad Sojib,Momotaz Begum*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出GiB算法，能够自动识别并丢弃人类演示中的错误子任务，同时保留高质量子任务，从而在混合质量演示数据中学习更鲁棒的机器人策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模仿学习算法假设演示数据高质量，但非专家用户收集的数据常包含无意错误。直接学习会导致不安全策略，而丢弃整段演示则会浪费有价值的数据，尤其在低数据场景下。

Method: GiB首先训练自监督模型学习潜在特征，并分配二值权重标记每段演示为好或差；然后建模高质量片段的潜在特征分布，利用马氏距离检测和评估低质量子任务。

Result: 在Franka机器人的模拟和真实世界多步任务中验证，GiB能够从混合质量的人类演示中提升策略学习效果。

Conclusion: GiB通过自动识别和过滤低质量子任务，有效利用非专家演示数据中的高质量部分，提升模仿学习策略的鲁棒性和安全性。

Abstract: Imitation learning offers a promising framework for enabling robots to acquire diverse skills from human users. However, most imitation learning algorithms assume access to high-quality demonstrations an unrealistic expectation when collecting data from non-expert users, whose demonstrations often contain inadvertent errors. Naively learning from such demonstrations can result in unsafe policy behavior, while discarding entire demonstrations due to occasional mistakes wastes valuable data, especially in low-data settings. In this work, we introduce GiB (Good-in-Bad), an algorithm that automatically identifies and discards erroneous subtasks within demonstrations while preserving high-quality subtasks. The filtered data can then be used by any policy learning algorithm to train more robust policies. GiB first trains a self-supervised model to learn latent features and assigns binary weights to label each demonstration as good or bad. It then models the latent feature distribution of high-quality segments and uses the Mahalanobis distance to detect and evaluate poor-quality subtasks. We validate GiB on the Franka robot in both simulated and real-world multi-step tasks, demonstrating improved policy performance when learning from mixed-quality human demonstrations.

</details>


### [138] [An Efficient Metric for Data Quality Measurement in Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01544)
*Noushad Sojib,Momotaz Begum*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于功率谱密度（PSD）的演示数据排序指标，用于自动筛选高质量演示数据以微调模仿学习策略，无需策略学习、环境交互或专家标注，实验表明PSD筛选后的数据能显著提升任务成功率和执行平滑度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模仿学习策略在部署时面临分布外场景挑战，微调预训练策略需要终端用户演示数据，但用户演示常存在过多修正动作、振荡和突变等质量问题。现有自动筛选方法需要策略回滚，计算成本高且不适用于实际部署场景。

Method: 提出基于功率谱密度（PSD）的演示轨迹排序指标，PSD越低代表轨迹越平滑、质量越高。该方法无需策略学习、环境交互或专家标注，直接对轨迹频域特征进行分析。

Result: 在两个基准模仿学习数据集（包含专家和普通用户演示）以及养老院老年人用户研究中，PSD筛选后的数据微调策略相比未筛选基线和两种竞争排序方法，实现了更高的任务成功率和更平滑的执行轨迹。

Conclusion: PSD-based演示排序是一种快速、高效、全自动的数据筛选方法，适合实际部署场景中的可扩展数据整理，能有效提升模仿学习策略在分布外场景下的性能。

Abstract: Imitation learning (IL) has seen remarkable progress, yet field deployment of IL-powered robots remains hindered by the challenge of out-of-distribution (OOD) scenarios. Fine-tuning pre-trained policies with end-user demonstrations collected in deployment environments is a promising strategy to address this challenge. However, end-user demonstrations are frequently of poor quality, characterized by excessive corrective motions, oscillations, and abrupt adjustments that degrade both learned and fine-tuned policy performance. Existing automated approaches for curating demonstration data require policy rollouts in the environment, making them computationally expensive and impractical for real-world deployment. In this paper, we propose a fast, efficient, and fully automated demonstration ranking metric based on the power spectral density (PSD) of demonstration trajectories. The PSD metric requires no policy learning, environment interaction, or expert labeling, making it well-suited for scalable, in-the-field data curation. Lower PSD values correspond to smoother, higher-quality demonstrations, while higher PSD values indicate erratic, artifact-laden trajectories. We evaluate the proposed metric on two benchmark imitation learning datasets comprising expert and lay-user demonstrations, and through a user study with older adults at a retirement facility, where collected demonstrations are used to fine-tune $\pi0.5$ \cite{intelligence2025pi_} for a daily living task. Results demonstrate that PSD-curated data yields policies with higher task success rates and smoother execution trajectories compared to uncurated baselines and two competitive data-ranking methods.

</details>


### [139] [Hydra-DP3: Frequency-Aware Right-Sizing of 3D Diffusion Policies for Visuomotor Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01581)
*Jinhao Zhang,Zhexuan Zhou,Huizhe Li,Yichen Lai,Wenlong Xia,Haoming Song,Youmin Gong,Jie Mei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文从频域角度重新审视扩散策略，发现机器人动作轨迹能量集中在低频DCT模式，最优去噪器误差受限于低频子空间维度和高频残差能量，因此提出仅需两步DDIM推理的轻量级3D扩散策略Hydra-DP3（HDP3），以不到先前1%的参数实现SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的视觉运动策略继承了图像生成样式的解码器和多步采样，计算开销大；作者从频域角度发现机器人动作轨迹具有高度平滑性，暗示当前扩散策略在去噪模型设计上存在过度复杂化问题。

Method: 从频域角度分析动作轨迹的DCT能量分布，证明最优去噪器的误差界由低频子空间维度和高频残差能量决定，表明去噪误差在很少反向步后即饱和；据此提出HDP3，采用轻量级Diffusion Mixer解码器，支持两步DDIM采样。

Result: 在RoboTwin2.0、Adroit、MetaWorld及真实世界任务中，HDP3以不到先前3D扩散策略1%的参数和更低的推理延迟，实现了SOTA性能。

Conclusion: 机器人动作去噪本质上比图像生成简单得多，仅需两步去噪即可达到饱和性能；超轻量级扩散策略在保持高性能的同时大幅降低参数和延迟，为端到端机器人学习提供了高效方案。

Abstract: Diffusion-based visuomotor policies perform well in robotic manipulation, yet current methods still inherit image-generation-style decoders and multi-step sampling. We revisit this design from a frequency-domain perspective. Robot action trajectories are highly smooth, with most energy concentrated in a few low-frequency discrete cosine transform modes. Under this structure, we show that the error of the optimal denoiser is bounded by the low-frequency subspace dimension and residual high-frequency energy, implying that denoising error saturates after very few reverse steps. This further suggests that action denoising requires a much simpler denoising model than image generation. Motivated by this insight, we propose Hydra-DP3(HDP3), a pocket-scale 3D diffusion policy with a lightweight Diffusion Mixer decoder that supports two-step DDIM inference. Our synthetic experiments validate the theory and support the sufficiency of two-step denoising. Futhermore, across RoboTwin2.0, Adroit, MetaWorld, and real-world tasks, HDP3 achieves state-of-the-art performance with fewer than 1% of the parameters of prior 3D diffusion-based policies and substantially lower inference latency.

</details>


### [140] [Lateral String Stability for Vehicle Platoons: Formulation, Definition, and Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01731)
*Sixu Li,Swaroop Darbha,Yang Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出基于弧长（欧拉）视角的横向队列稳定性框架，定义L2横向队列稳定性，并对比仅依赖车载感知的反馈-前馈策略与利用V2V通信的“向先驱学习”策略。结果表明：仅靠车载感知无法保证路径跟踪误差衰减（存在本质安全局限），而V2V通信可实现真正的误差衰减，并揭示了非零反馈对特定测量的结构必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纵向队列稳定性已得到广泛研究，但横向队列稳定性（控制路径跟踪误差沿队列传播）尚不充分。随着自动驾驶车辆依赖车载感知和无地图导航，传感器遮挡和紧密编队加剧了安全风险，亟需建立聚焦于安全关键的路径相对跟踪误差的横向队列稳定性分析框架。

Method: 1）采用弧长（欧拉）视角取代传统拉格朗日视角，明确路径上同一点处跟踪误差在前后车之间的传播关系；2）提出L2横向队列稳定性定义；3）设计两种控制策略——仅依赖车载感知的反馈-前馈策略和利用V2V通信的“向先驱学习”策略；4）对两种策略分别从跟踪误差向量和横向（侧向）误差两个指标进行稳定性分析。

Result: 1）仅依靠车载感知无法保证路径跟踪误差的衰减，存在本质安全局限；2）V2V通信能够实现真正的误差衰减；3）识别出结构性的控制器要求——对特定测量的非零反馈是保证稳定性的必要条件。

Conclusion: 横向队列稳定性研究中，弧长（欧拉）视角有效揭示了路径跟踪误差的传播机制；V2V通信是实现横向队列稳定性、保障编队安全的关键技术手段；控制器设计必须满足特定结构要求。

Abstract: Platooning of connected and automated vehicles provides significant benefits in terms of energy efficiency, traffic throughput, and, most critically, safety. These safety benefits depend on string stability, which dictates how disturbances propagate along a vehicle string. Although longitudinal string stability has been extensively examined, lateral string stability, which governs the propagation of path-tracking errors that can lead to unsafe deviations from the desired path, remains underexplored. Its importance is growing as autonomous vehicles increasingly depend on onboard sensing and map-free navigation, where sensor occlusions and tight formations amplify safety risks. This paper presents a framework for lateral string stability that focuses directly on safety-critical, path-relative tracking errors and enables consistent comparison across vehicles that follow the same planned path. The key element of the framework is an arc-length (Eulerian) viewpoint, a departure from traditional analyses, that clarifies how tracking errors at a given point on the path propagate from one vehicle to the next. Building on this foundation, we propose the definition of L2 lateral string stability along with two control strategies: a feedback-feedforward strategy that relies solely on onboard sensing, and a novel learn-from-predecessor strategy that makes use of vehicle-to-vehicle communication. Both strategies are analyzed for lateral string stability with respect to two error measures: tracking error vector and lateral (cross-track) error. Our results show that onboard sensing alone cannot guarantee attenuation of path-tracking errors, imposing a fundamental safety limitation, while V2V communication enables true error attenuation. The analysis further identifies structural controller requirements, showing that nonzero feedback on specific measurements is essential for guaranteeing stability.

</details>


### [141] [Anticipation-VLA: Solving Long-Horizon Embodied Tasks via Anticipation-based Subgoal Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01772)
*Zhilong Zhang,Wenyu Luo,Haonan Wang,Yifei Sheng,Yidi Wang,Hanyuan Guo,Haoxiang Ren,Xinghao Du,Yuhan Che,Tongtong Cao,Lei Yuan,Yang Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Anticipation-VLA，一种通过自适应递归生成子目标来增强VLA模型处理长时域任务能力的层次化框架，在仿真和真实机器人任务中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在长时域任务中因误差累积表现不佳，而先前方法使用固定粒度的子任务分解无法适应执行状态的动态变化，限制了鲁棒性。

Method: 提出Anticipation Model自适应递归生成未来子目标，并构建Anticipation-VLA层次化框架：高层使用统一多模态模型(UMM)微调生成子目标，底层使用目标条件VLA策略执行动作。

Result: 在仿真和真实机器人任务中，Anticipation-VLA表现出有效性，验证了自适应递归子目标生成对鲁棒策略执行的重要性。

Conclusion: 自适应递归的子目标生成机制能够有效应对长时域任务中的动态变化，提升VLA模型的规划可靠性和执行鲁棒性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have emerged as a powerful paradigm for embodied intelligence, enabling robots to perform tasks based on natural language instructions and current visual input. However, existing VLA models struggle with long-horizon tasks due to compounding errors. Prior methods decompose tasks into subtasks of fixed granularity, which cannot adapt to the varying complexity of execution states, limiting their robustness in long-horizon tasks. To overcome this, we introduce Anticipation Model, which adaptively and recursively generates future subgoals. This model continuously adapts as the task unfolds, adjusting future subgoals in response to evolving dynamics, facilitating more reliable planning paths. Building on this concept, we propose Anticipation-VLA, a hierarchical VLA model that leverages the anticipation model to generate actionable subgoals that guide VLA policy execution. We implement Anticipation-VLA with finetuning a Unified Multimodal Model (UMM) for high-level subgoal generation and a goal-conditioned VLA policy for low-level action execution. Experiments in both simulated and real-world robotic tasks demonstrate the effectiveness of Anticipation-VLA, highlighting the importance of adaptive and recursive subgoal generation for robust policy execution.

</details>


### [142] [On the Characterization and Limits of 4D Radar for Aided Inertial Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01773)
*Morten Nissov,Kostas Alexis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对FMCW雷达在辅助惯性导航中测量噪声复杂的问题，通过推导噪声模型并设计基于因子图的估计器，实现了在多种实际环境中的鲁棒高精度定位，性能超越雷达标准工作范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FMCW雷达在恶劣环境下具有鲁棒性优势，但其复杂、噪声大的测量数据导致可靠估计困难，阻碍了其广泛应用。

Method: 从第一性原理出发分析雷达信号处理流程推导噪声模型，基于因子图设计估计器，并采用一阶近似处理测量噪声传播。

Result: 仿真验证了不同噪声源的重要性、一阶近似的有效性及协方差的状态依赖性；实际实验在不同环境和飞行剖面中展示了方法的鲁棒性和准确性，包括超出雷达标准工作范围的场景。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法在多种实际条件下具有优越的鲁棒性和精度，且实验验证了仿真中关于估计器配置与运行条件之间关系的洞察。

Abstract: Frequency Modulated Continuous Wave (FMCW) radar is a promising sensor for aided inertial navigation, due to its robustness in environments that challenge traditional alternatives, such as LiDAR and vision. However, its widespread adoption is hindered by complex, noisy measurements, which make reliable estimation difficult. This manuscript addresses these challenges by analyzing the fundamental measurement relations of FMCW radar sensing and developing a reliable estimator. Noise models are derived by applying first principles to the underlying signal processing of a typical radar sensor. These models guide the design of a factor graph-based estimator, utilizing a first-order approximation for the measurement noise propagation. The approach is first examined through simulation, evaluating the significance of different noise sources, the validity of the first-order approximation, and the state-dependent nature of the covariance expressions. Extensive experiments demonstrate the superior robustness and accuracy of the proposed method across diverse field environments and flight profiles, including beyond the radar's standard operating range. Furthermore, the experiments confirm the insights from the simulation regarding the behavior and performance of different estimator configurations relative to their operating conditions. The evaluation data and estimator implementation are made available at https://github.com/ntnu-arl/rig.

</details>


### [143] [Optimizing Trajectory-Trees in Belief Space: An Application from Model Predictive Control to Task and Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01860)
*Camille Phiquepal,Marc Toussaint*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出在部分可观测机器人规划问题中使用树状轨迹（轨迹树）替代传统序列轨迹，分别针对模型预测控制（MPC）和任务与运动规划（TAMP）开发了PO-MPC和PO-LGP方法，通过分支结构捕获观测依赖的多种可能情景，并利用分布式增广拉格朗日算法实现实时优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统序列轨迹仅建模单一的系统前向演化，无法有效处理部分可观测环境下依赖观测信息的多分支决策问题，导致控制成本较高且规划质量受限。

Method: 1) 提出PO-MPC，在信念空间中优化单分支树状轨迹，并设计分布式增广拉格朗日（D-AuLa）算法利用问题可分解性并行加速优化；2) 提出PO-LGP，扩展逻辑几何规划框架至部分可观测问题，在任务层推理决策树，在运动规划层使用轨迹树，并通过探索性策略作为宏动作扩展至大规模问题。

Result: 在自动驾驶的线性和非线性MPC实验中，树状轨迹相比序列轨迹降低了控制成本；PO-LGP在小规模信念状态问题上有效，并通过探索性策略宏动作扩展到更大规模问题。

Conclusion: 在部分可观测规划问题中，采用树状轨迹（轨迹树）优于传统序列轨迹，能够通过多分支结构更好地应对观测不确定性，且D-AuLa算法可满足实时性要求。

Abstract: This paper explores the benefits of computing arborescent trajectories (trajectory-trees) instead of commonly used sequential trajectories for partially observable robotic planning problems. In such environments, a robot infers knowledge from observations, and the optimal course of action depends on these observations. \revise{Trajectory-trees, optimized in belief space, naturally capture this dependency by branching where the belief state is expected to evolve into multiple distinct scenarios, such as upon receiving an observation. Unlike sequential trajectories, which model a single forward evolution of the system, trajectory-trees capture multiple possible contingencies.}
  First, we focus on Model Predictive Control (MPC) and demonstrate the benefits of planning tree-like trajectories. We formulate the control problem as the optimization of a tree with a single branching (PO-MPC). This improves performance by reducing control costs through more informed planning. To satisfy the real-time constraints of MPC, we develop an optimization algorithm called Distributed Augmented Lagrangian (D-AuLa), which leverages the decomposability of the PO-MPC formulation to parallelize and accelerate the optimization. We apply the method to both linear and non-linear MPC problems using autonomous driving examples.
  Second, we address Task And Motion Planning (TAMP), and introduce a planner (PO-LGP) reasoning on decision trees at task level, and trajectory-trees at motion-planning level. This approach builds upon the Logic-Geometric-Programming Framework (LGP) and extends it to partially observable problems. The experiments show the method's applicability to problems with a small belief state size, and scales to larger problems by optimizing explorative policies, which are used as macro-actions in an overarching task plan.

</details>


### [144] [Optimized and kinematically feasible multi-agent motion planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01996)
*Anja Hellander,Kristoffer Bergman,Daniel Axehill*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种两步法解决多智能体运动规划问题：先用CBS或PBS等算法获取初始可行解，再通过多相最优控制问题对解进行优化改进；同时提出了在时间约束下优化生成运动原语的方法。在拖拉机-挂车系统上进行了评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体运动规划（MAMP）是自主多智能体系统的重要问题，现有方法能找到可行解但往往不是最优解，需要一种既能保证运动学可行性又能优化解质量的方法。

Method: 两步法：第一步使用CBS或PBS等状态-of-the-art方法获取初始可行解；第二步将初始解作为热启动，求解多相最优控制问题（OCP）进行改进。同时提出了一种在原始持续时间均为相同采样时间倍数的约束下优化生成运动原语的方法。扩展了SIPP-IP算法以处理更通用的代价函数和更大尺寸的智能体。

Result: 在拖拉机-挂车系统上：基于格子的规划器由于保守碰撞检测较少而优于扩展的SIPP-IP算法；CBS相比PBS在障碍物环境中成功率更高且平均运行时间更低，但两种规划器在改进步骤后能获得质量相似的解。

Conclusion: 两步法能有效优化MAMP问题的解质量，CBS在拖拉机-挂车系统中表现优于PBS，格子规划器比基于区间投影的方法更适合该系统的运动学约束。

Abstract: Multi-agent motion planning (MAMP) is an important problem for autonomous systems with multiple agents. In this work we propose a two-step method for finding optimized and kinematically feasible solutions to MAMP problems. The first step finds an initial feasible solution using state-of-the-art methods such as conflict-based search (CBS) or priority-based search (PBS), and the second step is an improvement step which improves the solution by solving a multi-phase optimal control problem (OCP) where the initial solution is used to warm-start the solver. We also propose a method for generating motion primitives in an optimized way under the constraint that the primitive durations are all multiples of the same sample time.
  We evaluate our proposed framework on a MAMP problem for tractor-trailer systems. We extend the safe interval path planning with interval projections (SIPP-IP) algorithm so it can handle more general cost functions and larger agents, but our results show that for the tractor-trailer system a simple lattice-based planner performs better due to less conservative collision checks. Our experiments also indicate that CBS performs better than PBS for this system as it achieves a higher success rate in environments with obstacles and had a lower average runtime, although both planners achieve solutions of similar quality after the improvement step.

</details>


### [145] [Phone2Act: A Low-Cost, Hardware-Agnostic Teleoperation System for Scalable VLA Data Collection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01948)
*Om Mandhane,Bipin Yadav,Sangeetha Prasanna Ram,Gopalakrishnan Narayanan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Phone2Act提出了一种低成本、硬件无关的遥操作框架，利用智能手机和Google ARCore实现6-DoF机器人控制，通过模块化ROS 2架构和通用记录器支持多种机器人平台，并原生导出LeRobot格式数据用于VLA模型微调。在Dobot CR5上完成130个episodes的GR00T-N1.5微调，多阶段拾取任务成功率达90%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型训练所需的高质量操作数据采集成本高昂，遥操作框架依赖专用硬件或与特定机器人平台紧密耦合，限制了研究团队的数据获取能力。

Method: 将智能手机通过Google ARCore转换为6-DoF控制器；基于模块化ROS 2架构设计可互换桥接节点，解耦控制逻辑与硬件；通用记录器同步多相机RGB流与机器人状态反馈，原生导出LeRobot格式数据集。

Result: 在Dobot CR5平台上采集130个episodes，微调GR00T-N1.5模型，在真实世界多阶段拾取任务中达到90%的成功率。

Conclusion: Phone2Act证明了使用低成本、平台无关的遥操作框架即可高效采集VLA训练数据并取得优异性能，降低了VLA研究的数据采集门槛。

Abstract: Collecting diverse, high-quality manipulation data for Vision-Language-Action (VLA) model training remains prohibitively expensive for many research groups, as existing teleoperation frameworks rely on specialized hardware or are tightly coupled to specific robot platforms. We present Phone2Act, a low-cost, hardware-agnostic teleoperation framework that transforms a commodity smartphone into a 6-DoF robot controller via Google ARCore. Built on a modular ROS 2 architecture, Phone2Act decouples control logic from hardware specifics through interchangeable bridge nodes, supporting platforms from industrial cobots to low-cost bimanual arms without code modification. A Universal Recorder synchronizes multi-camera RGB streams with robot state feedback and exports demonstrations natively in the LeRobot dataset format, eliminating post-processing and enabling immediate VLA fine-tuning. We validate the framework by fine-tuning GR00T-N1.5 on 130 collected episodes, achieving a 90% success rate on a real-world multi-stage pick-and-place task deployed on a physical Dobot CR5.

</details>


### [146] [Sampling-Based Control via Entropy-Regularized Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02147)
*Vincent Pacelli,Akash Ratheesh,Evangelos A. Theodorou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出OT-MPC算法，通过熵正则化最优传输公式替代传统信息论目标，利用Sinkhorn算法实现无梯度、闭式更新的采样预测控制，在导航、操作和 locomotion 任务中提高了成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于采样的MPC方法（如MPPI和CEM）源自信息论目标，忽略控制问题的几何结构，在复杂成本景观中会出现模式平均等病理行为。

Method: 熵正则化最优传输公式：计算候选控制序列与低成本候选之间的最优耦合，通过Sinkhorn算法推导闭式、无梯度更新，协调跨样本更新以维持解空间覆盖。

Result: 在导航、操作和 locomotion 任务中，成功率优于现有方法（MPPI、CEM等）。

Conclusion: OT-MPC通过最优传输引入几何感知能力，克服了传统信息论方法的模式平均问题，实现了实时性能。

Abstract: Sampling-based model predictive control methods like MPPI and CEM are essential for real-time control of nonlinear robotic systems, particularly where discontinuous dynamics preclude gradient-based optimization. However, these methods derive from information-theoretic objectives that are agnostic to the geometry of the control problem, leading to pathological behaviors such as mode-averaging when the cost landscape is complex. We present OT-MPC, a sampling-based algorithm that overcomes these limitations through an entropy-regularized optimal transport formulation. By computing an optimal coupling between candidate control sequences and low-cost proposals, OT-MPC refines candidates toward nearby promising samples while coordinating updates across the ensemble to maintain coverage of the solution space. We derive closed-form, gradient-free updates via the Sinkhorn algorithm, enabling real-time performance. Experiments on navigation, manipulation, and locomotion tasks demonstrate improved success rates over existing methods.

</details>


### [147] [Sonar-GPS Fusion for Seabed Mapping in Turbid Shallow Waters with an Autonomous Surface Vehicle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01949)
*Yisheng Zhang,Michael Xu,Alan Williams,Matthew Gray,Nare Karapetyan,Miao Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合Fourier-Mellin变换与扩展卡尔曼滤波的漂移鲁棒海底测绘框架，在结构化牡蛎养殖场试验中将RMSE漂移降低了9.5%，实现了亚米级重建精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在浑浊浅海环境中（如贝类养殖场），传统光学方法失效，而现有基于声纳的测绘系统在长轨迹上存在定位分辨率低和累积漂移的问题，难以实现高分辨率海底测绘。

Method: 采用Fourier-Mellin变换（FMT）进行局部前视声纳帧对齐，结合扩展卡尔曼滤波（EKF）融合GPS、IMU和罗盘数据进行全局轨迹优化，并使用基于方差的图像融合策略减少重叠区域视觉伪影。

Result: 在结构化牡蛎养殖场实地试验中，相比纯FMT基线，RMSE漂移降低了9.5%，实现了亚米级重建精度并保留了高分辨率纹理。

Conclusion: 所提框架有效抑制了长轨迹下的累积漂移，能够实现高精度海底地图构建，且保留的高分辨率纹理可用于牡蛎存量估算等实际应用。

Abstract: Accurate seabed mapping is essential for habitat monitoring and infrastructure inspection. In turbid, shallow coastal waters, such as shellfish aquaculture farms, the effectiveness of traditional optical methods is limited. Autonomous surface vehicles (ASVs) equipped with forward-looking sonar (FLS) offer a promising alternative. However, existing sonar-based systems face challenges in achieving fine resolution mapping over long trajectories due to low-resolution positioning measurements and accumulated drift over long trajectories. In this paper, we present a drift-resilient seabed mapping framework that integrates local FLS frame alignment using the Fourier-Mellin transform (FMT) with global trajectory optimization based on an extended Kalman filter (EKF) that fuses global positioning system (GPS), inertial measurement unit (IMU), and compass data. A variance-based image blending strategy is used to further reduce visual artifacts in overlapping regions. Field trials on a structured oyster farm site show that our framework helps reduce drift in RMSE by 9.5% relative to the FMT-only baseline. This framework also enables sub-meter reconstruction accuracy and preservation of high-resolution textures needed for oyster inventory estimation within the mapped areas.

</details>


### [148] [Neural Backward Reach-Avoid Tubes with MPC Supervision for High-Dimensional Systems: An Application to Safe Spacecraft Docking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02021)
*Santiago Thorup,Luca Castelletto,Zeyuan Feng,Somil Bansal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于学习的后向可达管（BRAT）框架，通过将Hamilton-Jacobi可达性分析与MPC监督相结合，解决高维自主航天器对接中的避碰与目标可达性问题，在6D和13D系统中均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主航天器对接需要在耦合的高维平动-转动动力学下同时保证避碰和目标可达性。HJ可达性可提供形式化的避碰-可达保证，但经典求解器限于低维系统；学习方法虽开始扩展HJ分析，但在对接这类目标集与失效集紧密耦合的避碰-可达场景中表现不佳。

Method: 提出学习型BRAT框架：离线阶段，利用基于PDE的损失结合课程式MPC监督训练HJ值函数的神经逼近，MPC监督提供有价值的目标值并在纯PDE方法失败区域稳定训练；在线阶段，通过两种实时控制器部署学习到的值函数——值梯度驱动控制器和值函数增强的终端MPC控制器。

Result: 在6D平面对接问题上以网格地面真值验证，并扩展到完整的13D系统。在两个设置中，所提方法在成功率和计算效率上均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 紧密整合HJ结构与MPC监督的学习型BRAT框架能有效解决高维对接问题中的避碰-可达挑战，同时保证形式化安全性与实时计算效率。

Abstract: Autonomous spacecraft docking requires control policies that simultaneously ensure collision avoidance and target reachability under coupled, high-dimensional translational-rotational dynamics. Hamilton-Jacobi (HJ) reachability provides formal reach-avoid guarantees, but classical solvers are limited to low-dimensional systems. Learning-based approaches have begun to scale HJ analysis, yet they struggle in reach-avoid settings, especially where goal and failure sets are tightly coupled, as in docking. We propose a learning-based Backward Reach-Avoid Tube (BRAT) framework that addresses this challenge by tightly integrating HJ structure with MPC-based supervision. In the offline phase, we train a neural approximation of the HJ value function using PDE-based losses augmented with curriculum-driven MPC supervision, which provides informative value targets and stabilizes training in regions where purely PDE-based methods fail. In the online phase, the learned value function is deployed through two real-time controllers: (i) a value gradient-driven controller, and (ii) a value-function-augmented terminal MPC that explicitly enforces reachability at the horizon. We evaluate the proposed method on a 6D planar docking problem against grid-based ground truth and then scale to the full 13D system. Across both settings, our approach outperforms existing methods in success rate and computational efficiency.

</details>


### [149] [VILAS: A VLA-Integrated Low-cost Architecture with Soft Grasping for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02037)
*Zijian An,Hadi Khezam,Bill Cai,Ran Yang,Shijie Geng,Yiming Feng,Yue,Zheng,Lifeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VILAS是一个低成本模块化机器人操作平台，集成了Fairino FR5机械臂、Jodell RG52-50夹爪和双摄像头感知模块，通过ZMQ通信架构统一了遥操作、数据收集和策略部署。设计了基于kirigami的软体夹爪扩展用于易碎物体操作。在葡萄抓取任务上评估了pi_0、pi_0.5和GR00T N1.6三种VLA模型，验证了低成本硬件上训练和部署操作策略的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA策略学习通常依赖昂贵、专用的硬件平台，限制了研究的可及性和可复现性。本研究旨在构建一个完全低成本、模块化的机器人操作平台，使VLA策略能够在经济实惠的硬件上进行端到端学习与部署。

Method: 1) 集成Fairino FR5协作臂、Jodell RG52-50电夹爪、双摄像头感知模块的硬件系统；2) 基于ZMQ的通信架构统一遥操作、数据采集和策略部署；3) 基于kirigami的软体夹爪扩展实现无显式力传感器的易碎物体安全操作；4) 在VILAS平台上部署并微调pi_0、pi_0.5、GR00T N1.6三种VLA模型。

Result: 在葡萄抓取任务上验证了系统有效性，证明低成本模块化硬件上可以成功训练和部署有能力的操作策略，并提供了当前VLA模型在真实场景中部署特性的实用见解。

Conclusion: 低成本的模块化硬件平台能够有效支持VLA策略的学习与部署，为降低机器人学习研究的硬件门槛提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We present VILAS, a fully low-cost, modular robotic manipulation platform designed to support end-to-end vision-language-action (VLA) policy learning and deployment on accessible hardware. The system integrates a Fairino FR5 collaborative arm, a Jodell RG52-50 electric gripper, and a dual-camera perception module, unified through a ZMQ-based communication architecture that seamlessly coordinates teleoperation, data collection, and policy deployment within a single framework. To enable safe manipulation of fragile objects without relying on explicit force sensing, we design a kirigami-based soft compliant gripper extension that induces predictable deformation under compressive loading, providing gentle and repeatable contact with delicate targets. We deploy and evaluate three state-of-the-art VLA models on the VILAS platform: pi_0, pi_0.5, and GR00T N1.6. All models are fine-tuned from publicly released pretrained checkpoints using an identical demonstration dataset collected via our teleoperation pipeline. Experiments on a grape grasping task validate the effectiveness of the proposed system, confirming that capable manipulation policies can be successfully trained and deployed on low-cost modular hardware. Our results further provide practical insights into the deployment characteristics of current VLA models in real-world settings.

</details>


### [150] [Robotic Desk Organization: A Multi-Primitive Approach to Manipulating Heterogeneous Objects via Environmental Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02135)
*Yi Dong. Yangjun Liu,Jinjun Duan,Yang Li,Zhendong Dai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向任务的服务机器人桌面整理框架，通过环境辅助操作原语（接触抓取、边缘推抓、杠杆抓取）和任务规划器，实现对桌面刚性及可变形物体的多目标整理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 服务机器人在桌面整理中面临物体异构（刚性与可变形）以及操作目标多样化（如收集、堆叠）的挑战，现有方法缺乏统一的处理框架。

Method: 1) 感知流水线：扩充数据集以覆盖不常见的桌面物品，实现基于几何的位姿和关键点估计，并检测环境约束（如桌边）。2) 三种环境辅助操作原语：接触抓取（小物体）、边缘推抓（平面刚性物体）、杠杆抓取（平面可变形物体）。3) 任务规划器：整合上述原语实现多目标整理。

Result: 充分的真实世界实验证明了该框架的有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该研究为平面刚性和可变形物体提供了实用的操作原语，凸显了环境和物体间约束在复杂多物体操作任务中的作用。

Abstract: Desktop organization remains challenging for service robots because of heterogeneous objects and diverse manipulation objectives, such as collection and stacking. In this article, a task-oriented framework is presented for organizing planar rigid and deformable objects on desks. A perception pipeline was developed that augments existing datasets with uncommon desktop items and makes geometry-based pose and keypoint estimation possible, along with the detection of environmental constraints, such as table edges. To handle diverse manipulation requirements, environment-assisted primitives are used, including contact-based grasping for small objects, edge-based push-grasping for planar rigid objects, and levering-based grasping for planar deformable objects. These primitives leverage environmental and interobject constraints to improve robustness. A task planner was designed to integrate these primitives into multiobject organization. Sufficient real-world experiments demonstrate the effectiveness and robustness of the proposed framework. This research provides practical manipulation primitives for planar rigid and deformable objects, highlighting the role of environmental and interobject constraints in complex multiobject manipulation tasks. Code and video are available online.

</details>


### [151] [Do We Really Need Immediate Resets? Rethinking Collision Handling for Efficient Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02192)
*Shanze Wang,Xinming Zhang,Siwei Cheng,Xianghui Wang,Hailong Huang,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出多碰撞重置预算框架，允许智能体在同一导航回合中多次尝试而非单次碰撞即终止，从而加速早期探索并提升导航成功率与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统深度强化学习导航框架中，单次碰撞即触发全局环境重置并被惩罚为完整任务失败，这阻碍了智能体在训练早期探索具有挑战性的障碍物配置，减缓了学习进度。

Method: 提出多碰撞重置预算框架，将局部碰撞终止与全局环境重置解耦，允许智能体在同一回合内对困难配置进行重试。

Result: 在多个仿真和真实机器人平台上，该框架相较于传统单碰撞重置基线，加速了早期探索，并提升了成功率和导航效率，较小的碰撞预算带来最大收益。

Conclusion: 单次碰撞即终止全局回合并非最优策略，允许有限次数的重试可显著改善深度强化学习导航训练效果。

Abstract: Should a single collision necessarily terminate an entire navigation episode? In most deep reinforcement learning (DRL) frameworks for robot navigation, this remains the standard practice: every collision immediately triggers a global environment reset and is penalized as a complete task failure. While a collision during deployment naturally indicates task failure, applying the same treatment during training prevents the agent from exploring challenging obstacle configurations, which slows learning progress in the early training phase. In this work, we challenge this convention and propose a Multi-Collision reset Budget (MCB) framework that decouples local collision termination from global environment resets, allowing the agent to retry difficult configurations within the same episode. Experiments on multiple simulated and real-world robotic platforms show that the framework accelerates early-stage exploration and improves both success rate and navigation efficiency over conventional single-collision reset baselines, with a small collision budget producing the largest gains.

</details>


### [152] [Change-Robust Online Spatial-Semantic Topological Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02227)
*Jiaming Wang,Jizhuo Chen,Diwen Liu,Atharva Ghotavadekar,Jiaxuan Da,Linh Kästner,Harold Soh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CROSS表征，用在线姿态感知拓扑图替代全局一致度量地图，通过连续SE(3)上的序列假设检验处理感知歧义，在光照变化和家具重排等环境下展现优于SLAM和拓扑基线的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主机器人在环境变化下进行空间-语义推理时，现有基于SLAM的度量地图方法在视觉外观变化和场景动态下数据关联和重定位性能退化，需要更鲁棒的解决方案。

Method: 1) 用在线、姿态感知的RGB-D关键帧拓扑图替代全局一致度量地图；2) 在连续SE(3)空间中使用序列假设检验显式推理感知歧义；3) 维护有界高斯混合姿态置信度，实现闭环检测和绑架机器人事件的原则性处理。

Result: 在严重外观变化（包括真实机器人物体目标导航、光照变化和家具重排）的实验下，CROSS在鲁棒性和安全性方面优于基于SLAM和拓扑的基线方法。

Conclusion: CROSS表征有效解决了环境变化下空间-语义推理的鲁棒性问题，为机器人在动态和变化环境中的导航提供了更可靠的方案。

Abstract: Autonomous robots require change-robust spatial-semantic reasoning: using spatial and semantic knowledge to decide where to go, how to get there, and where the robot is despite environmental change. Existing approaches typically attach semantics to SLAM-built metric maps, but these pipelines are brittle under appearance shifts and scene dynamics, where data association and relocalization degrade. We propose a Change-Robust Online Spatial-Semantic (CROSS) representation that replaces a globally consistent metric substrate with an online, pose-aware topological graph of RGB-D keyframes. The system explicitly reasons over perceptual ambiguity using sequential hypothesis testing in continuous SE(3). Our estimator maintains a bounded Gaussian-mixture belief over poses, enabling principled handling of loop closures and kidnapped-robot events. Experiments under severe appearance change, including real-robot object-goal navigation with lighting shifts and furniture rearrangement, demonstrate improved robustness over SLAM-based and topological baselines while remaining safe under perceptual aliasing.

</details>


### [153] [Exact Higher-Order Derivatives for SE(3) via Analytical/AD Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02252)
*Frank O. Kuehnel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合解析/自动微分（AD）方法，用于SE(3)负对数似然函数的高阶导数计算。通过在点作用接口y=Tx处设置解析/AD分界，结合前向模式对偶数和嵌套对偶数，实现了从一阶梯度到精确Hessian矩阵和高阶导数张量的高效计算，速度比有限差分法快约5倍，且仅需增加约70行解析雅可比代码。同时修复了SO(3)/SE(3)标量基在原点处的可移除奇异性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有李群框架（如GTSAM、manif、Sophus、SymForce、Ceres）针对一阶工作负载优化，但从一阶原语到精确Hessian、观测信息矩阵和高阶导数张量之间存在空白，缺乏一种紧凑且支持自动微分的路径来获得这些精确牛顿步、观测信息协方差估计和协方差修正所需的量。

Method: 采用混合解析/AD方法：在点作用接口y=Tx处设置分界，使用封闭形式的李群雅可比矩阵（解析侧），仅在接口之后应用自动微分（AD侧）。同一源码通过不同标量类型实例化：普通浮点数用于梯度，向量播种对偶数用于单次前向模式精确Hessian，嵌套对偶数用于高阶导数张量。同时修复了标准SO(3)/SE(3)标量基在原点处的可移除奇异性（导致NaN问题）。

Result: 在6自由度、5个地标的SE(3)负对数似然基准测试中，所提出的播种Hessian方法比有限差分AD梯度快约5倍，且与嵌套AD参考值精确匹配至机器精度。实现仅需在纯AD基线上增加约70行解析雅可比代码。

Conclusion: 提供了一条从快速编写的SE(3)目标函数到精确高阶导数的实用路径，具有可预测的运行时间且无需有限差分调参。该方法适用于精确牛顿步、观测信息协方差估计和协方差修正等应用场景。

Abstract: Fast prototyping of new SE(3) estimation objectives remains awkward in practice. Modern Lie-group frameworks -- GTSAM, manif, Sophus, SymForce, Ceres -- target first-order workloads through different code-generation and automatic-differentiation strategies, each optimized for a particular seam between hand-derived geometry and generic differentiation. The remaining gap is a compact, AD-safe path from these first-order primitives to exact Hessians, observed-information matrices, and higher-order derivative tensors: the quantities needed for exact Newton steps, observed-information covariance estimates, and covariance correction.
  This paper presents a hybrid analytical/AD recipe for SE(3) negative log-likelihoods. The practitioner writes the NLL gradient once, generic over a scalar type, and places the analytical/AD seam at the point-action interface y = Tx. Closed-form Lie-group Jacobians are used up to this interface; AD is applied only beyond it. The same source is then instantiated with ordinary floating-point scalars for gradients, vector-seeded dual numbers for exact Hessians in a single forward-mode pass, and nested dual numbers for higher-order derivative tensors. On a representative 6-DoF, 5-landmark SE(3) NLL, the advocated seeded-Hessian path is approximately 5x faster than finite-differencing the AD gradient on this benchmark while matching a nested-AD oracle to machine precision. The implementation adds roughly 70 lines of analytical-Jacobian code over an AD-only baseline. We also identify and fix a removable singularity in the standard SO(3)/SE(3) scalar basis that would otherwise produce NaNs at the origin under seeded AD, and we audit which Lie-group derivative tensors require this stabilized basis. The result is a practical path from rapidly written SE(3) objectives to exact higher-order derivatives, with predictable runtime and no finite-difference tuning.

</details>


### [154] [SAGA: A Robust Self-Attention and Goal-Aware Anchor-based Planner for Safe UAV Autonomous Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02301)
*Junhao Wei,Yanxiao Li,Dexing Yao,Yifu Zhao,Haochen Li,Qibin He,Baili Lu,Xiaofan Zou,Dingcheng Yang,Sio-Kei Im,Yapeng Wang,Xu Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SAGA，一种基于自注意力和目标感知锚点的无人机局部规划器，将局部规划建模为单阶段联合回归与排序问题，在复杂环境中实现了100%的导航成功率，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在杂乱环境中，无人机敏捷导航需要既计算高效又能对多种可行运动进行结构化推理的规划架构，现有方法在高速度下成功率和安全性显著下降。

Method: SAGA将局部规划表述为在固定运动锚点格栅上的单阶段联合回归与排序问题；通过自注意力机制对锚点对齐特征进行全局推理；引入极坐标位置编码（PPE）保留锚点偏航和俯仰的方向结构；设计目标感知调制模块将速度、加速度和目标信息注入令牌表示。

Result: 在2.0、3.0、4.0 m/s最大速度下均实现100%成功率，而YOPO从90.91%降至62.50%，Ego-planner从71.43%降至52.63%，Fast-planner从52.63%降至38.46%。4.0 m/s下，平均安全距离从1.9843m提升至2.3888m，最低安全距离从0.4390m提升至0.7576m，总飞行时间从40.4631s缩短至27.4901s。

Conclusion: SAGA在导航成功率、安全性和飞行效率上全面超越现有方法；极坐标位置编码对稳定跨锚点推理和安全通道选择至关重要。

Abstract: Agile unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) navigation in cluttered environments demands a planning architecture that is both computationally efficient and structurally expressive enough to reason over multiple feasible motions. This paper presents SAGA, a robust self-attention and goal-aware anchor-based planner for safe UAV autonomous navigation. SAGA formulates local planning as a one-stage joint regression-and-ranking problem over a fixed lattice of motion anchors. Given a depth image and a body-frame motion state, the planner predicts refined terminal states and planning scores for all anchors in a single forward pass, after which the best candidate is decoded into a dynamically feasible trajectory. The key idea of SAGA is to transform anchor-aligned features into geometry-aware tokens and perform cross-anchor global reasoning with self-attention. To preserve directional structure in the token space, we further introduce a polar positional encoding derived from anchor yaw and pitch. In addition, a goal-aware modulation module injects velocity, acceleration, and target information into the token representation before final score prediction. Experiments in cluttered pillar-map environments under maximum speed settings of 2.0, 3.0, and 4.0~m/s show that SAGA consistently achieves a 100\% success rate, while YOPO drops from 90.91\% to 62.50\%, Ego-planner from 71.43\% to 52.63\%, and Fast-planner from 52.63\% to 38.46\%. Under the 4.0~m/s maximum speed setting, SAGA also improves average safety from 1.9843~m to 2.3888~m and minimum safety from 0.4390~m to 0.7576~m over YOPO, while reducing total flight time from 40.4631~s to 27.4901~s. The comparison with SAGA w/o PPE further shows that explicit polar positional encoding is critical for stable cross-anchor reasoning and safe passage selection in cluttered scenes.

</details>


### [155] [Natural Gradient Bayesian Filtering: Geometry-Aware Filter for Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02306)
*Chang Liu,Wenhan Cao,Zeju Sun,Tianyi Zhang,Jiayu Yuan,Yi Zeng,Ting Yuan,Yao Lyu,Wei Wu,Stephen Shing-Toung Yau,Shengbo Eben Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文从信息几何视角重新审视高斯滤波，提出基于自然梯度下降的NANO滤波器，通过迭代优化后验均值和协方差，在尊重高斯族内在几何结构的同时保持协方差正定性。在线性高斯情况下，单步自然梯度可精确恢复卡尔曼测量更新。在卫星姿态估计、SLAM及机器人状态估计等非线性问题中验证了方法有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高斯滤波器（如EKF、UKF）在处理非线性系统时依赖欧几里得空间中的近似，忽略了高斯分布统计流形的内在几何结构，导致估计精度和数值稳定性受限。本文旨在从信息几何角度统一高斯滤波框架，并利用自然梯度下降改善非线性状态估计。

Method: 将预测和测量更新视为高斯分布统计流形上的推理过程，采用自然梯度下降迭代更新后验均值和协方差。利用Fisher信息矩阵作为黎曼度量，在尊重高斯族内在几何结构的同时保证协方差矩阵的正定性。在线性高斯情形下，单步自然梯度更新等价于经典卡尔曼滤波测量更新。

Result: 卫星姿态估计、SLAM以及四足/人形机器人状态估计等典型非线性问题中，NANO滤波器相比传统高斯滤波器展现出更好的估计精度和数值稳定性。

Conclusion: 信息几何视角为高斯滤波提供了统一的理论框架，自然梯度方法在保持与经典方法联系的同时，为非线性状态估计提供了更优的近似方案。

Abstract: Bayesian filtering is a cornerstone of state estimation in complex systems such as aerospace systems, yet exact solutions are available only for linear Gaussian models. In practice,nonlinear systems are handled through tractable approximations,with Gaussian filters such as the extended and unscented Kalman filters being among the most widely used methods. This tutorial revisits Gaussian filtering from an information-geometric perspective, viewing the prediction and measurement update steps as inference procedures over state distributions. Within this framework, we introduce a geometry-aware Gaussian filtering approach that leverages natural gradient descent on the statistical manifold of Gaussian distributions. The resulting Natural Gradient Gaussian Approximation (NANO) filter iteratively refines the posterior mean and covariance while respecting the intrinsic geometry of the Gaussian family and preserving the positive definiteness of the covariance matrix. We further highlight fundamental connections to the classical Kalman filtering, showing that a single natural-gradient step exactly recovers the Kalman measurement update in the linear-Gaussian case. The practical implications of the proposed framework are illustrated through case studies in representative nonlinear estimation problems,including satellite attitude estimation, simultaneous localization and mapping, and state estimation for robotic systems including quadruped and humanoid robots.

</details>


### [156] [ShapeGrasp: Simultaneous Visuo-Haptic Shape Completion and Grasping for Improved Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02347)
*Lukas Rustler,Matej Hoffmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ShapeGrasp是一种机器人抓取方法，通过结合视觉初始估计与触觉/ proprioceptive 反馈，迭代地进行形状补全和抓取规划。在真实世界实验中，使用两种不同机器人手臂和夹爪，抓取成功率分别达到84%（三指夹爪）和91%（两指夹爪），同时提升了3D形状重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类抓取陌生物体时会结合视觉初始估计与触觉反馈，而现有机器人抓取方法通常缺乏在抓取过程中动态更新物体形状表示的能力，导致抓取鲁棒性不足。

Method: 提出迭代式抓取-补全流程：1) 从单张RGB-D图像推断完整形状；2) 通过刚体仿真生成候选抓取并执行最佳抓取；3) 每次抓取尝试后融合触觉表面接触和夹爪占用空间作为几何约束，更新物体形状；4) 失败时利用精化形状重新估计位姿并再次抓取。

Result: 在真实世界中使用两种不同机器人（及三指和两指夹爪）进行评估，抓取成功率分别为84%和91%，优于基线方法；所有评估指标下的3D形状重建质量均得到提升。

Conclusion: ShapeGrasp是首个在真实世界抓取后更新形状表示的方法，证明了结合触觉反馈进行迭代形状补全能显著提升抓取成功率和形状重建质量。

Abstract: Humans grasp unfamiliar objects by combining an initial visual estimate with tactile and proprioceptive feedback during interaction. We present ShapeGrasp, a robotic implementation of this approach. The proposed method is an iterative grasp-and-complete pipeline that couples implicit surface visuo-haptic shape completion (creation of full 3D shape from partial information) with physics-based grasp planning. From a single RGB-D view, ShapeGrasp infers a complete shape (point cloud or triangular mesh), generates candidate grasps via rigid-body simulation, and executes the best feasible grasp. Each grasp attempt yields additional geometric constraints -- tactile surface contacts and space occupied by the gripper body -- which are fused to update the object shape. Failures trigger pose re-estimation and regrasping using the refined shape. We evaluate ShapeGrasp in the real world using two different robots and grippers. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first approach that updates shape representations following a real-world grasp. We achieved superior results over baselines for both grippers (grasp success rate of 84% with a three-finger gripper and 91% with a two-finger gripper), while improving the 3D shape reconstruction quality in all evaluation metrics used.

</details>


### [157] [Feedback Motion Planning for Stochastic Nonlinear Systems with Signal Temporal Logic Specifications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02361)
*Liqian Ma,Zishun Liu,Glen Chou,Yongxin Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于谓词侵蚀策略和概率可达管的框架，用于连续时间随机非线性系统在信号时序逻辑规范下的反馈运动规划，通过将随机问题转化为确定性优化问题，实现了高概率满足STL规范的控制策略合成，并在仿真和实际四足机器人上验证了其有效性和优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以处理连续时间随机非线性系统在STL规范下的运动规划问题，尤其是确保闭环系统以极高概率（如99.99%）满足给定STL公式的控制策略综合。

Method: 提出谓词侵蚀策略，利用概率可达管（PRT）来量化随机轨迹与名义轨迹之间的偏差，基于收缩理论和反馈设计计算侵蚀量，将随机机会约束优化问题转化为确定性的STL轨迹优化问题，并开发了多种跟踪控制器。

Result: 在多个机器人系统仿真和真实四足机器人实验中，所提框架相比代表性基线方法具有更低的保守性和更高的规范满足概率。

Conclusion: 该框架为随机非线性系统在STL规范下的反馈运动规划提供了一条完整的、可数值优化实现的途径，兼顾了理论保证与实际可行性。

Abstract: We study feedback motion planning for continuous-time stochastic nonlinear systems under signal temporal logic (STL) specifications. We propose a framework that synthesizes control policies for chance-constrained STL trajectory optimization problems, with the goal of ensuring that the closed-loop stochastic system satisfies a given STL formula with high probability (e.g., 99.99\%). Our approach is based on a predicate erosion strategy that transforms the intractable stochastic problem into a deterministic STL trajectory optimization problem with tightened STL formula constraints. The amount of erosion is determined by a probabilistic reachable tube (PRT) that bounds the deviation between the stochastic trajectory and an associated nominal trajectory. To compute such bounds, we leverage contraction theory and feedback design, and develop several tracking controllers. This yields a complete feedback motion planning pipeline which can be implemented by numerical optimizations. We demonstrate the efficacy and versatility of the proposed framework through simulations on several robotic systems and through experiments on a real-world quadrupedal robot, and show that it is less conservative and achieves higher specification satisfaction probability than representative baselines.

</details>


### [158] [Robust Adaptive Predictive Control for Hook-Based Aerial Transportation Between Moving Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02370)
*Péter Antal,Andrea Carron,Melanie Zeilinger,Roland Tóth,Tamás Péni*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于鲁棒自适应模型预测控制的方法，利用MuJoCo数字孪生模型和零阶鲁棒优化与扩展卡尔曼滤波结合，实现了钩式空中机械臂在移动平台间的自主抓取与放置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中机械臂在移动平台间执行抓取放置任务时，系统动力学复杂且存在气动效应、负载变化等不确定性，传统控制方法难以同时保证鲁棒性、高性能和计算效率。

Method: 1) 在MuJoCo中构建带钩式夹爪的四旋翼数字孪生模型作为MPC预测模型；2) 提出鲁棒自适应MPC方法，集成零阶鲁棒优化（zoRO）进行不确定性传播，结合扩展卡尔曼滤波（EKF）进行参数估计，实现鲁棒约束满足。

Result: 在复杂仿真场景和真实飞行实验中验证了方法的有效性，表明该方法在鲁棒性、性能和计算效率方面表现良好。

Conclusion: 所提出的鲁棒自适应MPC方法能够有效处理空中机械臂在动态环境中的模型不确定性，实现可靠的自主抓取放置操作。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel model predictive control (MPC) approach for autonomous pick-and-place between moving platforms with a hook-equipped aerial manipulator. First, for accurate and rapid modeling of the complex dynamics, a digital twin model of the quadcopter equipped with a hook-based gripper, implemented in MuJoCo, is constructed and used as the predictive model for the MPC. To handle uncertainties of the predictive model (e.g. due to aerodynamics and uncertain payloads), a robust adaptive MPC approach is proposed. By systematic integration of zero-order robust optimization (zoRO) based uncertainty propagation and an extended Kalman filter (EKF) for parameter estimation, the MPC algorithm ensures robust constraint satisfaction, high performance, and computational efficiency. The effectiveness of the proposed method is evaluated in complex simulated scenarios and in real-world flight experiments.

</details>


### [159] [Shared Autonomy Assisted by Impedance-Driven Anisotropic Guidance Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02410)
*Sihan Chen,Hang Xu,Yupu Lu,Chen Wang,Benfang Duan,Ruixing Jia,Jia Pan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出阻抗驱动各向异性引导场增强共享自主（IAGF-SA），通过阻抗控制构建具身物理通信通道，使机器人能自适应调节对人输入的动态响应，实现直观、连续、物理具身的意图传达，提升人机协作效率与体验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有共享自主研究侧重提升机器人对人类意图的推理能力，但忽略了人类能否理解机器人意图这一关键问题。缺乏这种相互理解会导致协作效率低下，而以往通过附加界面显式传达机器人意图的方式既不直观，表达能力也有限。

Method: 受阻抗控制启发，提出IAGF-SA范式，在共享自主框架中嵌入一个具身的、物理层面的通信通道。该通道通过自适应调节机器人对外部输入的动态响应（即各向异性引导场），实现机器人意图的直观、连续、物理嵌入式的传达，同时自然引导人类操作行为。

Result: 在三个场景和两种遥操作界面上的用户研究表明，IAGF-SA在任务性能、人机一致性以及主观体验方面均有显著提升。

Conclusion: IAGF-SA通过物理具身的隐式通信机制有效增强了人机之间的相互理解与协作，为解决共享自主中的双向意图沟通问题提供了一种新颖且有效的范式。

Abstract: Shared autonomy (SA) enables robots to infer human intent and assist in its achievement. While most research focuses on improving intent inference, it overlooks whether humans can understand the robot's intent in return. Without such mutual understanding, collaboration becomes less effective, degrading user experience and task performance. To address this gap, previous studies have explicitly conveyed the robot intent through additional interfaces, which remain unintuitive and limited in expressiveness. Inspired by impedance control, we propose Impedance-Driven Anisotropic Guidance Field Enhanced Shared Autonomy (IAGF-SA), a novel paradigm that extends SA with an embodied, physically-grounded communication channel. This channel adaptively modulates the robot's dynamic response to human input, enabling intuitive, continuous, physically-grounded robot intent communication while naturally guiding human actions. User studies across three scenarios and two teleoperation interfaces indicate that IAGF-SA improves task performance, human-robot agreement, and subjective experience, thus demonstrating its effectiveness in enhancing human-robot communication and collaboration.

</details>


### [160] [Higher-Order Flexible Configurations of Planar Parallel Manipulators Constructed by Averaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02434)
*Yudi Zhao,Georg Nawratil*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究平面3-RPR并联机器人的奇异构型，通过参数化输入对并确定相对方向，在不计算六次多项式零点的情况下提高平均构型的弯曲阶数，并可视化结果。该方法可推广到球面和空间类似机构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究平面3-RPR并联机器人中由于对直接运动学问题解对应用平均技术而产生的奇异构型，旨在不通过计算六次多项式零点的方式理解和分析这些奇异构型。

Method: 参数化输入对并确定其相对方向，从而在不计算六次多项式零点的情况下提高平均构型的弯曲阶数；通过具体例子可视化结果。

Result: 成功实现了对奇异构型中平均配置弯曲阶数的提升，并通过具体算例进行了可视化展示。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法不仅适用于平面3-RPR并联机器人，还可推广到球面和空间类似并联机构的研究中。

Abstract: This paper investigates singular configurations of planar 3-RPR parallel manipulators, which result from applying the averaging technique to solution pairs of their direct kinematic problem. Without computing the zeros of the corresponding degree 6 polynomial we parametrize the input pairs and determine their relative orientation in a way that the flexion order of the averaged configurations increases. Moreover, the obtained results are visualized for concrete examples. The presented methodology can also be used for studying the spherical and spatial analogues of planar 3-RPR parallel manipulators.

</details>


### [161] [Visibility-Aware Mobile Grasping in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02487)
*Tianrun Hu,Anxing Xiao,David Hsu,Hanbo Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向动态未知环境、有限视场下移动机器人抓取的统一系统，包含基于速度感知的底层全身规划器和基于行为树的分层高层规划器，在400组仿真和真实Fetch机器人上验证，成功率达68.8%（静态未知）和58.0%（动态未知），较基线方法分别提升22.8%和18.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机器人在动态未知环境中进行抓取时面临"观察"（减少环境不确定性）与"移动"（完成任务进展）之间的根本性权衡，且受限于有限视场。现有方法通常假设环境已知或静态，将感知与运动解耦，无法保证在操作过程中未观测的动态障碍物与机器人路径相交时的安全性。

Method: 提出统一移动抓取系统，包含两个核心组件：(1) 底层全身规划器（迭代式）耦合速度感知主动感知机制，安全导航动态环境；(2) 基于行为树的分层高层规划器，自适应生成子目标以引导机器人探索并处理运行时失败。

Result: 在400组随机仿真场景和真实Fetch移动机械臂上部署。在未知静态和动态环境中分别达到68.8%和58.0%的成功率，较基线方法在两种环境下分别提升22.8%和18.0%，碰撞安全性也得到改善。

Conclusion: 所提出的统一系统通过将主动感知与全身规划耦合、结合行为树分层决策，有效解决了有限视场下移动抓取中观察与运动的权衡问题，显著提升了动态未知环境中的成功率和安全性。

Abstract: This paper addresses the problem of mobile grasping in dynamic, unknown environments where a robot must operate under a limited field-of-view. The fundamental challenge is the inherent trade-off between ``seeing'' around to reduce environmental uncertainty and ``moving'' the body to achieve task progress in a high-dimensional configuration space, subject to visibility constraints. Previous approaches often assume known or static environments and decouple these objectives, failing to guarantee safety when unobserved dynamic obstacles intersect the robot's path during manipulation. In this paper, we propose a unified mobile grasping system comprising two core components: (1) an iterative low-level whole-body planner coupled with velocity-aware active perception to navigate dynamic environments safely; and (2) a hierarchical high-level planner based on behavior trees that adaptively generates subgoals to guide the robot through exploration and runtime failures. We provide experimental results across 400 randomized simulation scenarios and real-world deployment on a Fetch mobile manipulator. Results show that our system achieves a success rate of 68.8\% and 58.0\% in unknown static and dynamic environments, respectively, significantly boosting success rates by 22.8\% and 18.0\% over the \nam approach in both unknown static and dynamic environments, with improved collision safety.

</details>


### [162] [Adaptive Gait Generation for Multi-Terrain Exoskeletons via Constrained Kernelized Movement Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02513)
*Edoardo Trombin,Miroljub Mihailovic,Matheus Henrique Ferreira Moura,Luca Tonin,Emanuele Menegatti,Stefano Tortora*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于核化运动原语(KMP)的自适应步态生成框架，通过学习有限的人类示教数据中的概率步态表征，并结合RGB-D相机提取的环境信息进行轨迹优化，实现在多种室内地形（平地、斜坡、楼梯、障碍物）上的环境感知步态规划，在商业下肢外骨骼上验证了其有效性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前下肢外骨骼缺乏有效的自适应步态规划方法，仅能行走于平坦地面，无法在真实世界的多种地形中应用，亟需一种环境感知、生理一致且能实时生成步态轨迹的方法。

Method: 基于核化运动原语(KMP)框架，从少量人类示教中学习关节空间和任务空间中的概率步态表征；将自适应步态生成建模为带via-point的线性约束优化问题，利用RGB-D相机提取的环境信息进行轨迹自适应调整。

Result: 在仿真中验证了多种场景（平地、斜坡、楼梯、障碍物）下的步态生成效果，并在商业下肢外骨骼上通过真实世界实验证明了方法的有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提出的环境感知步态规划系统可行，为新一代智能下肢外骨骼辅助残疾人日常行走提供了技术基础。

Abstract: Lower limb exoskeletons (LLEs) present the potential to make motor-impaired individuals walk again. Their application in real-world environments is still limited by the lack of effective adaptive gait planning. Indeed, current exoskeletons are meant to walk only on a flat and even terrain. Generating environment-aware, physiologically consistent gait trajectories in real-time is an open challenge. To overcome this, we propose a novel Kernelized Movement Primitives (KMP)-based framework for adaptive gait generation (AGG) across multiple indoor terrains. The proposed approach learns a probabilistic representation of human gait in both the joint and task spaces from a limited number of human demonstrations, representing natural gait characteristics and ensuring kinematic feasibility. In addition, the learned trajectories are adapted using environmental information extracted from an onboard RGB-D camera by treating the AGG as a linearly constrained optimization problem with via-points. The proposed method has been thoroughly validated first in simulations for gait generation in different scenarios, such as flat-ground walking, slopes, stairs, and obstacles crossing. Finally, the effectiveness and robustness of the method have been demonstrated with experiments on a commercial LLE in real-world scenarios. The results obtained demonstrate the feasibility of an environment-aware gait planning system for a new generation of intelligent lower limb exoskeletons for assisting people with disabilities in their every-day life.

</details>


### [163] [A Semantic Autonomy Framework for VLM-Integrated Indoor Mobile Robots: Hybrid Deterministic Reasoning and Cross-Robot Adaptive Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02525)
*Bogdan Felician Abaza,Andrei-Alexandru Staicu,Cristian Vasile Doicin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出语义自主栈（Semantic Autonomy Stack），一种六层参考框架，通过在物理机器人上结合混合确定性-VLM推理和跨机器人自适应记忆，实现室内移动机器人的语义导航。实验表明，88%的指令可在0.1毫秒内由参数解析器处理，VLM仅处理真正的歧义指令；跨机器人知识迁移实现103,000倍的延迟降低，语义迁移准确率100%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主室内移动机器人能够可靠地导航到度量坐标，但缺乏解释自然语言指令的能力。VLM虽能提供语义推理，但其推理延迟高（消费硬件上每次决策2-9秒）且存在会话性遗忘问题，限制了实际部署。

Method: 提出六层语义自主栈参考框架，包含七个步骤的参数解析器（处理88%的指令，无需VLM/摄像头/GPU）、五类语义记忆框架（带显式范围分类：全局环境知识、操作员偏好、机器人能力），以及混合确定性-VLM推理机制，实现跨会话学习和跨机器人知识迁移。

Result: 在82个场景级决策和三个会话中，实现100%语义迁移准确率（33/33, 95% CI [0.894, 1.000]）、100%语义解析准确率，跨机器人知识迁移实现103,000倍延迟降低。所有实验在Raspberry Pi 5平台上完成，无需GPU和训练数据。

Conclusion: 该框架证明，通过混合确定性-VLM推理和结构化语义记忆，可以在低成本边缘硬件上实现高效的语义导航，同时支持多机器人并发操作，无需训练数据。

Abstract: Autonomous indoor mobile robots can navigate reliably to metric coordinates using established frameworks such as ROS 2 Navigation 2, yet they lack the ability to interpret natural language instructions that express intent rather than positions. Vision-Language Models offer the semantic reasoning required to bridge this gap, but their inference latency (2-9 seconds per decision on consumer hardware) and session-by-session amnesia limit practical deployment. This paper presents the Semantic Autonomy Stack, a six-layer reference framework for semantically autonomous indoor navigation, and validates a complete instance featuring hybrid deterministic-VLM reasoning and cross-robot adaptive memory on physical robots with off-the-shelf edge hardware. A seven-step parametric resolver handles 88% of instructions in under 0.1 milliseconds without invoking a language model, camera, or GPU; only genuinely ambiguous instructions escalate to VLM reasoning. A five-category semantic memory framework with explicit scope taxonomy (global environment knowledge, per-operator preferences, per-robot capabilities) enables cross-session learning and cross-robot knowledge transfer: preferences learned through VLM interactions on one robot are promoted to deterministic resolution and transferred to a second robot via a shared compiled digest, achieving a measured latency reduction of 103,000-fold. Experimental validation on two custom-built differential-drive robots across 82 scenario-level decisions and three sessions demonstrates 100% semantic transfer accuracy (33/33, 95% CI [0.894, 1.000]), 100% semantic resolution accuracy, and concurrent multi-robot operation feasibility - all on Raspberry Pi 5 platforms with no onboard GPU, requiring zero training data.

</details>


### [164] [Beyond Specialization: Robust Reinforcement Learning Navigation via Procedural Map Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02528)
*Christian Jestel,Nicolas Bach,Marvin Wiedemann,Jan Finke,Peter Detzner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了四种程序化地图生成器（稀疏、迷宫、图、波函数坍缩）对DRL导航策略泛化能力的影响，发现跨生成器迁移呈现强不对称性；结合A*路径规划子目标输入可显著提升鲁棒性（成功率从90.2%提升至98.9%）；学习到的速度适应是DRL策略超越经典控制器的决定性优势；真实机器人实验验证了仿真到现实的迁移能力，同时揭示了循环结构对缓解失败模式的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DRL导航策略常过拟合训练环境结构，环境多样性受限于人工设计场景的投入；程序化地图生成虽可提供可扩展多样性，但尚无工作系统比较不同生成器类型对策略泛化的影响。

Method: 将四种具可导航性保证的地图生成器（稀疏、迷宫、图、WFC）集成到2D仿真器MuRoSim中；跨评估五种导航策略，每种生成器使用1000个种子地图，三个训练种子；进行跨生成器迁移测试、消融实验（A*子目标输入 vs GRU循环）、与经典Carrot+A*控制器对比，以及真实RoboMaster平台验证。

Result: 跨生成器迁移呈强不对称性（稀疏布局专长者迷宫成功率仅3.3%）；组合生成器训练策略达91.5±1.1%平均成功率；A*子目标输入将成功率从90.2±1.4%提升至98.9±0.4%；GRU仅改善反应式基线；DRL在高速（2.0m/s）远超Carrot+A*（24.9% vs 近100%）；真实场景中迷宫布局暴露了循环结构可缓解的失败模式。

Conclusion: 组合多种程序化地图生成器训练可显著提升DRL导航策略的泛化能力；A*路径规划子目标输入是鲁棒性的主导因素；学习到的速度适应是DRL相对经典方法的决定性优势；循环结构有助于缓解特定失败模式，但A*输入贡献更大。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning (DRL) navigation policies often overfit to the structure of their training environments, as environmental diversity is typically constrained by the manual effort required to design diverse scenarios. While procedural map generation offers scalable diversity, no prior work systematically compares how different generator types affect policy generalization. We integrate four generators (sparse, maze, graph, and Wave Function Collapse) with guaranteed navigability into MuRoSim, a 2D simulator focusing on training efficiency for LiDAR-based navigation. We cross-evaluate five navigation policies on 1000 seeded maps per generator across three training seeds. Results show a strongly asymmetric cross-generator transfer: a specialist trained on sparse layouts falls to 3.3% success on mazes, whereas a policy trained on the combined generator set achieves 91.5 +/- 1.1% mean success. We further demonstrate that A* path-planner subgoal inputs are the dominant factor for robustness, raising success from the 90.2 +/- 1.4% feedforward baseline to 98.9 +/- 0.4% and outperforming GRU recurrence, which only improves the reactive baseline. The DRL policies outperform a classical Carrot+A* controller, which matches their success only at low speeds (1.0 m/s) but collapses to 24.9% at 2.0 m/s. This highlights learned speed adaptation as the decisive advantage of the learned approach. Real-world experiments on a RoboMaster confirm sim-to-real transfer in a cluttered arena, while a maze-like layout exposes remaining failure modes that recurrence helps mitigate.

</details>


### [165] [Sim-to-Real Transfer and Robustness Evaluation of Reinforcement Learning Control with Integrated Perception on an ASV for Floating Waste Capture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02529)
*Luis F. W. Batista,Stéphanie Aravecchia,Cédric Pradalier*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了一个结合偏振视觉感知与轻量级深度强化学习控制器的自主水面垃圾清理系统，通过两阶段仿真协议和感知抽象模块实现从仿真到真实世界的可靠迁移，在14种扰动条件下达到厘米级终端精度，并在450平方米的真实水域成功完成搜索捕获任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主水面垃圾清理船面临复杂水动力学、外部扰动和 challenging 的水面感知问题，现有方法在从仿真迁移到真实环境时存在显著性能退化，缺乏系统性的 sim-to-real 评估方法。

Method: 提出两阶段仿真协议（先训练控制器，再评估 sim-to-real 差距）与感知抽象模块（模拟真实相机行为）；采用偏振相机进行水面目标检测，将检测结果转换为水面目标点；使用轻量级DRL控制器进行跟踪；在14种扰动条件下进行匹配的仿真与实地实验。

Result: 实现了厘米级终端精度，在评估的扰动条件下展现出鲁棒的控制性能；在450平方米真实水域中成功完成基于真实相机检测的搜索捕获任务；主要性能退化来源为执行机构模型保真度不足。

Conclusion: 提高执行机构模型保真度、针对性域随机化、谨慎管理模块间延迟和时间戳是可靠迁移的关键；尽管取得了成功，但仍存在挑战有待解决。

Abstract: Autonomous surface vessels for floating-waste removal operate under varying hydrodynamics, external disturbances, and challenging water-surface perception. We present a field-validated system that combines camera-based polarimetric perception with a lightweight DRL-based controller for floating-waste detection and capture. Camera detections are converted into water-surface target points and tracked by a controller trained entirely in simulation and deployed directly on a retrofitted ASV platform. Our main contribution is a sim-to-real testing methodology that combines a two-stage simulation protocol with a perception abstraction module designed to mimic real camera behavior, enabling reproducible field trials and explicit evaluation of the sim-to-real gap. We apply this framework in matched simulation and field experiments across 14 disturbance regimes to expose failure modes and evaluate robustness. The results show centimeter-level terminal accuracy and indicate robust control performance under the evaluated perturbation regimes. The main source of degradation is insufficient actuation-model fidelity. We also demonstrate the system in a search-and-capture application using real camera detections in real-world conditions over areas of up to $450~m^2$. The study distills practical lessons for reliable transfer, including improved actuation-model fidelity, targeted domain randomization, and careful management of latency and timestamps across modules, while highlighting remaining challenges.

</details>


### [166] [Orchestrating Spatial Semantics via a Zone-Graph Paradigm for Intricate Indoor Scene Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02537)
*Meisheng Zhang,Shizhao Sun,Yang Zhao,Ziyuan Liu,Zhijun Gao,Jiang Bian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ZoneMaestro提出了一种基于区域图编排（Zone-Graph Orchestration）的统一框架，将室内场景合成从以物体为中心转向以区域逻辑驱动，通过构建Zone-Scene-10K数据集和交替对齐策略（Z-GRPO），在复杂非凸房间中实现了语义丰富性与几何有效性的协同优化，并在新定义的复杂空间编排任务上显著超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主3D室内场景合成方法在非凸房间中因空间约束耦合紧密而失效；数据驱动的生成器缺乏用于长程规划的拓扑先验，迭代式智能体则导致语义碎片化和几何脆弱性。亟需一种能够统一处理复杂建筑形态、兼顾拓扑约束与语义意图的框架。

Method: 提出ZoneMaestro框架，核心包括：(1) 区域图编曲范式，将高层语义意图转化为功能区域和拓扑约束；(2) Zone-Scene-10K大规模数据集，带有显式区域图标注；(3) 交替对齐策略（Alternating Alignment Strategy），在推理内化与区域感知组相对策略优化（Z-GRPO）之间循环迭代，无需外部物理引擎即可调和语义丰富性与几何有效性。

Result: 在SCALE压力测试基准上，ZoneMaestro在结构一致性和意图遵循度上显著优于现有SOTA方法，有效解决了高密度布局与安全性之间的二难困境。

Conclusion: ZoneMaestro通过区域图编曲范式将室内场景合成从物体级拼凑提升到拓扑结构级编排，为复杂室内空间智能合成提供了新的方法论，并验证了在非凸、密集空间关系场景下的鲁棒性和优越性。

Abstract: Autonomous 3D indoor scene synthesis breaks down in non-convex rooms with tightly coupled spatial constraints. Data-driven generators lack topological priors for long-horizon planning, while iterative agents fragment semantics and become geometrically brittle. We present ZoneMaestro, a unified framework that shifts the paradigm from object-centric synthesis to Zone-Graph Orchestration. By internalizing a novel zone-based logic, ZoneMaestro translates high-level semantic intent into functional zones and topological constraints, enabling robust adaptation to diverse architectural forms. To support this, we construct Zone-Scene-10K, a large-scale dataset enriched with explicit Zone-Graph annotations. We further introduce an Alternating Alignment Strategy that cycles between reasoning internalization and Zone-Aware Group Relative Policy Optimization (Z-GRPO), effectively reconciling the tension between semantic richness and geometric validity without relying on external physics engines. To rigorously evaluate spatial intelligence beyond convex primitives, we formally define the task of Intricate Spatial Orchestration and release SCALE, a stress-test benchmark for irregular indoor scenarios with complex, dense spatial relations. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ZoneMaestro resolves the density-safety dichotomy, significantly outperforming state-of-the-art baselines in both structural coherence and intent adherence.

</details>


### [167] [CoRAL: Contact-Rich Adaptive LLM-based Control for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02600)
*Berk Çiçek,Mert K. Er,Özgür S. Öğüz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CoRAL是一种模块化框架，通过将LLM作为成本函数设计器而非直接控制器，结合神经符号自适应循环和检索记忆单元，实现零样本接触丰富操作规划，在模拟和真实场景中成功率平均提升超50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM和VLM在高层次推理和语义理解方面表现出色，但直接应用于接触丰富操作时缺乏显式物理基础和自适应控制能力，需要弥合高层次语义推理与动态接触需求之间的鸿沟。

Method: 提出CoRAL框架：(1)将LLM作为成本函数设计器，为基于采样的运动规划器(MPPI)合成上下文感知的目标函数；(2)神经符号自适应循环：VLM提供环境动力学语义先验（如质量、摩擦力估计），通过在线系统辨识实时精化，LLM根据交互反馈迭代调制成本函数结构；(3)基于检索的记忆单元复用成功策略；(4)解耦高层次语义推理与反应式执行确保实时控制稳定性。

Result: 在模拟和真实硬件上的新颖任务（如利用墙体外接触翻转物体）中，CoRAL相比SOTA VLA和基于基础模型的规划器基线，在未见过的接触丰富场景中成功率平均提升超过50%，有效处理了sim-to-real差距。

Conclusion: CoRAL通过将LLM/VLM用于高层次语义推理和成本函数设计，而非直接控制，结合在线自适应和记忆机制，有效弥合了语义推理与物理控制之间的鸿沟，实现了零样本接触丰富操作。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) and Vision-Language Models (VLMs) demonstrate remarkable capabilities in high-level reasoning and semantic understanding, applying them directly to contact-rich manipulation remains a challenge due to their lack of explicit physical grounding and inability to perform adaptive control. To bridge this gap, we propose CoRAL (Contact-Rich Adaptive LLM-based control), a modular framework that enables zero-shot planning by decoupling high-level reasoning from low-level control. Unlike black-box policies, CoRAL uses LLMs not as direct controllers, but as cost designers that synthesize context-aware objective functions for a sampling-based motion planner (MPPI). To address the ambiguity of physical parameters in visual data, we introduce a neuro-symbolic adaptation loop: a VLM provides semantic priors for environmental dynamics, such as mass and friction estimates, which are then explicitly refined in real time via online system identification, while the LLM iteratively modulates the cost-function structure to correct strategic errors based on interaction feedback. Furthermore, a retrieval-based memory unit allows the system to reuse successful strategies across recurrent tasks. This hierarchical architecture ensures real-time control stability by decoupling high-level semantic reasoning from reactive execution, effectively bridging the gap between slow LLM inference and dynamic contact requirements. We validate CoRAL on both simulation and real-world hardware across challenging and novel tasks, such as flipping objects against walls by leveraging extrinsic contacts. Experiments demonstrate that CoRAL outperforms state-of-the-art VLA and foundation-model-based planner baselines by boosting success rates over 50% on average in unseen contact-rich scenarios, effectively handling sim-to-real gaps through its adaptive physical understanding.

</details>


### [168] [AnchorD: Metric Grounding of Monocular Depth Using Factor Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02667)
*Simon Dorer,Martin Büchner,Nick Heppert,Abhinav Valada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AnchorDepth，一种无需训练的深度接地框架，通过因子图优化将单目深度估计先验与原始传感器深度进行局部仿射对齐，以解决透明、高光等非朗伯表面上的深度估计误差，并在包含非朗伯物体的新基准数据集上展示了性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度传感器在透明、高光及非朗伯表面对上存在显著误差，而大规模单目深度估计方法虽能提供强结构先验，但预测结果在度量单位上存在偏差或缩放问题，限制了其在机器人领域的直接应用。

Method: 提出训练无关的深度接地框架，通过因子图优化将深度基础模型的单目深度估计先验锚定到原始传感器深度中；具体采用逐块仿射对齐方法，在保持细粒度几何结构和不连续性的同时，将单目预测局部接地到度量真实世界深度。

Result: 在多种传感器和不同领域的广泛评估中，无需任何（再）训练即实现了一致的深度性能提升；同时引入了一个包含非朗伯物体的密集场景级真实深度基准数据集，通过哑光反射喷雾和多相机融合获取真实深度。

Conclusion: 该方法有效融合了单目深度先验与传感器深度信息的优势，为机器人操作、抓取和导航中的深度估计问题提供了一种实用且无需重新训练的解决方案。

Abstract: Dense and accurate depth estimation is essential for robotic manipulation, grasping, and navigation, yet currently available depth sensors are prone to errors on transparent, specular, and general non-Lambertian surfaces. To mitigate these errors, large-scale monocular depth estimation approaches provide strong structural priors, but their predictions can be potentially skewed or mis-scaled in metric units, limiting their direct use in robotics.
