Thus, in this work, we propose a training-free depth grounding framework that anchors monocular depth estimation priors from a depth foundation model in raw sensor depth through factor graph optimization. Our method performs a patch-wise affine alignment, locally grounding monocular predictions in metric real-world depth while preserving fine-grained geometric structure and discontinuities. To facilitate evaluation in challenging real-world conditions, we introduce a benchmark dataset with dense scene-wide ground truth depth in the presence of non-Lambertian objects. Ground truth is obtained via matte reflection spray and multi-camera fusion, overcoming the reliance on object-only CAD-based annotations used in prior datasets. Extensive evaluations across diverse sensors and domains demonstrate consistent improvements in depth performance without any (re-)training. We make our implementation publicly available at https://anchord.cs.uni-freiburg.de.

</details>


### [169] [Temporally Consistent Object 6D Pose Estimation for Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02708)
*Kateryna Zorina,Vojtech Priban,Mederic Fourmy,Josef Sivic,Vladimir Petrik*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出基于因子图的方法，通过引入物体运动模型、显式估计测量不确定性以及在线优化，增强单视角RGB物体姿态估计的时间一致性，从而提升机器人视觉反馈控制的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现成的单视图RGB物体姿态估计器虽然精度高、效率好，但缺乏时间一致性和鲁棒性，无法满足视觉反馈控制对稳定性的要求。

Method: 采用因子图方法，包含三个关键组件：(i) 融合物体运动模型，(ii) 显式估计物体姿态测量的不确定性，(iii) 将上述两者整合到在线优化估计器中，并配合异常值剔除与平滑处理。

Result: 在标准姿态估计基准上显著提升了结果；在基于反馈的机器人控制任务（相机安装在力矩控制机械臂上追踪物体）中验证了方法的稳定性。

Conclusion: 因子图方法能有效增强姿态估计的时间一致性，是实现稳定机器人视觉反馈控制的有效途径。

Abstract: Single-view RGB object pose estimators have reached a level of precision and efficiency that makes them good candidates for vision-based robot control. However, off-the-shelf methods lack temporal consistency and robustness that are mandatory for a stable feedback control. In this work, we develop a factor graph approach to enforce temporal consistency of the object pose estimates. In particular, the proposed approach: (i) incorporates object motion models, (ii) explicitly estimates the object pose measurement uncertainty, and (iii) integrates the above two components in an online optimization-based estimator. We demonstrate that with appropriate outlier rejection and smoothing using the proposed factor graph approach, we can significantly improve the results on standardized pose estimation benchmarks. We experimentally validate the stability of the proposed approach for a feedback-based robot control task in which the object is tracked by the camera attached to a torque controlled manipulator.

</details>


### [170] [Tensegrity crutches with compliance from a pre-stressed self-tensile module improve ground reaction force profiles, speed, effort, comfort, and perceived stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02710)
*Jingxian Gu,Joanna Spyra,Andrew Walski,Lyla Elsaesser,Samuel Bierner,Dobromir Dotov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 受生物启发的张拉整体拐杖相比传统刚性拐杖能降低峰值负载率，并比弹簧拐杖在舒适度、用力感和可用性方面表现更优，同时不牺牲稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 美国约600万人使用拐杖作为移动辅助工具，但现有刚性设计缺乏轴向移动性，限制感觉反馈并导致上肢关节继发性损伤；弹簧加载设计虽提供顺应性但可能损害稳定性。因此需要一种同时具备顺应性和稳定性的拐杖设计。

Method: 设计了基于生物启发张拉整体结构的拐杖，终端模块为预张紧自拉伸双细胞张拉整体结构。通过轴向加载机械测试、地面直线及转弯行走试验，并招募18名健康年轻成人进行主观体验评估（努力程度、舒适度、疼痛、稳定性和可用性），与商用刚性和弹簧加载拐杖进行对比。

Result: 张拉整体拐杖和弹簧加载拐杖均降低了峰值负载率；张拉整体设计在努力程度、舒适度、疼痛和可用性方面显著改善；弹簧加载拐杖降低了感知稳定性和步行速度。

Conclusion: 生物启发的张拉整体拐杖是对现有设计的整体改进，其非线性刚度、地面跟随和力反馈等力学特性是改善表现的关键因素。

Abstract: Purpose: Six million people use crutches as mobile aids in the US. Rigid designs with no axial mobility limit sensory feedback and lead to secondary injury on the upper joints. Spring-loaded designs offer compliance but may compromise stability. We designed a biologically inspired tensegrity crutch with a compliant module aiming to achieve favorable mechanical properties. The terminal module was a pre-stressed self-tensile two-cell tensegrity structure. We compared the tensegrity crutch to commercial rigid and spring-loaded crutches in mechanical tests using axial loading, in overground straight and turning walking, and in participant experience. Methods: In human trials, healthy young adults (N=18) with no recent lower-body injury performed straight walking and turning trials at a comfortable self-selected pace. A knee blocker simulated unilateral injury of the dominant leg. After using each type of crutch, participants reported their perceived levels of effort, comfort, pain, stability, and usability. Results: Compared to the rigid design, both spring-loaded and tensegrity conditions reduced peak loading rates. The tensegrity design improved effort, comfort, pain, and usability. Spring-loaded crutches reduced perceived stability and walking speed. Conclusion: The biologically inspired tensegrity crutches were an overall improvement to existing designs. Simulations and mechanical testing suggest that nonlinear stiffness, ground-following, and force feedback are among the beneficial mechanical properties that underlie this improvement.

</details>


### [171] [Parking Assistance for Trailer-Truck Transport Vehicles Using Sensor Fusion and Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02716)
*George Alenchery,Thomas Jeske,Tova Quinones,Lentz Fortune,Tristan Lindo-Slones,Amber Jones,Jordan Fletcher*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个面向拖挂卡车自动泊车的集成框架，融合了感知、混合A*路径规划、非线性模型预测控制和数据驱动泊车系统，并通过在开源A*仿真中集成牵引车-挂车运动学模型进行了概念验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低速泊车尤其是拖挂卡车这类铰接式车辆的自动泊车，由于车辆运动学特性和环境约束，仍是自动驾驶中最具挑战性的难题之一。

Method: 提出将传感器融合、混合A*路径规划、非线性模型预测控制（NMPC）和数据驱动泊车系统相结合的框架，并在开源A*路径规划仿真中集成牵引车-挂车运动学模型作为概念验证。

Result: 在命令行仿真环境中实现了铰接式车辆的路径规划，同时指出防折叠（jackknife prevention）是尚需进一步发展的方向。

Conclusion: 强调了系统级协调对于实现可靠、可扩展的自动泊车解决方案的重要性。

Abstract: Autonomous driving technology has rapidly evolved over the past decade, offering significant improvements in transportation efficiency, safety, and cost reduction. While much of the progress has focused on highway driving and obstacle avoidance, low-speed maneuvers such as parking remain among the most difficult challenges for autonomous systems. This challenge is especially pronounced in trailer-truck transport vehicles due to their articulated motion and environmental constraints. This paper presents a proposed framework for autonomous truck parking that integrates perception, motion planning, control systems, and infrastructure awareness. By combining sensor fusion, Hybrid A* path planning, nonlinear model predictive control (NMPC), and data-driven parking systems, this work highlights the importance of system-level coordination for reliable and scalable autonomous parking solutions. As a proof-of-concept implementation, we adapted an open-source A* path planning simulation to incorporate a tractor-trailer kinematic model, demonstrating articulated vehicle path planning within a command-line simulation environment, with jackknife prevention identified as an area requiring further development.

</details>


### [172] [Semantic Risk-Aware Heuristic Planning for Robotic Navigation in Dynamic Environments: An LLM-Inspired Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02862)
*Hamza Ahmed Durrani,Rafay Suleman Durrani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出语义风险感知启发式（SRAH）规划器，将LLM启发的成本函数编码到A*搜索框架中，并结合闭环重规划以应对动态障碍物。在200次随机实验中，SRAH的任务成功率达62.0%，优于BFS（56.5%）和Greedy（4.0%），表明轻量级LLM启发式启发能够提升导航安全性与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索将大语言模型（LLM）的推理原则融入经典机器人路径规划，以应对动态障碍环境下导航的安全性与鲁棒性挑战。

Method: 提出SRAH规划器，将LLM启发的成本函数（惩罚几何拥堵或高风险区域）嵌入A*搜索框架，并引入闭环重规划机制以应对动态障碍物。在15×15网格世界中进行200次随机实验，静态障碍密度20%，含随机动态障碍物。

Result: SRAH任务成功率为62.0%，相比BFS（56.5%）相对提升9.7%，Greedy（4.0%）大幅领先。通过障碍密度消融实验验证语义成本塑形在不同环境难度下的一致性改进。

Conclusion: 即使轻量级、LLM启发的启发式方法也能为自主机器人导航带来可量化的安全性和鲁棒性增益。

Abstract: The integration of Large Language Model (LLM) reasoning principles into classical robot path planning represents a rapidly emerging research direction. In this paper, we propose a Semantic Risk-Aware Heuristic (SRAH) planner that encodes LLM-inspired cost functions penalising geometrically cluttered or high-risk zones into an A$^*$ search framework, augmented with closed-loop replanning upon dynamic obstacle detection. We evaluate SRAH against two established baselines Breadth-First Search (BFS) with replanning and a Greedy heuristic without replanning across 200 randomised trials in a $15{\times}15$ grid-world with 20\% static obstacle density and stochastic dynamic obstacles. SRAH achieves a task success rate of 62.0\%, outperforming BFS (56.5\%) by 9.7\% relative improvement and Greedy (4.0\%) by a large margin. We further analyse the trade-off between planning overhead, path efficiency, and failure-recovery count, and demonstrate via an obstacle-density ablation that semantic cost shaping consistently improves navigation across environments of varying difficulty. Our results suggest that even lightweight, LLM-inspired heuristics provide measurable safety and robustness gains for autonomous robot navigation.

</details>


### [173] [MolmoAct2: Action Reasoning Models for Real-world Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02881)
*Haoquan Fang,Jiafei Duan,Donovan Clay,Sam Wang,Shuo Liu,Weikai Huang,Xiang Fan,Wei-Chuan Tsai,Shirui Chen,Yi Ru Wang,Shanli Xing,Jaemin Cho,Jae Sung Park,Ainaz Eftekhar,Peter Sushko,Karen Farley,Angad Wadhwa,Cole Harrison,Winson Han,Ying-Chun Lee,Eli VanderBilt,Rose Hendrix,Suveen Ellawela,Lucas Ngoo,Joyce Chai,Zhongzheng Ren,Ali Farhadi,Dieter Fox,Ranjay Krishna*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MolmoAct2 是一个完全开放的动作推理模型，通过引入 MolmoER 空间推理骨干、大规模双机械臂数据集 MolmoAct2-BimanualYAM、OpenFAST 动作分词器、流匹配连续动作专家架构以及自适应深度推理 MolmoThink，在7个仿真与真实世界基准上超越 Pi-0.5 等基线，MolmoER 在13个具身推理基准上超越 GPT-5 和 Gemini Robotics ER-1.5。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的VLA模型在现实部署中面临封闭性、高硬件成本、推理延迟大、微调成功率不足等问题，缺乏一个完全开放、高效且可靠的通用机器人控制器。

Method: 1) 提出MolmoER，基于3.3M样本的"专精-排练"训练策略的VLM骨干；2) 发布三个新数据集，包括720小时遥操作的双臂数据集MolmoAct2-BimanualYAM；3) 提供OpenFAST，基于数百万轨迹训练的开放动作分词器；4) 设计流匹配连续动作专家架构，通过逐层KV缓存条件化嫁接于离散分词VLM之上；5) 提出MolmoThink自适应深度推理，仅对场景中变化区域重新预测深度token。

Result: 在7个仿真与真实世界基准上，MolmoAct2超越Pi-0.5等强基线；MolmoER在13个具身推理基准上超越GPT-5和Gemini Robotics ER-1.5。

Conclusion: MolmoAct2展示了开放VLA模型在现实部署中的可行性与领先性能，提供了完整的模型权重、训练代码和训练数据。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models aim to provide a single generalist controller for robots, but today's systems fall short on the criteria that matter for real-world deployment. Frontier models are closed, open-weight alternatives are tied to expensive hardware, reasoning-augmented policies pay prohibitive latency for their grounding, and fine-tuned success rates remain below the threshold for dependable use. We present MolmoAct2, a fully open action reasoning model built for practical deployment, advancing its predecessor along five axes. We introduce MolmoER, a VLM backbone specialized for spatial and embodied reasoning, trained on a 3.3M-sample corpus with a specialize-then-rehearse recipe. We release three new datasets spanning low-to-medium cost platforms, including MolmoAct2-BimanualYAM, 720 hours of teleoperated bimanual trajectories that constitute the largest open bimanual dataset to date, together with quality-filtered Franka (DROID) and SO100/101 subsets. We provide OpenFAST, an open-weight, open-data action tokenizer trained on millions of trajectories across five embodiments. We redesign the architecture to graft a flow-matching continuous-action expert onto a discrete-token VLM via per-layer KV-cache conditioning. Finally, we propose MolmoThink, an adaptive-depth reasoning variant that re-predicts depth tokens only for scene regions that change between timesteps, retaining geometric grounding at a fraction of prior latency. In the most extensive empirical study of any open VLA to date, spanning 7 simulation and real-world benchmarks, MolmoAct2 outperforms strong baselines including Pi-05, while MolmoER surpasses GPT-5 and Gemini Robotics ER-1.5 across 13 embodied-reasoning benchmarks. We release model weights, training code, and complete training data. Project page: https://allenai.org/blog/molmoact2

</details>


<div id='hep-ex'></div>

# hep-ex [[Back]](#toc)

### [174] [Probing Quantum Entanglement in $τ^+τ^-$ Pairs via the $ππ$ Channel at STCF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01233)
*Xiaokang Li,Chentao Bao,Hai Chen,Mingyi Liu,Dayong Wang*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文基于STCF设施的7 GeV能量下e⁺e⁻→τ⁺τ⁻过程的完整蒙特卡洛模拟，通过τ±→π±ν衰变道（自旋分析能力最大|κ|=1）验证量子纠缠与贝尔不等式违反的可行性。树级QED预言、真值级和探测器级重建的一致性链条显示，使用好解方法重构的并发度为0.279±0.007，表明STCF可为τ轻子对量子关联的精确研究提供竞争性平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用τ轻子对的高能产生过程探测量子纠缠和贝尔不等式违反，需要高精度实验平台。STCF作为新一代tau-粲工厂，具备在阈值附近大量产生τ对的能力，但尚未有人系统评估其用于量子关联研究的可行性。

Method: 基于完整蒙特卡洛模拟，在STCF能区√s=7 GeV下研究e⁺e⁻→τ⁺τ⁻过程，选择ππ衰变道（τ±→π±ν，自旋分析功率|κ|=1）作为主分析道，同时补充ρρ道研究。采用好解方法进行量子态重构，建立从树级QED预言到探测器级重建的一致性验证链。

Result: 重构并发度为0.279±0.007（好解方法），ππ道验证了从理论到探测器模拟的一致性链条。

Conclusion: STCF具备为τ轻子对量子关联研究提供精确测量平台的潜力，特别是通过ππ衰变道可实现高精度量子层析。ρρ道的补充模拟进一步佐证了这一结论。

Abstract: Quantum entanglement and Bell-inequality violation in $τ^+τ^-$ pairs provide a sensitive probe of quantum correlations in high-energy interactions. We present a feasibility study of $e^+e^- \to τ^+τ^-$ at the proposed Super Tau-Charm Facility based on full Monte Carlo simulation at $\sqrt{s} = 7$ GeV, focusing on the $ππ$ channel ($τ^\pm \to π^\pmν$), which offers the maximal spin-analyzing power $|κ| = 1$ and the simplest final-state topology for validating the quantum-tomography framework. We establish a consistency chain from the tree-level QED prediction through truth-level and detector-level reconstruction, yielding a reconstructed concurrence of $0.279 \pm 0.007$ with the good-solution approach. A complementary full-simulation study of the $ρρ$ channel is also briefly reported. These results demonstrate that the STCF can provide a competitive platform for precision studies of quantum correlations in $τ$-lepton pairs.

</details>


### [175] [Data-Driven, Geometry-Aware Optimal-Transport Calibration of Flavor Tagger](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01363)
*Yeonjoon Kim,Un-ki Yang*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出一个几何感知的味标记校准框架，将校准问题转化为概率单纯形上的最优传输问题，利用等距对数比坐标系和Brenier传输实现最小形变，并通过EM算法结合归一化流从控制区数据提取味条件目标分布，最后引入线性化反馈算子分析传播协方差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有味标记校准方法仅在工作点提供标量刻度因子或对一维判别量进行分箱校正，无法对现代标记器的多分量输出提供连续、事件级的校准，导致信息丢失，限制了高性能味标记分析。

Method: 将校准建模为概率单纯形上的最优传输问题；在等距对数比坐标系中参数化并训练传输映射；利用Brenier传输的二次欧氏代价与Aitchison距离的等价性实现最小形变；采用EM技术同时拟合多个控制区，用归一化流建模每个味分量并估计区域混合分数；引入线性化反馈算子分析，将拟合的组成协方差传播到提取的分量密度中。

Result: 模拟闭合研究表明，在专用控制区和独立验证混合物中均实现了改进的闭合效果。

Conclusion: 所提出的几何感知最优传输框架能够为现代味标记器提供连续、事件级的多分量校准，克服了传统方法的信息损失问题，并通过闭合研究验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: Flavor-tagging calibrations are often provided either as scale factors measured at a finite set of working points or as binned corrections to a chosen one-dimensional discriminant. However, this approach falls short of providing continuous, event-level calibration across the full multicomponent outputs of modern taggers. This limitation leads to information loss in analyses that demand high-performance flavor tagging, restricting analyses to a limited set of predefined variables.
  In this work, we propose a geometry-aware framework that formulates flavor-tagger calibration as an optimal transport problem on the probability simplex. The transport maps are parameterized and trained in the isometric log-ratio coordinate system. Because the quadratic Euclidean cost of Brenier transport in this coordinate system is equivalent to the Aitchison distance on the simplex, the learned map induces a minimal deformation under the Aitchison geometry. Furthermore, we extract flavor-conditional target distributions directly from control-region data using an expectation-maximization (EM) technique that simultaneously fits multiple control regions, models each flavor component with a normalizing flow, and estimates the regional mixture fractions. The extracted targets are subsequently used to learn flavor-factorized transport maps. Because the joint estimation of mixture fractions and flexible component densities admits weakly constrained directions, we further introduce a linearized feedback-operator analysis that propagates the fitted composition covariance into the extracted component densities, separating data-constrained modes from those dominated by the composition prior. The simulation-based closure study demonstrates improved closure in dedicated control regions and in independent validation mixtures.

</details>


### [176] [Study of $t\bar{t}$ threshold effects in $eμ$ differential distributions measured in $\sqrt{s}=13$\,TeV $pp$ collisions with the ATLAS detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02341)
*ATLAS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: ATLAS实验测量了13 TeV pp碰撞中的eμ双轻子不变质量和方位角分布，发现包含t\bar{t}阈值附近准束缚态形成的QCD模型能更好描述数据，观测显著性超过3σ，其截面与阈值区专门研究一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展ATLAS实验对eμ双轻子不变质量和方位角分布的测量，研究其对t\bar{t}阈值附近准束缚态形成的敏感性。

Method: 将实测微分分布与（含或不含色单态准束缚态）的t\bar{t}模型（含微扰QCD硬过程预言）进行比较，并对双轻子不变质量分布进行拟合。

Result: 包含准束缚态形成的模型更好描述数据；观测显著性超过3倍标准差；测得的截面与阈值区专门研究结果一致。

Conclusion: 数据支持t\bar{t}阈值附近准束缚态的形成过程。

Abstract: The recent ATLAS measurement of the normalised $eμ$ dilepton invariant mass and azimuthal angle distributions in $\sqrt{s}=13$\,TeV $pp$ collisions at the Large Hadron Collider is extended to study the sensitivity to the formation of quasi-bound states near the $t\bar{t}$ threshold. The measured differential distributions are compared with $t\bar{t}$ models incorporating perturbative QCD predictions for the hard process, with or without the addition of colour-singlet quasi-bound states. The data are better described by the predictions incorporating quasi-bound-state formation. Fits to the dilepton invariant mass distribution show evidence for the latter process with an observed significance exceeding three standard deviations, and a measured cross-section in agreement with dedicated studies of the threshold region.

</details>


### [177] [Women in STEM: Interview with Iris Abt](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02344)
*Elisabetta Gallo,Henriette Ullmann*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文访谈了Iris Abt，她曾在德国学习，是当年该课程唯一完成学业的女性。随后她在中微子物理领域开始职业生涯，在SLC时代前往SLAC，后返回欧洲参与HERA项目，并从事锗探测器的研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 记录Iris Abt作为女性科学家在粒子物理领域的独特职业经历，展现其在多个国际知名实验中的贡献。

Method: 通过访谈形式，追溯Iris Abt从德国求学、中微子物理研究、SLAC的SLC实验、HERA项目到锗探测器研究的职业轨迹。

Result: 呈现了一位女性物理学家跨越多个重要粒子物理实验的职业生涯全景。

Conclusion: Iris Abt的职业生涯反映了粒子物理领域数十年来的发展历程，以及女性科学家在该领域中的坚持与贡献。

Abstract: We interviewed Iris Abt, who studied in Germany, being the only woman finishing studies in her course during that year. She then started her career in neutrino physics, moved to SLAC at the times of SLC, came back to Europe shaping part of the HERA program and made studies on germanium detectors.

</details>


### [178] [Recent Results from NA62 in Kaon and Dump Mode](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02415)
*Jonathan Leon Schubert*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: NA62实验报告了基于2016-2024年数据的超稀有衰变K⁺→π⁺νν̃测量结果，以及2021-2024年束流-dump模式下对质量150-2000 MeV的新物理粒子衰变到h^±ℓ^∓的搜索，未观测到事件并设定了耦合上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测量超稀有衰变K⁺→π⁺νν̃以检验标准模型，并利用束流-dump模式搜索可能存在的重中性轻子等新物理粒子。

Method: 基于NA62固定靶实验在2016-2024年收集的数据，分别通过标准衰变模式和束流-dump模式进行测量与搜索，分析新物理粒子衰变到h^±ℓ^∓（h^±∈(π^±,π^±π⁰,π^±2π⁰,K^±)，ℓ∈(e,μ)）的信号通道。

Result: K⁺→π⁺νν̃测量结果与先前测量及标准模型预测一致；束流-dump模式所有信号通道均未观测到事件，设定了新物理粒子与标准模型耦合的上限。

Conclusion: NA62实验的结果支持标准模型，同时对质量150-2000 MeV区域的新物理粒子与标准模型耦合施加了新的实验约束。

Abstract: NA62 is a fixed-target kaon experiment at the CERN SPS. The recent measurement of the ultra-rare decay $K^+\toπ^+ν\barν$, based on data collected between 2016 and 2024 is reported. The measurement is compatible with previous measurements and the Standard Model. The experiment can also be operated in an alternative beam-dump mode. From this mode, a search for new-physics particles with masses in the range of 150 to 2000~MeV, based on data collected in dedicated runs between 2021 and 2024 is reported. The search focuses on their decays to $h^\pm\ell^\mp$, where $h^\pm\in(π^\pm,π^\pmπ^0,π^\pm2π^0,K^\pm)$, and $\ell\in(e,μ)$, which are expected in heavy neutral lepton scenarios. No event was observed across all considered signal channels, and upper limits were set on the coupling of such particles to the Standard Model.

</details>


### [179] [Surface background of the BULLKID detector array operated with moderate shielding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02468)
*D. Delicato,A. Acevedo-Rentería,M. Folcarelli,G. Del Castello,M. Cappelli,L. E. Ardila-Perez,L. Bandiera,C. Bonomo,M. Calvo,R. Caravita,F. Carillo,F. Cescato,U. Chowdhury,D. A. Crovo,A. Cruciani,A. D'Addabbo,M. De Lucia,M. del Gallo Roccagiovine,F. Ferraro,S. Fu,R. Gartmann,M. Giammei,M. Grassi,V. Guidi,D. L. Helis,T. Lari,L. Malagutti,A. Mazzolari,A. Monfardini,T. Muscheid,D. Nicolò,F. Paolucci,D. Pasciuto,L. Pesce,C. Puglia,D. Quaranta,C. M. A. Roda,S. Roddaro,M. Romagnoni,G. Signorelli,F. Simon,A. Tartari,E. Vázquez-Jáuregui,M. Vignati,K. Zhao*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文介绍了BULLKID探测器的表面实验室运行情况，该探测器由60个硅颗粒吸收体和低温动能电感探测器组成，用于搜寻轻暗物质或相干弹性中微子-核散射。290小时暴露实验显示低能区（2 keV至600 eV）背景与模拟一致，但225 eV至600 eV区域出现与模拟不符的背景上升，高能区则与模拟总体吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一种能够探测轻暗物质和相干弹性中微子-核散射的低温探测器，并在表面实验室条件下使用中等辐射屏蔽进行验证。

Method: 使用由60个0.34g立方体硅颗粒吸收体组成的阵列，配合低温动能电感探测器进行 sensing。利用15个阵列单元的数据，其中2个中心单元用于背景评估，周围单元用作反符合 veto。在290小时内暴露于环境本底，并采用外部和内部辐射屏蔽。

Result: 低能区（2 keV至600 eV）的本底计数率为$(6.8\pm0.4\,{\rm stat.}\pm0.1\,{\rm syst.})\times10^4$ counts/keV kg days，与模拟一致。225 eV至600 eV区域出现背景上升，与模拟不符但不同于其他低温实验的低能过剩特征。高能区与模拟总体吻合，显示铅屏蔽的典型粒子诱导X射线发射。

Conclusion: BULLKID探测器在表面实验室环境下配合中等辐射屏蔽可实现有效运行，低能区背景可控，但225 eV以下区域仍需进一步研究以理解异常背景上升的来源。

Abstract: We present the operation with moderate radiation shield in a surface laboratory of BULLKID (BULky and Low-threshold Kinetic Inductance Detector), a cryogenic detector for searches of light Dark Matter or Coherent Elastic Neutrino-Nucleus Scattering. The detector consists of an array of 60 cubic silicon particle absorbers of 0.34 g each, sensed by cryogenic kinetic inductance detectors. The analysis presented focuses on data from 15 elements of the array, with two central units used to evaluate the background and with their surrounding elements used as veto. The low energy spectrum resulting from an exposure of 290 hours to ambient backgrounds, acquired with the use of external and internal radiation shields, is compatible with the simulations at the level of $(6.8\pm0.4\,{\rm stat.}\pm0.1\,{\rm syst.})\times10^4$ counts / keV kg days from 2 keV down to an energy of 600 eV. The region between 225 eV and 600 eV shows a rise in background in disagreement with the simulations, while not sharing some of the key traits of the low energy excess observed in other cryogenic experiments. The high energy spectrum shape is in overall agreement with the simulations and displays the typical particle-induced X-ray emission of the surrounding lead.

</details>


### [180] [Cascade Pipeline for Leading-Order Matrix Element Evaluation on AMD Versal AI Engine Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02481)
*P. Leguina López,C. Vico Villalba,F. Hervás Álvarez,H. Gutiérrez Arance,S. Folgueras,L. Fiorini,A. Valero,J. Fernández Menéndez,F. Carrió,A. Oyanguren*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于AMD Versal AI Engine阵列的级联流水线架构，用于高效评估高能物理中的领先阶矩阵元素（\ggttg过程）。通过将计算分解为五级流水线并映射到VCK190平台的400个AI Engine瓦片上，实现了每秒1.0×10^6次矩阵元素评估，功耗仅54.8W，相比单CPU核心获得34倍加速和7.7倍能效提升，数值精度达到百万分之一量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能物理事件生成器中的矩阵元素积分计算是计算瓶颈，需要大量相位空间点评估。进入高亮度LHC阶段后，传统CPU扩展无法满足能效需求，亟需利用GPU等高度并行计算平台进行加速。

Method: 采用级联流水线架构在AMD Versal AI Engine阵列上实现\ggttg过程的领先阶矩阵元素评估。受限于16 kB每瓦片程序内存，将计算分解为五级流水线，通过波函数令牌协议在片上级联接口通信。将80条独立流水线映射到VCK190平台的400个AI Engine瓦片上。

Result: 投影吞吐量为每秒1.0×10^6次矩阵元素评估，功耗54.8W，相比单CPU核心获得34倍加速和7.7倍能效提升。与\amcnlo双精度参考的数值一致性在平均相对误差百万分之一量级得到验证。

Conclusion: 提出的基于AI Engine阵列的级联流水线架构能有效加速高能物理矩阵元素计算，在吞吐量、能效和数值精度方面均表现出优势，为高能物理计算提供了传统CPU之外的可行替代方案。

Abstract: A major computational bottleneck in modern High Energy Physics event generators arises from the integration of the matrix element, which requires repeated evaluations at different phase-space points to cover all possible initial- and final-state configurations. As the Large Hadron Collider enters its High-Luminosity phase, the demand for energy-efficient acceleration is expected to exceed the limits of conventional CPU scaling, motivating the use of highly parallel computing platforms such as graphics processing units (GPUs). In this work, we present an alternative approach based on a cascade pipeline architecture for evaluating leading-order matrix elements of the \ggttg process on AMD Versal AI Engine (\aie) arrays. Due to the 16\,kB per-tile program memory constraint, the computation is decomposed into a five-stage pipeline, with stages communicating via a wavefunction-token protocol over the on-chip cascade interface. Mapping 80 independent pipelines onto the 400 \aie tiles of the VCK190 platform yields a projected throughput of $1.0\times10^6$ matrix element evaluations per second at 54.8\,W, corresponding to a $34\times$ speedup over a single CPU core and a $7.7\times$ improvement in energy efficiency. Numerical agreement with the \amcnlo double-precision reference is validated at the parts-per-million level in mean relative error.

</details>


### [181] [From Experimental Limits to Physical Insight: A Retrieval-Augmented Multi-Agent Framework for Interpreting Searches Beyond the Standard Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02491)
*Altan Cakir,Ayca Yerlikaya*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出HEP-CoPilot，一个面向高能物理文献的检索增强多智能体AI框架，能够统一处理文本、数值数据和图形排除限等多种异构信息，实现基于证据的推理和跨论文比较，无需手动数据整合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前超越标准模型的新物理搜索文献快速增长，包含文本分析、数值数据集和图形排除限等异构信息，但这些分散资源的整合仍依赖耗时的手动操作。

Method: 构建检索增强的多智能体AI框架HEP-CoPilot，通过多模态检索与推理架构统一处理文献文本、HEPData结构化实验数据和重建的物理图形，结合检索增强语言模型与协调的智能体工作流。

Result: 在CMS新物理搜索案例上，HEP-CoPilot能够检索相关测量结果、从HEPData记录中直接重建排除限，并执行跨论文的实验约束比较。

Conclusion: 检索增强AI系统可作为粒子物理的科学副驾驶，促进复杂文献导航、异构证据结构化，并加速新物理搜索的解释流程。

Abstract: Modern searches for physics beyond the Standard Model produce rapidly expanding literature containing heterogeneous information, including textual analyses, numerical datasets, and graphical exclusion limits. Integrating these distributed sources remains a time-consuming and manual process for physicists.
  We present HEP-CoPilot, a retrieval-augmented multi-agent AI framework for the exploration and interpretation of high-energy physics literature. The system unifies textual information from publications, structured experimental data from HEPData, and reconstructed physics plots within a multimodal retrieval and reasoning architecture. By combining retrieval-augmented language models with coordinated agent workflows, it enables evidence-grounded reasoning over experimental analyses and structured interpretation of collider results.
  We evaluate the framework on recent CMS searches for physics beyond the Standard Model. Case studies show that HEP-CoPilot can retrieve relevant measurements, reconstruct exclusion limits directly from HEPData records, and perform cross-paper comparisons of experimental constraints. This enables consistent, physics-aware comparison across analyses without manual data integration.
  These results demonstrate that retrieval-augmented AI systems can function as scientific co-pilots for particle physics, facilitating navigation of complex literature, structuring heterogeneous evidence, and accelerating the interpretation pipeline for new physics searches.

</details>


### [182] [Magnetic Monopoles -- From Dirac to the Large Hadron Collider](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02869)
*Vasiliki A. Mitsou*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文综述了磁单极子的理论研究基础、实验探索历史，以及当前在宇宙线实验和对撞机实验（尤其是CERN大型强子对撞机）中的最新搜索进展。尽管有强有力的理论预测，磁单极子至今尚未被实验发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁单极子是物理学基本问题，其存在与否对电磁理论、粒子物理和宇宙学有深远影响。虽然理论上预测其存在，但实验上始终未能观测到，这一矛盾驱动了持续的理论与实验研究。

Method: 本文采用综述性方法，首先概述磁单极子的理论基础（如狄拉克量子化条件等），然后按历史脉络梳理实验探测手段，重点聚焦当代宇宙线实验和加速器实验，尤其是LHC上的搜索策略。

Result: 迄今为止，所有实验搜索均未发现磁单极子的确凿证据。LHC等实验设定了越来越严格的质量和磁荷上限约束，但尚未排除磁单极子存在的可能性。

Conclusion: 磁单极子的实验搜寻仍在继续，未来更高能量的对撞机和更灵敏的宇宙线探测器有望进一步缩小搜索范围或最终发现磁单极子。

Abstract: One of the basic properties of magnetism is that a magnet has always two poles, north and south, which cannot be separated into isolated poles, the magnetic monopoles. There are strong theoretical arguments in favour of monopoles' existence, but in spite of extensive searches they are yet to be found. In this review article, after highlighting briefly the theoretical foundations of monopoles, a historical overview of experimental endeavours to observe them is given, with emphasis on the state-of-the-art of searches in cosmic and collider experiments and in particular the Large Hadron Collider at CERN.

</details>

# cs.CG [[Back]](#toc)

### [183] [Spherical Geometrical Bases of Spherical Origami](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01184)
*Takashi Yoshino*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文建立了球面折纸的严格几何框架，涵盖单位球面上的折纸和球面薄片的三维折叠，将欧几里得折纸定义扩展到球面几何，并引入等距曲线作为折叠线以丰富折叠类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有折纸理论主要基于欧几里得几何，缺乏针对球面（非平面）表面的严格几何框架，限制了折纸在球面结构上的理论探索与实际应用。

Method: 系统地将欧几里得折纸的定义扩展到球面几何；为所有七条Huzita–Justin公理推导出球面几何下的显式方程；引入等距曲线替代测地线作为三维折叠的折痕曲线。

Result: 成功构建了球面折纸鸟的计算机图形，验证了该框架的理论完备性和实际可用性。

Conclusion: 所提出的球面折纸几何框架不仅在理论上自洽完备，而且具备实际应用价值，可支持球面折纸结构的建模与计算机生成。

Abstract: This paper establishes a rigorous geometrical framework for spherical origami, origami using spherical sheets based on spherical geometry. Two settings are treated: origami restricted to the unit sphere ($\mathbb{S}^2$), and three-dimensional folding of spherical sheets in space. For origami on $\mathbb{S}^2$, the definitions of Euclidean origami are systematically extended to the spherical setting, and all seven Huzita--Justin axioms are shown to admit explicit equations in spherical geometry. For three-dimensional folding, equidistant curves are introduced as fold curves, replacing geodesics and enabling a richer family of folds. The framework is validated by successfully constructing computer graphics of spherical origami birds, demonstrating both the theoretical completeness and practical utility of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [184] [Witness Set: A Visibility Problem in $NP\cap XP$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01592)
*Satyabrata Jana,Debabrata Pal,Bodhayan Roy,Sasanka Roy*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文研究见证集问题（Art Gallery问题的对偶），证明简单多边形上该问题属于NP和XP，具有有限离散化性质，并提出组合化n^{f(k)}时间算法；同时证明离散版本在带孔直线多边形上是NP完全的，但在简单多边形上有多项式时间算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Art Gallery问题的对偶——Witness Set问题，探索其计算复杂度与Art Gallery问题（已知为∃ℝ-完全）的差异，并澄清十多年前声称的NP困难性是否成立。

Method: 通过有限离散化方法，将Witness Set问题转化为组合问题，给出n^{f(k)}时间算法；通过归约证明Discrete Witness Set在带孔直线多边形上的NP完全性。

Result: 1) 简单多边形上Witness Set ∈ NP ∩ XP；2) 存在大小为n^{f(k)}的有限离散化；3) 简单多边形上存在n^{f(k)}时间组合算法；4) Discrete Witness Set在带孔直线多边形上是NP完全的；5) 简单多边形上Discrete Witness Set有多项式时间算法。

Conclusion: Witness Set问题与Art Gallery问题在计算复杂度上存在根本性差异（前者避免实代数几何工具），但Witness Set本身是否NP困难仍是开放问题。

Abstract: We study the Witness Set problem, a natural dual to the classical Art Gallery problem. In the Witness Set problem, we are given a polygon $P$ and an integer $k$ as input, and the objective is to determine whether $P$ has a witness set of size at least $k$. A point set $X$ in $P$ is called a witness set if every point in $P$ is visible from at most one point in $X$.
  For simple polygons, we show that Witness Set lies in both $NP$ and $XP$. This stands in sharp contrast to its dual, the Art Gallery problem, which was recently shown to be $\exists \mathbb{R}$-complete by Abrahamsen et al. and is therefore neither in $NP$ nor admits a polynomial-size discretization unless $NP=\exists \mathbb{R}$. In contrast, we prove that Witness Set for simple polygons admits a finite discretization of size $n^{f(k)}$ for some function $f$.
  For comparison, even for simple polygons, Efrat and Har-Peled gave an algorithm for Art Gallery running in time $n^{O(k)}$ using tools from real algebraic geometry, and it appears difficult to obtain such algorithms without this machinery. On the other hand, our approach for Witness Set is purely combinatorial and relies on discretization, leading to an $n^{f(k)}$-time algorithm.
  Although Amit et al. claimed more than fifteen years ago that Witness Set is $NP$-hard, no proof or reference was provided. We show that the discrete version of the Witness Set problem - where the witness set must be chosen from a given finite point set $Q$ (instead of allowing witnesses to be chosen anywhere in the polygon), referred to as Discrete Witness Set - is $NP$-complete, even when the input is restricted to rectilinear polygons with holes. However, for simple polygons, Discrete Witness Set admits a polynomial-time algorithm by Das et al. Thus, it remains an open question whether the Witness Set problem is $NP$-hard.

</details>


### [185] [A greedy maximal sweepline algorithm for a Jordan curve](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01861)
*Apurva Mudgal*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文针对Jordan曲线提出了一种贪心扫描线算法，并利用Kőnig引理证明了该算法在[1]定义的意义下是最大化的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在计算几何中，Jordan曲线的扫描线算法通常难以保证最大化性质，现有方法可能存在效率或最优性不足的问题。本文旨在设计一种能够在给定意义下达到最大化的贪心扫描线算法。

Method: 提出贪心扫描线算法处理Jordan曲线；利用Kőnig引理（图论中关于无限树的经典结论）来证明算法的最大化性质。

Result: 成功证明了所提出的贪心扫描线算法在[1]定义的意义下是最大化的。

Conclusion: 本文为Jordan曲线的扫描线处理提供了一种具有最大化保证的贪心算法，其证明方法依赖于Kőnig引理，为该类问题的理论分析提供了新思路。

Abstract: We give a greedy sweepline algorithm for a Jordan curve and prove that it is maximal in the sense of [1]. Our proof uses Kőnig's lemma.

</details>


### [186] [Manifold k-NN: Accelerated k-NN Queries for Manifold Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02224)
*Pengfei Wang,Qinghao Guo,Haisen Zhao,Shiqing Xin,Shuangmin Chen,Changhe Tu,Wenping Wang*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文提出Manifold k-NN，将DP-NNS框架从单近邻搜索推广到任意k近邻搜索，利用流形对齐数据的几何特性，实现比传统kd-tree快1-10倍的速度，并支持动态前缀查询和点删除操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的k-NN搜索方法（如kd-tree）对流形对齐数据的低维结构利用不充分；而DP-NNS框架虽能利用Voronoi图加速查询，但仅支持k=1的单近邻搜索，无法满足需要局部邻域统计信息的应用需求。

Method: 基于几何观察：若p_i是查询点q的最近邻，则第二近邻要么在前缀集P_{1:i-1}中，要么在p_i的后继列表中。通过递归扩展这一原理，提出Manifold k-NN递归算法；并扩展框架支持点删除（局部Delaunay更新）。

Result: 在体到面的查询场景中实现1-10倍加速；支持任意前缀子集P_{1:m}的k-NN搜索且零额外开销；在多种几何数据集上验证了效率和广泛适用性。

Conclusion: Manifold k-NN有效推广了DP-NNS框架，为现代图形管线中的流形对齐数据提供了一套完整的动态k近邻搜索方案。

Abstract: k-nearest neighbor (k-NN) search is a fundamental primitive in geometry processing and computer graphics. While spatial partitioning structures such as kd-trees are standard, they are often manifold-blind, failing to exploit the intrinsic low-dimensional structure of points sampled from 2-manifolds. Recent advances in dynamic programming-based nearest neighbor search (DP-NNS) leverage incrementally constructed Voronoi diagrams to accelerate queries, where each site p maintains a list of successors that progressively refine its Voronoi cell. However, DP-NNS is restricted to single nearest neighbor (k=1) searches, precluding their adoption in applications that require local neighborhood statistics.
  In this paper, we generalize the DP-NNS framework to support arbitrary k-NN queries for manifold-aligned data. Our approach is founded on the geometric observation that if p_i is the nearest neighbor of a query q in P, then the second nearest neighbor of q must reside either within the prefix set P_{1:i-1} = {p_1, \dots, p_{i-1}} or within p_i's successor list. By recursively extending this principle, we introduce Manifold k-NN, a recursive algorithmic scheme that significantly outperforms conventional kd-trees for manifold-aligned data. Our method achieves a 1\times--10\times speedup in volume-to-surface query scenarios and inherently supports dynamic prefix queries -- enabling k-NN searches within any subset P_{1:m} (m \leq n) with zero overhead. Furthermore, we extend the framework to support point deletion via local Delaunay updates, providing a complete suite of dynamic operations for point set modification. Comprehensive experiments on diverse geometric datasets demonstrate the efficiency and broad applicability of our approach for modern graphics pipelines.
  Source code is available at https://github.com/sssomeone/manifold-knn.

</details>

### [187] [Heat-tree: Cross-platform software for interactive and embeddable phylogenetic tree visualization and editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01109)
*Zachary S. L. Foster,Jeff H. Chang,Niklaus J. Grunwald*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文介绍了heat-tree，一个跨JavaScript、R和Python的软件包，用于交互式系统发育树可视化、操作和编辑，支持可定制、可嵌入的Web兼容树形图，并包含图形用户界面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统发育树可视化工具多局限于静态图形、需要编码技能、或为特定网站开发而难以复用和扩展。

Method: 开发了heat-tree软件包集合，覆盖JavaScript、R和Python三种语言，支持径向和矩形布局，自动将元数据映射为视觉编码，提供交互式树编辑功能和完整GUI。

Result: 实现了可嵌入R Markdown、Jupyter Notebooks、Quarto文档及HTML/JavaScript应用的自包含交互式树形可视化，并支持导出出版级质量的图形。

Conclusion: Heat-tree提供了用户友好的跨环境交互式系统发育树可视化解决方案，兼顾程序化控制和图形界面操作。

Abstract: Phylogenetic trees are the primary framework for conveying evolutionary relationships. While many tools exist for visualizing phylogenetic trees, most are limited to static graphics, require coding expertise, or are developed for a specific website and not easily reusable or extensible. To address these limitations, we developed heat-tree, a collection of software packages in JavaScript, R, and Python for interactive visualization, manipulation, and editing of phylogenetic trees and their associated metadata. Heat-tree allows for the creation of customizable, web-compatible tree visualizations that can be easily embedded in R Markdown, Jupyter Notebooks, and Quarto documents, as well as directly in HTML/JavaScript applications and websites. The package supports radial and rectangular tree layouts, automated translation of metadata values into visual encodings on the tree, interactive tree editing, and export capabilities for publication-quality figures. All visualization parameters are definable programmatically or interactively using the comprehensive graphical user interface included with each visualization. Heat-tree was designed to be a user-friendly software package for interactive tree viewing, manipulation, editing, and self-contained, embeddable visualization across software environments.

</details>


### [188] [Emergent population dynamics of random walkers with cooperative reproduction and spatial selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01770)
*Ohad Vilk,Baruch Meerson*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文研究了高阶无性繁殖对种群入侵动力学的影响，发现超过二元繁殖时入侵前沿消失，二元繁殖时速度与扩散无关，三元繁殖表现出临界行为（超临界态形成"入侵子弹"，亚临界态扩散传播，临界态存在连续前沿族）。结果表明自然界中分裂和二元繁殖的主导地位反映了入侵动力学的根本约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将N分支布朗运动模型推广到高阶无性繁殖，探究繁殖阶数对种群入侵前沿动力学的影响，理解自然界中二元繁殖占主导地位的根本原因。

Method: 通过数学建模扩展N分支布朗运动模型至高阶无性繁殖（三元及以上），分析不同繁殖阶数下的入侵前沿行为、传播速度及临界现象。

Result: (1) 繁殖阶数超过二元时，入侵前沿不再存在；(2) 二元繁殖时入侵速度与扩散系数无关；(3) 三元繁殖呈现临界行为：超临界态形成强局域化的"入侵子弹"，亚临界态呈扩散传播，临界态存在连续的前沿族。

Conclusion: 自然界中分裂和二元繁殖的主导地位反映了入侵动力学的根本约束，高阶繁殖无法形成有效入侵前沿。

Abstract: We extend the $N$ branching Brownian motions model of population invasion to higher-order asexual reproduction. Increasing reproduction order leads to qualitative changes: invasion fronts generically cease to exist beyond binary reproduction; and in the binary case itself, their speed becomes diffusion-independent. Ternary reproduction shows critical behavior, with collapse into a strongly localized `invasion bullet' in the supercritical regime, diffusive spreading in the subcritical regime, and a continuous family of fronts at criticality. These results suggest that the dominance of division and binary reproduction in nature reflects fundamental constraints on invasion dynamics.

</details>

### [189] [Cohomological Maschke's Theorem for Generalized Digroups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01215)
*José Gregorio Rodríguez-Nieto,Olga Patricia Salazar-Díaz,Andrés Sarrazola-Alzate,Raúl Velásquez*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究了广义二群（generalized digroups）表示论中的Maschke型现象。通过构造结合包络代数$A_D$，证明了$Rep(D)$与左$A_D$-模范畴等价。在群分量满足Maschke型条件时，证明了短正合列在$ρ$-侧可分裂，完全分裂的障碍由闭链描述并等同于$Ext^1_{Rep(D)}(Q,W)$。此外推导了一个谱序列，揭示了分裂性与非半单性的推论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典Maschke定理推广到广义二群的表示范畴中，探索其表示范畴的半单性条件及分裂性质。

Method: 构造广义二群$D$的结合包络代数$A_D$，建立表示范畴与模范畴的等价；利用群分量的Maschke型条件分析短正合列的分裂性；通过闭链描述分裂障碍并识别为Ext群；应用谱序列技术进一步刻画分裂与非半单性。

Result: 证明了$Rep(D) \cong A_D\text{-Mod}$；在群分量满足Maschke条件时短正合列在$ρ$-侧可分裂；完全分裂的障碍由$Ext^1_{Rep(D)}(Q,W)$刻画；推导了一个谱序列用于分裂性和非半单性的分析。

Conclusion: 广义二群的表示论中，Maschke型条件仅保证部分分裂（$ρ$-侧），完全分裂存在由Ext群度量的障碍，且谱序列提供了更细致的分裂与非半单性判定工具。

Abstract: We study Maschke-type phenomena in the representation theory of generalized digroups. For a generalized digroup $D$, we construct an associative enveloping algebra $A_D$ and prove that $Rep(D)$ is equivalent to the category of left $A_D$-modules. Under a Maschke-type condition on the group component, we show that short exact sequences split on the $ρ$-side, while the obstruction to full splitting is described by cocycles and identified with $Ext^1_{Rep(D)}(Q,W)$. We also derive a spectral sequence with consequences for splitting and non-semisimplicity.

</details>


### [190] [A partial dictionary between universal central extensions and orthogonal polynomials in the superelliptic Krichever--Novikov setting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02530)
*Felipe Albino dos Santos*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文建立了超椭圆曲线坐标环的导子代数与当前代数的万有中心扩张中关系与正交多项式族之间的系统字典，包含三项规范对应：三项递推、生成函数的Sturm-Liouville方程以及混合2-上循环的Legendre原函数形式，并在二次和四次回文情形下完全证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建超椭圆曲线坐标环的导子代数Der(A)和当前代数g⊗A的万有中心扩张中线性代数关系与正交多项式族之间的系统对应关系，统一这两个看似不同领域的数学结构。

Method: 建立三项规范对应字典：(1)中心基约化关系等价于正交多项式族的三项递推；(2)中心的生成函数满足该族对应的Sturm-Liouville常微分方程；(3)混合扇区2-上循环在二次情形下等于Legendre原函数(P_{n-1}(a)-P_{n+1}(a))/(2n+1)。对P(x)=x²-2ax+1（Legendre多项式）和P(x)=x⁴-2ax²+1（四次回文情形）完全证明该字典。

Result: 二次情形下回文对称强制递推为Legendre三项递推；四次情形下奇数扇区为Legendre，偶数扇区满足带回文系数的两分量递推。猜测偶数次回文P均强制对称递推系数。该字典同样适用于Kähler侧Ω¹_A/dA：所有扇区在重缩放参数下归约为扇区-1族，递推和ODE条目是规范的，混合上循环条目部分依赖于选择。

Conclusion: 超椭圆曲线相关的万有中心扩张与正交多项式之间存在深刻的内在联系，回文对称性在多项式的奇偶扇区中分别诱导出Legendre多项式结构和带对称系数的多分量递推结构。

Abstract: Let $m \geq 2$, let $P(x) \in \mathbb{C}[x]$ have simple roots, and let $A = \mathbb{C}[x^{\pm 1},\,u \mid u^m = P(x)]$ be the coordinate ring of the associated superelliptic curve. The derivation algebra $\mathrm{Der}(A)$ and the current algebra $\mathfrak{g}\otimes A$ (for $\mathfrak{g}$ a simple Lie algebra) each admit a universal central extension whose center is multi-dimensional and carries linear algebraic relations among its basis elements. We establish a systematic dictionary between these relations and families of orthogonal polynomials in the parameter $a$ encoding the branch locus of $P$.
  The dictionary has three canonical entries: (1)~the basis reduction relations in the center of $\widehat{\mathrm{Der}(A)}$ are exactly the three-term recurrence of an orthogonal polynomial family; (2)~the generating function of the center satisfies the Sturm--Liouville ODE of that family; (3)~the mixed-sector $2$-cocycle equals the Legendre antiderivative $(P_{n-1}(a)-P_{n+1}(a))/(2n+1)$ in the quadratic case.
  We prove the dictionary completely for $P(x)=x^2-2ax+1$ (Legendre polynomials) and for the quartic palindromic case $P(x)=x^4-2ax^2+1$. In the quadratic case, palindromic symmetry forces the recurrence to be the Legendre three-term recurrence; in the quartic case, the odd sector is Legendre and the even sector satisfies a two-component recurrence with palindromic coefficients. We conjecture this pattern -- palindromic $P$ forcing symmetric recurrence coefficients -- holds in all even degrees. The same dictionary governs the Kähler side $Ω^1_A/dA$: all sectors reduce to the sector-$1$ family at a rescaled parameter, and the recurrence and ODE entries are canonical while the mixed-cocycle entry is partially choice-dependent.

</details>
